## Phase 3 / Task 2 notes
- Added the Q7 API (types, commands, events, `CoreError::ItemNotFound` and `NotAllowed`) to `api/types.rs` and `api/mod.rs`; `TimelineItem::new` and `MessageDetails::default()` carry the old struct literals (core mapping, app and core tests). Mapping in `timeline.rs` still yields only `Message` (with `details: Box::default()`) and `Other`; the real mapping is Task 3.
- Redacted `Debug` (sentinel-tested): `Inline`, `Block`, `RichText` (counts only), `ReplyPreview`, `ReactionGroup`, `MentionRef`, `OutgoingMessage`, and a manual `Command` impl (hides reaction keys and search queries; `Redact` reason and message text are already redacted newtypes). `MessageDetails` derives `Debug` because every field that holds content has a redacted `Debug`.
- Engine: every new command is accepted. Pure helpers `command_action`, `action_error` and `unfinished_action_event` pick the answer; `NotLoggedIn` without a session, `NoRoomSelected` without a matching selected room, else `ActionFailed(Internal("not implemented yet"))`. `SearchMembers` with a matching room answers `Members { members: [] }`; without session or room it answers `ActionFailed` like the others (the plan sentence is ambiguous on this). The matching-room case is unit-tested on the pure helper, not through `Engine::handle`, because a `Selected` needs an SDK `Timeline` and the SDK offers no offline way to create a room without its `testing` feature; no-session and no-selection are tested through `handle`.
- Files outside the plan's list that had to change to compile: `crates/app/src/state.rs` (ignores the four new events for now) and `crates/app/src/bridge.rs` (`Pending` boxes its `Command`, because `Command` grew and clippy `result_large_err` fired on `try_send`).
- `CoreEvent::Members` shows its `query` in `Debug` (a fragment of composer text); `Command::SearchMembers` hides it. Revisit in Task 6 if the event should hide it too.
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (146 core lib, 177 app lib), `check-app-deps.sh`, `check-core-api.sh` and `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored run against the local Synapse (`--ignored --skip keyring`): all pass in about 55 s total, then `docker compose stop`.
- APIs used: none from matrix-sdk, ruma or iced beyond what Phase 2 already used (the new code is plain Rust types and engine glue).

## Phase 3 / Task 4 notes
- New private module `crates/core/src/rich_text.rs` (`message_rich_text`, `select_html`, `from_html`, `from_plain`, `normalize_url`, a pure `linkify`); `timeline.rs` calls `message_rich_text` with `event.latest_json()` and the SDK formatted body (text, notice, emote), and `plain_rich_text` moved into `from_plain`. Fixtures in `crates/core/tests/fixtures/html/*.html` (used by unit tests via `include_str!`, because the module is private).
- Source selection (Q4) is the pure `select_html`: with raw JSON only the raw event counts (original `formatted_body`, or `m.new_content` when `m.relates_to.rel_type` is `m.replace`), and only when `format` is `org.matrix.custom.html`; the SDK body is used only when there is no raw JSON. I added the `m.replace` check so a non-edit event cannot pick its formatted body from a made-up `m.new_content`.
- Task 1-3 follow-ups handled: link scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`, `mailto`, no userinfo, at most 2,048 bytes before and after normalization; anything else becomes text; `matrix.to` and `matrix:` parse to mentions); nesting capped at 32 (deeper content flattened to text); paragraphs, headings, code blocks and rules capped at 1,000 per message, which also covers plain bodies (`from_plain`), plus the 16,384-char, 2,000-inline and 1,000-list-item caps. The `Inline::Link.url` doc now matches reality. Not done (other tasks): ambiguity flag on reply and membership names, `Option` for an unloaded reply sender, reaction-group and reaction-key caps.
- Found by test: dropping a parsed tree of a few thousand nested elements overflows the stack (recursive `Drop` in ruma-html; reproduced at 6,000 `<div>` in a debug test thread, 2 MB stack), and an event can carry about 13,000 open tags. `from_html` therefore counts start-tag candidates (`<` followed by an ASCII letter) before parsing (`too_many_tags`, `MAX_TAGS` = 4,000) and uses the plain body above that. Security follow-up: the first guard mirrored the tokenizer (counting `</` as a close) and was bypassed by `<b></>` (ignored by html5ever), `</ x>` and `<!--</-->` (bogus comments), which parsed 10,900 levels deep. The count does not depend on tokenizer behaviour: a tree cannot be deeper than its element count, and every element needs such a candidate. It over-counts closed, void and commented tags (safe side). Why 4,000: 6,000 nested elements overflowed a 2 MB debug thread (smallest stack and largest frames we run on, tokio workers default to 2 MB), so 4,000 keeps a margin of 1.5x; the test parses exactly 4,000 nested `div`, `b`, `blockquote` and `ul`/`li` in a debug test thread. Normal messages are far below it (the 2,000-inline cap alone needs about 3,000 tags). Tests: `<b></>`, `</ x>`, `<!--</-->`, 5,000 `<div>`, and the max-depth case.
- Security follow-ups (linkifier and links): trailing-`)` stripping was quadratic (each strip rescanned the token; a 65,000-`)` token took 9 s in release, 39.5 s in debug). Parens are now counted once and updated while stripping, and tokens longer than `MAX_URL_BYTES` + 64 (`MAX_TOKEN_MARGIN`) are skipped, since they cannot be links. Regression test `linkify_trailing_parentheses_is_linear` (65,000 `)`, under 1 s). `mailto:` links keep only `subject`, `body`, `cc` and `bcc` (case-insensitive) in the query, so `?attach=/path` is dropped (`keep_safe_mailto_query`; the query is re-encoded, so `@` becomes `%40`).
- Not fixable in the core (spoofing): link and mention labels can differ from their targets (`<a href="https://evil.example">https://bank.example</a>`, a pill labelled with another user's name). Task 10 must show the real URL (or the real user/room ID) before opening a link or acting on a pill, and not rely on the label. Also on the Phase 3 human checkpoint.
- Choices the plan left open: the paragraph cap is 1,000 (`MAX_BLOCKS`); the text cap yields at most 16,385 chars (16,384 plus the ellipsis); a spoiler span flattens block elements inside it; `table` cells are joined by two spaces per row; the linkifier keeps `)` when balanced (Wikipedia-style URLs) and trims leading `( < ' "`.
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (187 core lib, 177 app lib), `check-app-deps.sh`, `check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored run (`--ignored --skip keyring`) against the local Synapse: all pass, about 66 s including the user setup, then `docker compose stop`. New integration test: `formatted_body_becomes_rich_text_without_script_content`. Re-run after the security fixes: same gate (190 core lib, 177 app lib) and the ignored run all pass.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: ruma 0.17.0 `lib.rs:156-158` (`html` re-export; compiled against), ruma-html 0.9.0 `html.rs` (`Html::parse` 37, `Html::children` 89, `NodeData` 297, `ElementData` 314, `NodeRef::children` 473, `Children` 513), ruma-common 0.20.0 `identifiers/matrix_uri.rs` (`MatrixId` 24, `MatrixToUri::parse` 283, `MatrixUri::parse` 474, `identifiers.rs:35` re-export), `serde/raw.rs:77` (`Raw::json`), ruma-events 0.35.0 `room/message.rs` (`FormattedBody` 754, `MessageFormat` 741), matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `event_item/mod.rs:498` (`latest_json`).
- Open issues: none. Human items: none (no rendering yet).

## Phase 3 / Task 5 notes
- New private module `crates/core/src/outgoing.rs` (`outgoing_content`, Q10). Mention labels are linked at their first free occurrence (ranges never overlap; a label inside another mention's range is skipped), the label is backslash-escaped (all ASCII punctuation) and the user ID percent-encoded (`@alice:localhost` gives `%40alice%3Alocalhost`, accepted by Synapse and our mapper). `body` is the text as typed; `m.mentions` lists only users whose label was found. Text, notice and emote go through `*_markdown`.
- Raw HTML (Task 1 security note): ruma's `parse_markdown` is private and passes `Html`/`InlineHtml` through, so the formatted body is sanitized after the fact with ruma-html's `SanitizerConfig::strict()` plus `remove_elements(["img", "script", "style"])` (those go with their content; other unknown tags are dropped and their text kept). It runs on every formatted body, because markdown itself makes `[x](javascript:alert(1))`, `data:` links and remote `![](url)` images (strict allows `href` schemes http, https, ftp, mailto, magnet, matrix and `img` `src` mxc only, and the depth is capped at 100). Before parsing, the generated HTML (not the source: markdown nests with `>` or list markers and no `<`) is counted for start tags (`<` plus a letter); above `MAX_TAGS` = 1,500 the message is sent as plain text with no `formatted_body` (also for the line-break fallback), so the tree parsed here and, later, by the Task 4 mapper (`MAX_TAGS` 4,000 there; 6,000 nested elements overflowed a 2 MB debug thread) stays far from the stack limit. Tests: `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>`, `<script>`, `<p onclick>`, `<iframe>`, `javascript:` links (unit), and an integration test that reads the event from the server and checks `formatted_body` holds `<strong>b</strong>` and no `<img`, `<script` or `onerror`. Code spans keep their `<` as `&lt;` (tested). Added: the three markdown-only leaks, `<a title href=javascript:>`, `>` x 50,000 with and without a leading `<b>x</b>`, `- ` x 50,000, 5,000 `![](u)` (all on a 2 MiB debug thread: no crash, no `formatted_body`; pulldown-cmark itself caps `>` nesting at 100, the guard does not depend on that), 1,490 raw `<div>` kept and mapped by `from_html` on a 2 MiB thread, and 1,510 lines sent without formatting.
- Mentions: a user goes into `m.mentions` only if their `href="https://matrix.to/#/..."` is in the final formatted body, so a name inside code (or in a body sent as plain text) notifies nobody. Known wart: a label inside code still shows the link markup as text in the formatted body (`hi [Alice](https://matrix.to/...)`), because the link is added before markdown runs; skipping code needs a markdown parse and a new direct dependency, so not done.
- Deviation found by test: ruma returns no `formatted` for a text whose only markdown is a newline (`a\nb`). The plan wants `<br />`, so `line_breaks_html` builds it (escaped lines joined by `<br />\n`) when there is no formatted body and the text has a newline.
- Engine: `Selected { room_id, timeline: Arc<Timeline>, task, tasks: JoinSet<()> }`; `Selected::stop` aborts the forwarder and `JoinSet::shutdown`s the action tasks before the timeline drops. `Command::Send`, `RetrySend` and `DiscardSend` go through `Engine::run_action`, which answers `NotLoggedIn` / `NoRoomSelected` as `ActionFailed` and otherwise spawns in the JoinSet. Reply: the item is resolved by `unique_id` (`ItemNotFound`), must be an event with `can_be_replied_to` and an event ID (`NotAllowed`), then `Timeline::send_reply`; otherwise `Timeline::send`. Errors from the SDK are the fixed text "the message could not be queued". Retry: `unwedge()` only when the echo is `SendingFailed { is_recoverable: false }`, then `room.send_queue().set_enabled(true)`. Discard: `abort()`, then `set_enabled(true)` even when the abort failed. No local echo gives `NotAllowed`.
- Resume (Q11): `sync::ResumeDetector` and `ResumeSink` (a `StateSink` wrapper that sees every published state, unlike the coalescing watch slot) call back on `Running` after `Offline` or `Reconnecting`. `forward_sync_state` turns that into `client.send_queue().set_enabled(true)` in the same task as the supervisor (a `Notify` plus a select), so no detached task holds the client. `run_supervised` gained an `on_resume` argument. Unit tests: `running_after_offline_or_reconnecting_is_a_resume`, `the_resume_sink_calls_back_and_still_publishes`.
- Integration tests (`crates/core/tests/timeline_actions.rs`, alice sends, carol receives, each with its own core): markdown round trip, raw HTML not sent, reply round trip (`reply_to.sender == carol`, body equals the first line), mention (`highlighted` and a `Mention(User(carol))` pill), local echo (Sending then Sent under one item ID), a 70,000-byte message (`Failed`, `recoverable == false` as the plan expected, so no stop-and-ask) with `DiscardSend` removing it and a following `Send` still arriving, and `ItemNotFound` / `NoRoomSelected`. Task 3 follow-up: replies, `send_failure` and the mention highlight are now covered; reactions are Task 6.
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (207 core lib, 177 app lib), `check-app-deps.sh`, `check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored run (`--ignored --skip keyring`): all pass, 1 min 41 s for the whole `cargo test` (incl. build), then `docker compose stop`. The first full ignored run had `timeline_items.rs` (3 tests, previous task) time out in `wait_for_room`; that file alone and two further full runs passed, so it was a one-off, not flaky twice in a row. Worth watching.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `send_queue/mod.rs` (`SendQueue::set_enabled` :286, `Client::send_queue` :389, `Room::send_queue` :452, `RoomSendQueue::set_enabled` :1232, `SendHandle::abort` :2838, `unwedge` :2986); matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `timeline/mod.rs` (`Timeline::send` :363, `send_reply` :451, `items` :1022, `room` :228), `event_item/mod.rs` (`event_id` :279, `can_be_replied_to` :461, `local_echo_send_handle` :642, `send_state` :224), `event_item/local.rs:78` (`EventSendState::SendingFailed { is_recoverable }`); ruma-events 0.35.0 `room/message/without_relation.rs` (`text_markdown` :61, `notice_markdown` :77, `emote_markdown` :93, `From<..WithoutRelation> for RoomMessageEventContent` :304, `add_mentions` :274), `room/message.rs:815` (`parse_markdown`, private) and `:754` (`FormattedBody`), `lib.rs:296` (`Mentions::with_user_ids`); ruma-html `sanitizer_config.rs` (`strict` :117, `remove_elements` :174), `helpers.rs:13` (`sanitize_html`, whose steps `Html::parse`, `sanitize_with`, `to_string` are reused); tokio `JoinSet::{shutdown :381, try_join_next :323}`; matrix-sdk `room/messages.rs:88` (`MessagesOptions::backward`, test only).
- Open issues: (1) the sanitizer still parses up to 1,500 start tags into a tree (about 4x under the 6,000 nested elements that overflowed a 2 MB debug thread; the Task 4 open Medium itself is not fixed here). (2) Reactions and edit are Task 6. (3) Unit tests cannot cover the engine's `send_task`/`retry_task`/`discard_task` offline (no `Timeline` without a server); they are covered by the integration tests. Human items: none.

## Phase 3 / Task 6 notes
- Resumed from the WIP commit 6e7874b (it compiled; its unit tests passed). Kept as is: the four engine handlers (`edit_task`, `redact_task`, `react_task`, `members_task`) behind `Engine::run_action`, `normalize_reaction_key` (trim, 64-byte cap at a char boundary, empty gives `NotAllowed`), `rank_members` (at most 8, starts-with before contains, then name; case-insensitive, leading `@` ignored; empty or over-256-byte query matches nobody), and the Task 3 follow-up caps on incoming reactions (`MAX_REACTION_GROUPS` 24, keys over 64 bytes dropped). Edit refuses non-`is_editable` items with `NotAllowed`; the redaction reason is exposed only at the SDK call; a member list is fetched with `sync_members()` once per selection (`OnceCell` in `Selected`), then `members_no_sync(JOIN)`. SDK errors give fixed texts (no content).

After `scripts/create-test-users.sh` (which makes them leave all rooms) the full run passed, and `timeline_items` alone passes. Run `create-test-users.sh` before each ignored run. ## Phase 3 / Task 7 notes
- `SelectRoom` now calls `room_list_service().set_room_subscriptions(&[id])` (only when sync runs), and spawns two forwarders into the selection's `JoinSet`, so they end with the selection: `forward_pagination` (current `live_back_pagination_status` value at once, then the stream, mapped by `pagination_state`) and `forward_typing` (`subscribe_to_typing_notifications`, names from `get_member_no_sync`, at most 16 users per event). `PaginateBackwards` runs `paginate_backwards(20)` in the set behind a `PaginationPermit` (an `AtomicBool` per selection; a request that finds it taken is dropped). `MarkRead` sends `Read`, plus `FullyRead` when asked. `SetTyping` calls `typing_notice`. The Task 2 "not implemented" fallbacks (`command_action`, `action_error`, `unfinished_action_event`) are gone; the handle-level tests for no session and no selected room still cover all ten action commands.
- TDD: unit tests (`pagination_runs_at_most_one_at_a_time`, `sdk_pagination_status_maps_to_the_api_state`, `typing_users_take_names_from_members`) failed to compile first, then passed. Integration tests (`crates/core/tests/timeline_live.rs`) were written after the code.
- Findings for Tasks 8, 9 and 13: (1) `Pagination { ReachedStart }` and the diff that inserts `TimelineStart` come from different forwarders, so the status can arrive first; the app must not assume the item is already there. (2) A `PaginateBackwards` sent in reaction to `Idle` can, in a tiny window, find the previous run's permit still held and be dropped silently (no event); the app must ask again on its next scroll or `Idle` (the test re-sends after 3 s of quiet). (3) `TimelineStart` is the first item, ahead of the date divider and state items. (4) In the first run a typing test timed out once (carol's subscription is applied after `SelectRoom` returns, so a one-shot notice can pass first); the test now re-sends, and 6 later runs passed. The app's typing sends repeat while the composer is non-empty (Q13), so this is a test-only concern.
- Integration tests: pagination (60 numbered messages, fresh alice core, repeated `PaginateBackwards` on `Idle`, `ReachedStart`, all 60 in order, `TimelineStart` first), receipts (carol `MarkRead { fully_read: false }`, alice's newest message has carol in `read_by`), typing (alice `SetTyping`, carol sees `[alice]`, then `[]`).
- `crates/core/examples/seed_big_room.rs` (Q25) compiles under `cargo test`. Human step, after `docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/create-test-users.sh`: `cargo run -p twicecat-core --example seed_big_room -- 10000` (count optional, default 10,000). It reads `.env.test`, refuses a non-loopback `HOMESERVER_URL`, logs in as test user 1, invites test user 3, sends 20 messages at a time and prints only the room ID.
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked`, `check-app-deps.sh`, `check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored run (`--ignored --skip keyring`, after `create-test-users.sh`): all pass, 2 min 37 s including build, then `docker compose stop`.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `room_list_service/mod.rs` (`set_room_subscriptions` :485), `sync_service.rs` (`room_list_service` :612), `timeline/pagination.rs` (`paginate_backwards` :30, `live_back_pagination_status` :124), `timeline/mod.rs` (`mark_as_read` :984); matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `room/mod.rs` (`subscribe_to_typing_notifications` :661, `get_member_no_sync` :1217, `typing_notice` :2104), `event_cache/caches/pagination.rs` (`PaginationStatus` :379, re-exported as `matrix_sdk::event_cache::PaginationStatus`), `event_handler/mod.rs` (`EventHandlerDropGuard` :552).

## Phase 3 / Task 8 notes
- New `crates/app/src/timeline.rs`: `TimelineView` (items, measured, prefix, offset_from_bottom, viewport_h, width, anchor, pagination, last_marked, plus `last_scroll_request` and a private `requested_at`) and the pure functions `estimate_height`, `is_continuation`, `rebuild_prefix`, `visible_window`, `spacer_heights`, `record_anchor`, `anchor_candidates`, `anchor_correction`, `should_paginate`. `App.timeline` is now a `TimelineView`; the Phase 2 tests and `main_view` read `app.timeline.items`. New messages `RowMeasured { id, height }` and `TimelineScrolled(Viewport)`; `CoreEvent::Pagination` is handled (selected room only). No view code yet (Task 9).
- TDD: the test module was written first and failed to compile (13 missing functions); then the implementation. Two tests failed on first run and exposed one real bug (`anchor_correction` moved on to a later candidate when the first gave no change) and one test typo.
- Design choices: (1) `anchor_correction` takes candidates (the anchor plus 8 neighbours each side, captured before a change) so a removed anchor falls back to the nearest surviving item, and returns the first surviving candidate's answer only. (2) The anchor is re-recorded after every change and every scroll event; at or under 2 px from the bottom it is `None` and nothing is corrected. (3) `Reset` moves the new list in (owned diffs), the app does not copy 10,000 items. (4) A measurement shifts the prefix tail in O(n) instead of rebuilding with hash lookups. (5) `measured` is pruned when it exceeds items + 256. (6) A `Viewport` cannot be built in tests, so `Message::TimelineScrolled` calls `App::on_timeline_scrolled(offset, viewport_h, width)`, which tests call directly. Scroll tasks are opaque, so tests read `timeline.last_scroll_request`.
- Pagination (Q16 plus the Task 7 findings): the request is sent when the content top is within one viewport above the viewport top (this also covers content shorter than the viewport, e.g. after the first `Reset` or a `Clear`), the state is `Idle`, and no `TimelineStart` item is first. At most one request per `Idle` period: `requested_at` is cleared on any `Pagination` event other than `Loading`. A request the core drops silently (permit held) gets no event, so a later scroll of `RETRY_SCROLL_PX` = 100 px or more from the offset of the request asks again. `ReachedStart` does not assume the `TimelineStart` item has arrived; both stop requests independently. Known gap: a dropped request with the user not scrolling (for example short content at the bottom) is not repeated until an `Idle` or a scroll; the core has no busy signal.
- Estimates (constants in `timeline.rs`, unmeasured, to be tuned by eye in Task 9): header 30 px, line 20 px, 7.5 px per character over `width - 80`, reply 44, reactions 30, receipts 18, failure 22, divider 36, state 28, marker 24, start 40, `Other` 0.
- Timing (Q15a), `cargo test -p twicecat-app --lib timeline_batches_under_16ms -- --nocapture`, debug build, median of 15 after one warm-up, budget 16 ms: 1 Reset of 10,000: 3.8 ms; 2 20 PushFront + Insert: 2.5 ms; 3 PushBack of 5 while scrolled up (with anchor correction): 2.2 ms; 4 100 Set + 10 Remove/Insert: 2.3 ms; 5 Clear then Reset of 10,000: 3.9 ms. The input message is built outside the timed region; the timed region is `App::update`.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget 0.14.2 `scrollable.rs` (`Viewport::{absolute_offset, bounds}` :1737-1783, bottom-anchor offset semantics :1713-1724, `AbsoluteOffset`), iced_runtime 0.14 `widget/operation.rs` (`scroll_to(id, impl Into<AbsoluteOffset<Option<f32>>>)` :31), re-exported as `iced::widget::{operation, scrollable}` (compiles). `Id` from a `&'static str` is accepted by `scroll_to`.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app lib 219 passed, 53 of them in `timeline`), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. No core or bridge change, so no ignored run.
- Open issues: none beyond the dropped-request gap above. Human items: none new (nothing is rendered yet); scroll stability under real measurement goes on the Task 9 checkpoint.

## Phase 3 / Task 9 notes
- New `crates/app/src/screens/timeline_pane.rs`: `view(&TimelineView)` (local time zone and clock) and `view_in(timeline, now_ms, tz)`. The content is `Column::from_vec([top spacer, window rows..., bottom spacer])` inside `scrollable(..).id(TIMELINE_ID).anchor_bottom().on_scroll(Message::TimelineScrolled)`; `from_vec` because `Column::push` silently drops zero-size children (found when the first layout test counted 15 children instead of 16). Each row is a `sensor` keyed by item ID with `anticipate(OVERSCAN_PX)`, `on_show` and `on_resize`, both sending `Message::RowMeasured`. Rows: message header (bold `sender_label`, `time_label`) and body (`body_text` = `multi_line_capped`, 4096 chars, 200 lines) in a separate widget below the header; continuation rows without header; `DateDivider` (`day_label`), `ReadMarker` ("New messages", palette primary), `TimelineStart`; `State` and `Placeholder` muted per Q23 (`state_text`, `placeholder_text`, pure and tested); highlighted messages on `Semantic::message_highlight` (new, contrast-tested in both palettes, as is primary on background). Ambiguous display names show the user ID too. The "Loading" row is a badge in a `stack` over the top edge of the scrollable (not a content row), so it does not change the content height.
- Removed from `main_view`: `last_messages`, `display_lines`, `DisplayLine`, `MAX_MESSAGES` and their tests (replaced by the pane tests below). `room_pane` calls `timeline_pane::view`.
- New `dates.rs` (`day_label`, `time_label`, generic over `chrono::TimeZone`, tests in +14:00, -12:00 and UTC across midnight and year boundaries, future days and out-of-range timestamps) and `text_safety::multi_line_capped` (newline variants incl. CRLF normalised to `\n`, bidi replaced, invisibles removed, line and char caps, multi-byte boundaries, early exit on huge input).
- Forward notes from Task 8: (1) A `Set` diff keeps the old measured height on purpose. If the row is built, its sensor reports the new size by itself when the height changed (`on_resize`), and a row that is not built is measured by `on_show` when it comes into view; dropping `measured` instead would leave the estimate in place when the size did not change, because the sensor would not fire. The sensor key is the item ID. Not testable headlessly, so this goes on the human list. (2) `estimate_height` is now clamped to `MAX_MEASURED_PX` (test first). (3) `last_marked` is still not wired: that is read-receipt work (Task 13). (4) The `Viewport` to model conversion in `state.rs` stays untested (a `Viewport` cannot be built); human list. (5) The estimate constants are untuned; the real row padding in the pane uses the same constants (`TOP_PAD`, `CONTINUATION_PAD`, `INDENT_PX`, `SIDE_PAD_PX`, `DIVIDER_H`, `MARKER_H`, `START_H`, now `pub`), so tuning is by eye with the seeded room (human).
- Tests: `tests/timeline_layout.rs` (Q15c, headless tiny-skia at 800x600, tokio current-thread `block_on` for `Headless::new`): 10,000 items give at most 62 content children (60 rows plus 2 spacers; about 14 at the default estimates), and after reporting each built row's laid-out height the way the sensors would, the content height is within 1 px of the prefix total; with the offset in the middle the window covers the visible index range, the top spacer equals `prefix[window.start]` and the content height again matches; an empty timeline has two empty spacers. In the lib (Q15b): a `#[cfg(test)]` thread-local counter shows built rows equal `window().len()` and at most `MAX_BUILT_ROWS` at four offsets (including `f32::MAX`), and the view build median of 15 for 10,000 items is 0.25 ms in a debug build (budget 16 ms). The Task 8 timing test still passes. TDD: the pure functions and the layout tests failed first (stubs with `todo!()`, then the child-count failure above); the view build tests were written after the view and passed on the first run.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget 0.14.2 `sensor.rs` (`Sensor::{on_show :63, on_resize :71, key :88, anticipate :133}`, `sensor` helper helpers.rs:1014), `scrollable.rs` (`id :144`, `height :156`, `on_scroll :164`, `anchor_bottom :175`), `column.rs` (`Column::push` drops void children :147, `from_vec` :77), `container.rs` (`center_x :145`, `center_y :150`, `padding :115`, `style :214`), `helpers.rs` (`stack :561`, `space :1748`); iced_core 0.14.0 `renderer.rs:121` (`Headless::new` is async, backend `Some("tiny-skia")`), `layout/limits.rs` (`Limits::new`), `layout/node.rs` (`children`, `bounds`), `widget/tree.rs` (`Tree::new`); iced 0.14.0 `advanced.rs` (`layout`, `renderer::Headless`, `widget::Tree`). chrono 0.4.45: `DateTime::from_timestamp_millis`, `with_timezone`, `naive_local().format` (a generic `DateTime<Tz>::format` needs `Offset: Display`, so the naive local time is formatted), `signed_duration_since`, `FixedOffset::{east_opt, west_opt}`.

No core or bridge change, so no ignored run.

## Phase 3 / Task 10 notes
- New `crates/app/src/links.rs`: `check_link_url` (https/http/mailto only; ASCII, no space/control/backslash, no userinfo, host via the Phase 2 `host_of`, max 2048 bytes; returns a `CheckedUrl` whose `Debug` is redacted), no label heuristic: every link asks first (decision after the final review, `phase3.fix: confirm every link before opening`; the earlier `needs_confirmation`, `visible_token` and `confirm` flag were removed because each version was bypassed), `LinkBar` and `open_link` (`open::that_detached`, reachable only through `Task::perform` in `state.rs`; a grep-style test checks that `state.rs` has no `open::`).
- New `crates/app/src/rich_text.rs`: `LinkTarget`, `SpanModel`, `Look`, `spans_for(inlines, ctx, walk)`, `to_span(model, theme)`, `blocks_view`. Each block is a `column` entry; paragraphs and headings are one `rich_text` each (`on_link_click(Message::LinkClicked)`, `Wrapping::WordOrGlyph`); code is a bordered monospace container; quote is `row![rule::vertical(3), column]` (same shape as iced's markdown widget); lists use `list_marker` (bullet, or number from `start`, saturating) in a fixed-width column so nesting indents. Mentions are pills (own user: `Semantic::highlight`; others: `Semantic::selection` with the primary colour); a spoiler is a "Spoiler" pill until clicked, then its text on a subtle background (click again to hide).
- Deviations from the plan text (all small): (1) `LinkTarget::Spoiler { item, index }` instead of `Spoiler(usize)`: `Message::LinkClicked` only carries the target, so the item ID (for "state per item and index") must travel in it; `Url(String)` is as planned. (2) `spans_for(inlines, ctx, walk)` takes a `Ctx` (own user, item ID, revealed set) and a `Walk` budget instead of a theme; colours are applied by `to_span(model, theme)`, so the model stays plain and testable. (3) `timeline_pane::view` and `view_in` take a `RichEnv { theme, own_user, revealed }` (spans cannot read the theme while drawing).
- Budget (Q20, and the Task 3/9 security notes): every text goes through `multi_line_capped` or `name_or_placeholder`/`one_line_capped` via `Walk`, which also caps a whole message at 16,384 characters and 200 lines over all blocks, and cuts block nesting at 32 (an ellipsis marks a cut). The input is truncated to the remaining budget before sanitising, so a huge text is cheap. Pill labels are one line, at most 256 characters, never blank. Link and pill labels have bidi controls replaced.
- State (`state.rs`): `Message::{LinkClicked, LinkConfirmed, LinkDismissed, LinkOpened}`, `App.revealed` (capped at 1,024, cleared on room change), `App.link_bar`. `Url` is re-checked with `check_link_url`; refused gives a "cannot be opened" bar; every allowed URL gives "Open <host>?" (for mailto, the mail domain) with Open and Cancel, and opens in a task only on `LinkConfirmed`; Cancel opens nothing. `Room` selects the room when the ID is listed (aliases are not in `RoomSummary`, so they do nothing); `User` does nothing (Phase 5); `Spoiler` toggles. The bar shows only the host, and no URL reaches `Debug` or logs (sentinel tests).
- Forward notes handled: (a) mailto cc/bcc: `check_link_url` rebuilds a mailto as `mailto:<one plain address>`, dropping the whole query and fragment, and refuses lists, `%` escapes and anything not `local@dns-domain`. (b) bidi in labels: replaced by `multi_line_capped` (kept as U+FFFD, so a label can no longer reorder itself). (c) header mimicry: rich text has no muted colour and no small size; a test asserts that no look, in either theme, gets `Semantic::muted` or a custom size, and heading sizes are all above 16. Bold plain text can still look like a name (residual risk of Q20, shared with every client). (d) Task 9 note "cap each line first": `Walk` truncates before sanitising for rich content; the plain fallback in the pane still uses `body_text`.
- Not done, by scope: typing display names (Task 13) and room-history actor names (`state_text` still uses `sender_label(.., false, ..)` for actor and target names, so ambiguous names show no user ID) stay open; the ambiguity flag needs a core change. A pill labelled with another person's name still acts without showing the target (pills do not open anything yet; Phase 5). Links no longer have that gap: every link shows its host first.
- Tests (red first): `links.rs` 10 tests (schemes, userinfo, spaces/controls/non-ASCII, length, mailto, host mismatch cases); `rich_text.rs` 17 (styles combine on a link, mention pill with `LinkTarget::User` and own-user look, room pill, bidi replaced, plain fallback lines, spoiler hide/reveal and stable indexes with nesting, budgets, label caps, no muted/size, colours, fonts, `Debug` redaction, markers, every block kind, nesting cut); `state.rs` 11 (every URL click asks first and opens only on `LinkConfirmed`, host shown, dismiss, refused links, failed launch, room pill, user pill, spoiler toggles and cap, reset on room change, no URL in `Debug`, grep test); `main_view.rs` bar text; `timeline_pane.rs` builds rich messages in both themes. The pane view tests were written after the view.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget 0.14.2 `helpers.rs:1194` (`rich_text`), `text/rich.rs` (`with_spans :68`, `size :78`, `wrapping :129`, `on_link_click :140`), `rule.rs` (`horizontal :30`, `vertical :42`), `markdown.rs:1451-1470` (quote shape), `column.rs` (`from_vec :77`); iced_core 0.14.0 `text.rs` (`Span :379`, `Span::new :415`, `font_maybe :441`, `color :447`, `link_maybe :465`, `background :471`, `border :500`, `padding :532`, `underline :538`, `strikethrough :544`), `font.rs:27` (`Font::MONOSPACE`), `padding.rs:227` (`From<[f32; 2]>`).

## Phase 3 / Task 11 notes
- New `crates/app/src/composer.rs` (pure state, no clock reads, no sends): `Composer { content: text_editor::Content, mentions, mode: Normal | Reply(id) | Edit(id), suggestions, search: SearchGate, typing: TypingGate }` with a manual `Debug` (length and mode only); `EditorAction` newtype (`Debug` shows the action kind only, so `Edit::Paste` text and typed characters never print); pure `bind(KeyInput, suggestions_open) -> Decision` plus the iced adapter `binding(KeyPress, open)`; `token_before_cursor(text, line, byte_column)`, `closed_shortcode_before_cursor`, `outgoing(&Composer, &RoomId) -> Option<OutgoingMessage>`. New `crates/app/src/emoji.rs`: `suggest` (at most 8, exact shortcode first, shortcode prefix, `[a-z0-9_+-]`), `exact(":smile:")`.
- State (`state.rs`): `Message::{Composer(EditorAction), ComposerKey(ComposerKey), SuggestionPicked(usize), MembersDue}` replace `ComposerChanged`/`ComposerSubmitted`; `App.composer: Composer`. Send builds `Command::Send(OutgoingMessage)` (Edit mode: `Command::Edit`), then resets the composer and stops typing. `CommandSink` gained `timer(after, Message)` (Bridge: the core runtime's timer, like `deadline`, which now calls it; the test sink returns no task, tests call `members_due_at` themselves). `CoreEvent::Members` fills the list only for the open room and the same query (stale answers ignored). `ActionFailed` for Send and Edit shows the non-secret error line for the open room (other actions stay ignored until Task 12/13).
- Forward notes handled: (a) member search is rate limited with a trailing edge (`SearchGate`, 150 ms): the first query goes out at once, a burst arms one timer and sends only the last query, the same query is never sent twice in a row, and a query dropped by moving the cursor away is not sent by the timer. The core still loads and sorts the whole member list per search (Task 6 note); the app now bounds it to about 6 per second. (b) typing: `SetTyping(true)` at most every 3 s while the buffer is non-empty, `false` when emptied, on send, and when another room is selected (sent for the room left, before `SelectRoom`). Deselect because the room vanished from the list, and logout, send nothing (the room or session is gone). (c) a mention label inside code: not fixable in the app (the core links the label wherever it first occurs in the source, including inside backticks, so the recipient sees raw link markup); still the Task 5 open item that needs pulldown-cmark as a direct dependency, i.e. human approval.
- Decisions inside the plan: mention label is `@` plus the display name, sanitised to one line and 64 characters (the localpart when the name is blank); at most 64 mentions are kept per message and only labels still in the text are sent; the typed `:shortcode:` closing colon replaces the shortcode with the emoji; tokens need a word start (not after a letter, digit, `_`, `:` or `@`), a mention query may hold `:` (user ID search) and is at most 64 characters, an emoji query is 2 to 32 characters. `/me` with no text sends nothing. Cancelling an Edit clears the buffer (it holds the old message, not a draft); cancelling a Reply keeps the draft. Task 12 sets `composer.mode`; the bar above the editor already shows "Replying to a message" / "Editing a message" with Cancel.
- Tests: `composer.rs` 43 (bindings, adapter, Debug redaction, tokens at line start, mid-word, after punctuation, multi-line, multi-byte, user-ID queries, closed shortcodes, search gate with a scripted clock, typing gate with a scripted clock, picking replaces multi-byte text, emoji pick, escape ladder, outgoing trim/blank/`/me`/mentions/reply/edit); `emoji.rs` 5; `state.rs` replaced the Phase 2 composer tests and added send, edit/reply commands, typing throttle through `update` (scripted `Instant`), stop on room switch, rate-limited search and answer filling, other-room answer ignored, suggestion keys, ActionFailed line; the Phase 2 redaction test now pastes `secret-body-x` through `Message::Composer`. `tests/bridge.rs`: ignored test `typing_markdown_and_pressing_enter_shows_a_bold_message_in_the_timeline` (alice selects a room, types `**hi**`, Enter via `bind`, the applied timeline shows a `Message` with bold `hi`; passed in about 5 s). TDD: the pure composer functions and the emoji module were red first (`todo!()` stubs, 23 and 5 failures seen); the `Composer` methods were red first as well (14 failures); the `state.rs` integration tests and the bridge test were written after the code.

## Phase 3 / Task 12 notes
- New `crates/app/src/actions.rs` (pure): `action_buttons(&MessageDetails, is_own) -> ActionButtons` (Edit needs `can_edit` and own; Delete only when the core's `can_redact` is set, since the server enforces power levels), `quote_line` (first non-blank line, one line, 120 characters), `quote_for(items, id)` (sanitised sender and quote for the reply bar), `key_label`, `notice_text`, and `ActionState { hovered, confirm_delete, picker, notice, notice_seq }` (manual `Debug`, no picker query). `emoji.rs` gained `QUICK_REACTIONS` and `picker_results(query)` (at most 64; empty gives the quick row then the first group; otherwise name or shortcode substring, query trimmed, lowercased and capped at 64 characters).
- State (`state.rs`): new messages `HoverEnter/HoverExit(id)` (an exit clears only its own ID), `ReplyRequested`, `EditRequested`, `CopyRequested`, `DeleteRequested`, `DeleteConfirmed`, `DeleteCancelled`, `ModeCancelled`, `PickerOpened/Query/Submitted/Picked/Closed`, `ReactionToggled { item, key }`, `NoticeExpired(seq)`. Typed text and reaction keys travel as `MessageText`, so `Debug` of a `Message` prints lengths only (unit-tested with sentinels). Every action re-checks the item and its `ItemActions` at the time it runs (an item that vanished or no longer allows it sends nothing). Delete asks inline (`confirm_delete`), then sends `Redact { reason: None }`. Copy is `clipboard::write(body)` (the plain body). The picker pick sends `ToggleReaction` and closes it. `ActionFailed` for `Redact` and `React` in the open room sets a one-line notice and arms a 5 s `sink.timer(NoticeExpired(seq))`; a stale timer cannot clear a newer notice. Selecting a room or deselecting resets the action state.
- Edit mode: `Composer::begin_edit` keeps the current draft in memory (`saved_draft`, never logged), loads the plain `body` and puts the cursor at the end. Escape and the bar's cross restore the draft (`cancel_mode`, `restore_draft`). Plan deviation: sending an edit also restores the draft (`finish_send`), rather than leaving an empty editor; otherwise the draft would be lost on a send. `Composer::clear` still drops it (room switch). Reply while editing leaves the edit first. Forward note from Task 11 handled for the paths this task touches: leaving Edit mode through Escape or the cross sends `SetTyping(false)` when the composer ends up empty (`after_edit_left`). NOT changed: `deselect()` (room vanished, logout) still resets the typing gate without a notice, because the room or session is gone and `deselect` cannot send.
- View: `timeline_pane` wraps each message row in a `mouse_area` (`on_enter`/`on_exit`) and, while hovered or while its delete question is open, a `stack` layer in the top right corner holds the action bar, so the bar never changes the row height (the sensors keep their measurements). `RichEnv` gained `actions: &ActionState`. The reactions row (`key count` chips, `button::primary` when `own`, a trailing "+" chip) is part of the row, in a wrapping `Row`; chips toggle only where `can_react`. The reply/edit bar in `main_view` shows `Replying to <sender>: <first line>` (or "Editing a message") with a cross (`ModeCancelled`). The picker is a `stack` layer over the timeline pane's bottom right corner (plan deviation: not iced's `float` widget, which only scales and translates content; it is not an overlay), with a search field (focused on open, Enter picks the first result) and a wrapping grid of emoji buttons. The failure notice sits under the timeline in the danger colour. The editor got `COMPOSER_ID` and is focused after Reply and Edit.
- Not done / limits: the picker grid shows only group 1 while the search is empty (as the acceptance criterion says); the other groups are reachable through search. No Escape key for the picker (use the cross); Enter in the search field picks the first result. Reaction chips are in SDK order, capped at 24 by the core (own reactions are not moved to the front; Task 6 Low). Delete is hidden when `can_redact` is false, but a lie from the core would still be refused by the server and shown as a notice.
- Tests: `actions.rs` 7, `emoji.rs` +2, `composer.rs` +4 (begin_edit, draft restore, cancel, finish_send), `timeline_pane.rs` +3 (chip label, bar visibility, rows with bar and chips build), `main_view.rs` +1 (mode text), `state.rs` +14 (hover, reply, edit and draft, typing stop on leaving edit, edit send, reply during edit, delete confirm and refusals, copy source, picker pick and Enter, chips, notice and expiry, room switch reset, Debug redaction). TDD: emoji `picker_results` and the `actions.rs` helpers were red first (stubs, 1 and 2 failures seen), the composer edit methods were red first (3 failures seen after neutralising `restore_draft`), the `state.rs` tests failed to compile before the messages existed; the pane and `main_view` view tests were written after the views.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget 0.14.2 `helpers.rs` (`stack :561`, `opaque :577`, `mouse_area :2010`), `mouse_area.rs` (`on_enter :106`, `on_exit :120`), `row.rs` (`wrap :158`, `Wrapping::vertical_spacing :376`), `container.rs` (`align_right :173`, `align_bottom :183`, `bordered_box :595`), `button.rs` (`danger :658`), `text_editor.rs` (`id :166`, `Content::with_text :405`), `text_input.rs` (`id :157`, `on_input :172`, `on_submit :194`); iced_runtime 0.14.0 `widget/operation.rs` (`focus :65`), `clipboard.rs` (`write :49`); iced_core 0.14.0 `text/editor.rs` (`Motion::DocumentEnd :146`); emojis 0.9.0 `lib.rs` (`Emoji::name :308`, `Group::emojis :667`, `Group::SmileysAndEmotion`). iced's `float` (`helpers.rs :2084`) was read and rejected (see View).
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked`, `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. No core or bridge change, so no ignored run.
- Open issues: the hover bar overlays the top right of a short (one-line) row and may cover the end of the text; tune at the checkpoint. A row's hover state follows `mouse_area` enter and exit, so fast moves between rows rely on the exit-only-if-same rule.

## Phase 3 / Task 13 notes
- Receipts (Q12), model in `timeline.rs`: `TimelineView::newest_receipt_target()` (newest event from another user that the server has: skips dividers, markers, the start item, local echoes and the user's own messages) and `read_step(focused, now) -> ReadStep::{Idle, Send, Wait(d)}` with `bottom_seen`, `last_marked` (now wired; it was only a field before), and a private last-send time and timer flag. A receipt is due when the window has focus, the bottom is in view (at most 2 px away) and the target differs from `last_marked`; at most one per second (`RECEIPT_MIN_INTERVAL`): a receipt that comes early asks for one timer (`Message::ReadDue`) and no more until it fires. `App::timeline_effects` (called after every diff, measurement, scroll and pagination change) and `Message::WindowFocus` / `Message::ReadDue` run it and send `Command::MarkRead { fully_read: false }`. `Message::SelectRoom` first sends `MarkRead { fully_read: true }` for the room being left when its `bottom_seen` is set. Window focus comes from `iced::event::listen_with` on `window::Event::{Focused, Unfocused}` in `App::subscription`; `App.window_focused` starts `true`, so a window that never reports a focus event still sends receipts.
- Display: `timeline_pane::seen_by_line(read_by, known)` gives "Seen by A, B, C +N" (3 names, `MAX_SEEN_NAMES`), a muted 12 px line under messages, placeholders and state rows. Names come from `known_name`: the sender name of the newest earlier message by that user (searching 500 items back), else the localpart; all names are `one_line_capped` to 24 characters; an ambiguous known name also shows the (capped) user ID.
- Typing (Q13) display: `App.typing` (at most `MAX_TYPING_KEPT` = 3 users, other rooms ignored, cleared on room switch and deselect) and `timeline_pane::typing_line(&[TypingUser])`: nothing, "A is typing\u{2026}", "A and B are typing\u{2026}", "Several people are typing\u{2026}". Shown as a muted line above the composer in `main_view`. Names are capped at 24 characters; two typing users whose cleaned names are equal both get their user ID. The sending side (3 s throttle, stop on send, clear and room switch) was Tasks 11 and 12 and is unchanged.
- Throttling (forward note): `MarkRead` is rate-limited and never repeated for the same item (see above); `SetTyping` was already throttled by `TypingGate`.
- Local echo (Q11): `Echo::{Delivered, Sending, Failed { recoverable }}` from `echo_of(sent, details)`. `Sending` rows get a muted "\u{25F7} Sending\u{2026}" line (and a muted plain-text body; formatted bodies stay in the normal colour because the rich text spans carry no muted colour); `Failed` rows get a danger-colour line "Failed to send" (plus ". Will retry when back online" when `recoverable`) with **Retry** and **Discard** buttons. New messages `RetryRequested(id)` and `DiscardRequested(id)` send `Command::RetrySend` / `DiscardSend` for the open room only while the item is a message with a `send_failure`; anything else sends nothing. The estimate adds room for the status line for any echo that is not `Sent`. The retry, discard, resume-on-reconnect and unwedge logic itself is core Task 5; the app only sends the commands.
- Unchanged because they need a core change: ambiguous-name flags for room-history actor and target names (`state_text` still passes `false`) and for typing names against the room's other members (only duplicates inside the typing list are detected). Typing and receipt names are sanitised at render. `deselect()` (room vanished, logout) sends no `fully_read` marker, because the room or session is gone.
- Tests (red first, seen as compile failures): `timeline.rs` +6 (receipt target, one send per new bottom item and none for the same item, none while scrolled up or unfocused, the 1 s debounce with a scripted clock, reset per room, echo estimate), `state.rs` +7 (receipt through `update`, scrolled-up case, focus, `fully_read` on leaving, no marker when the bottom was never seen, typing events, Retry/Discard commands), `timeline_pane.rs` +8 (`typing_line` for 0, 1, 2 and 3+ users, sanitising, caps and twins; `seen_by_line`; `known_name`; `echo_of`; `echo_text`; rows with echoes and receipts build). The pane view test and the estimate change were written after the code. The Task 8 timing test and the Task 9 layout test still pass. One existing test (`short_content_after_the_first_reset_requests_one_page`) now looks for the `PaginateBackwards` command anywhere in the batch instead of last, because a `MarkRead` can follow it.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced 0.14.0 `lib.rs:582-585` (`iced::event::{Event, listen_with}`), `window.rs:7-8` (re-exports of `window::Event`), iced_futures 0.14.0 `event.rs:26` (`listen_with` takes a plain `fn`, so the closure captures nothing), iced_core 0.14.0 `window/event.rs:43-46` (`Event::Focused`, `Event::Unfocused`), iced_runtime 0.14.0 `window.rs:216` (`events`, read for the pattern). No matrix-sdk API was added (the commands already existed in the core).
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app lib 384 passed), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. Ignored run (`create-test-users.sh`, then `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`): the first run failed once in `timeline_live.rs` (`typing_notices_reach_the_other_user`, panic at line 332; the Task 7 note already records one earlier timeout in this test; no core or bridge code changed here), and the whole run passed after `create-test-users.sh` again with `--no-fail-fast` (2 min 18 s with build); `docker compose stop` afterwards. Watch this test: one more failure in a row would be the rule 8 "flaky twice" stop.
- Open issues: see "Phase 3 open issues (summary)". Human items: the new lines in the Phase 3 human checkpoint.

### Scroll reset on back-pagination (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- Symptom: scrolling up the seeded room jumped back to the newest message after each loaded page, so history seemed to stop a few hundred messages up (9500, then 6700).
- Cause: `timeline_pane::view_in` returned `stack([list, badge])` while `Loading` and the bare scrollable otherwise. iced replaces a widget's state when the widget type at that place in the tree changes (iced_core 0.14.0 `widget/tree.rs:63-67`, `Tree::diff`), so the scrollable's offset reset to 0 (the bottom, as it is anchored there) at every `Loading` and `Idle`. Found with a temporary numbers-only trace in the app; the core was ruled out first (80 back-paginations through the seeded room, no `Clear` or `Reset`).
- Fix: the pane is always a stack with the scrollable as the first layer; the second layer is the badge or an empty `space()`. iced_widget 0.14.2 `stack.rs:153` diffs children by position, so the scrollable keeps its state.
- Tests: `the_scroll_state_survives_a_pagination` (`timeline_pane.rs`: the root and first-layer tags stay the same across `Loading` and `Idle`; seen failing on the old code). `tests/timeline_layout.rs` now goes stack, scrollable, content.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` pass. The human confirmed the fix in the real app.

### Edited marker (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- The core already set `MessageDetails::edited` (`timeline.rs:126`, from `Message::is_edited`), but the pane never drew it. Now `timeline_pane::edited_text` gives "(edited)" for edited messages, shown as a muted 12 px line under the body; the estimate adds `EDITED_H` (18 px). Under the body rather than inline after the last word, because rich text has no muted inline style.
- Tests (red first): `only_edited_messages_get_the_marker` (`timeline_pane.rs`), `an_edited_message_estimate_has_room_for_its_marker` (`timeline.rs`). Checks: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` pass. Rendering is on the human checkpoint.

### Hover background (human request at the checkpoint, option picked by the human)
- The human found it hard to tell where one message ends in a run from the same sender. Picked option: a hover background (alternatives offered: time on hover, an always-visible gap). New `Semantic::message_hover` (light `#F3F4F6`, dark `#2B2D31`); `timeline_pane::row_background(sem, highlighted, hovered)` gives the mention highlight first, then the hover colour; `framed` takes `hovered` (from `bar_visible`, so a row whose delete question is open keeps it too). Placeholder and state rows are not hoverable.
- Tests (red first): `a_hovered_row_gets_the_hover_background_and_a_mention_keeps_its_own` (`timeline_pane.rs`), `the_hover_background_differs_from_the_others` and two new pairs in `colours_are_readable_in_both_palettes` (`theme.rs`). Checks: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` pass. Rendering is on the human checkpoint.

### Reply quotes and jumping (human request at the checkpoint, option picked by the human)
- Found while doing it: the pane never drew reply quotes, though the plan asks for "reply (with a quote)" and the estimate reserved `REPLY_H`. Now `timeline_pane::reply_quote` draws a 3 px rule with the sender (bold 12 px) and the first line (muted 13 px), via `reply_quote_text` (sanitised and capped; `Loading` and `Unavailable` have their own lines). It is a `mouse_area` with a pointer cursor that sends `Message::ReplyQuoteClicked(EventId)`.
- Core API (additive): `api::EventId`; `TimelineItem.event_id: Option<EventId>` (from `EventTimelineItem::event_id`, matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `timeline/event_item/mod.rs:279`; `None` for virtual items and unsent echoes); `ReplyPreview.event_id` (from `InReplyToDetails::event_id`, `timeline/event_item/content/reply.rs:36`). `check-core-api.sh` and `check-app-deps.sh` pass.
- Jump (option picked: load back until found): `TimelineView::jump_to` scrolls to a loaded target (30% below the viewport top, `JUMP_TOP_FRACTION`) and sets `flash` (ended by `Message::FlashEnded` after `FLASH_DURATION`, 2 s; drawn with `Semantic::selection`, before mention and hover in `row_background(sem, RowLook)`). Otherwise it asks one page per `Idle` period, at most `JUMP_MAX_PAGES` (50, about 1,000 events), and gives up at the start of the room or when the budget is spent, with the notice `JUMP_FAILED_TEXT`. The core reports `Idle` before a page's items, so giving up is decided only in `apply_diffs`, never on a pagination state change or a row measurement. Selecting another room drops the jump and the flash.
- Tests (red first): `timeline.rs` +7 (loaded target, paging until found, start of room, page budget, a measurement before the last page does not end the jump (seen failing with the jump driven from `settle`), flash end, room switch); `state.rs` +3 (flash and its timer, `PaginateBackwards` for an unloaded target, notice at the start); `timeline_pane.rs` +4 (flash wins, hover/mention updated to `RowLook`, quote text, sanitising). Ignored `reply_round_trip` (`timeline_actions.rs`) now also checks the quote's event ID against the original's; run alone against the local server without `create-test-users.sh` (it would remove alice from the seeded room), passed.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 405 passed) pass. The human confirmed it in the real app.

### Slash commands as in Element (human request at the checkpoint)
- Asked: `/html …` from Twicecat sent "/html" plus a markdown link; the human asked to behave as Element Web. Rules taken from element-web v1.12.30 (the `docker-compose.yml` image) via `gh api`: `apps/web/src/editor/commands.tsx` (`isSlashCommand`, `shouldSendAnyway`), `slash-commands/SlashCommands.tsx` (`/html` = `makeHtmlMessage(text, text)`, `/plain` = `makeTextMessage`, `parseCommandString`), `components/views/rooms/SendMessageComposer.tsx` (`//` strips one slash; unknown command asks "Send as message"), `editor/serialize.ts` (`\/` unescape), `i18n/strings/en_EN.json` (wording).
- Core API (additive): `api::TextFormat { Markdown (default), Plain, Html }` and `OutgoingMessage.format`. `outgoing_content`: `Plain` gives `*_plain` content; `Html` gives `*_html(text, text)` with the formatted body through the same `sanitize_formatted` as markdown output. Both list the picked mentions in `m.mentions` without pills (`with_picked_mentions`), as Element's `attachMentions` (`apps/web/src/utils/messages.ts:46`, called for command content in `SendMessageComposer.tsx:401`); the first version wrongly sent none and was corrected after the human found `/me` alone did nothing. ruma-events 0.35.0 `room/message/without_relation.rs:50-87` for the constructors.
- App: `composer::outgoing` returns `Outgoing { Send, UnknownCommand(name), Nothing }` (pure `parse_command`); `composer::as_text` sends the whole text as markdown ("Send as message"). `App.command_bar` and `Message::{CommandSendAnyway, CommandCancelled}`; `submit_composer` and the bar share `send_composed`; the bar closes with the room (`reset_link_state`). `main_view::command_bar_text` uses Element's wording; the name is `one_line_capped` to 64 characters. Behaviour change, as in Element: `/mean` is now an unknown command (it was sent as text). A known command without its text (`/me`, `/html`, `/plain` alone) gives `Outgoing::CommandError` and the notice `COMMAND_ERROR_TEXT` (Element's "Command error: Unable to handle slash command.", from `command.ts` `run` rejecting `/me`, which has no `runFn`); Element would send an empty `/html` or `/plain`, Twicecat reports the error for all three.
- Tests (red first): `outgoing.rs` +2 (plain; html body, sanitising and no markdown, no mentions; the html test was tightened after it first passed without the change); `composer.rs` +4 and the `/mean` case changed; `state.rs` +3 (ask, send anyway, cancel, html format, bar closes with the room); `main_view.rs` +1; follow-up +3 (`plain_and_html_keep_the_mentions_like_element`, `a_command_without_its_text_is_an_error_like_element`, `me_alone_shows_elements_command_error`) and `commands_keep_their_mentions_like_element` replacing the opposite test. No live test: the core tests check the exact content sent. Checks: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 413, core lib 223), `check-core-api.sh`, `check-app-deps.sh` pass.

### Pasted room links become pills (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- Element sends a pasted "Copy room link" as plain text only (checked on the local server: no `formatted_body`, the body holds `https://matrix.to/#/!…:localhost?via=localhost`) and turns permalinks into pills when it displays them. Twicecat's `linkify` made them web links, so the raw URL showed and a click opened the browser.
- Core (`rich_text.rs`): a linkified URL that `mention_target` accepts (matrix.to user, room ID, alias or event permalink) becomes `Inline::Mention`, labelled by `id_label` (the user ID, room ID or alias); an anchor whose text is its own permalink gets the same label. Other URLs stay links.
- App: `RichEnv.rooms` / `Ctx.rooms` (the room list); a room pill whose label is its bare room ID shows the known room's name (through `take_label`, so sanitised and capped); a sender's own label is kept. Clicking already switched rooms (`LinkTarget::Room`).
- Tests (red first): core +2 (`a_pasted_permalink_in_plain_text_is_a_pill_like_element`, `an_anchor_whose_text_is_its_permalink_gets_the_id_as_label`), app +1 (`a_room_pill_without_its_own_label_shows_the_known_room_name`). Checks on the committed tree alone (the unconfirmed pill-margin and `/spoiler` work set aside): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 416, core lib 226), `check-core-api.sh`, `check-app-deps.sh` pass.

### `/spoiler` and emoticon commands (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- `/spoiler …` showed the unknown-command bar. Element v1.12.30 `SlashCommands.tsx`: `/spoiler` = `makeHtmlMessage(message, <span data-mx-spoiler>${message}</span>)` (unescaped); `slash-commands/emoticon.ts`: `/shrug`, `/tableflip`, `/unflip`, `/lenny` send the emoticon plus " <text>" as plain text.
- Core API (additive): `TextFormat::Spoiler`; `outgoing_content` sends the text as the body and the span (through `sanitize_formatted`, which keeps `data-mx-spoiler`) as the formatted body, with the picked mentions. App: `Parsed::{Spoiler, Emoticon}`; the emoticons are copied byte for byte from Element's source (`composer::emoticon`); `/spoiler` alone is a `CommandError`. `/rainbow` is now the unknown-command example in tests (`/shrug` became a command).
- Tests (red first): core `spoiler_format_wraps_the_text_like_element`; app `spoiler_and_emoticon_commands_like_element`. Checks on the committed tree alone: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 417, core lib 227), both boundary scripts pass.

### Pill margins and the leading mention colon (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- In Twicecat the ":" after an Element mention ("alice: hi") was drawn in the pill colour. The core maps it right (`Mention` then `Text(": hi")`); the pill's `.padding([0.0, 4.0])` was the cause: iced_core 0.14.0 `text.rs:525-528`, span padding "only affects the background and border", so the background covered 4 px of each neighbour. `rich_text::to_span` now puts a U+202F NARROW NO-BREAK SPACE (`PILL_MARGIN`) inside mention and spoiler pills and drops the padding.
- Composer: as Element (`parts.ts` `createMentionParts`, setting `MessageComposerInput.insertTrailingColon` default true in `Settings.tsx:704`), a member picked at the very start of the message is followed by ": ", elsewhere by " ". Twicecat keeps "@" in the label (labels are found by text when sending; without "@" an earlier plain name could be linked).
- Tests (red first): `pills_take_their_margins_in_the_text_so_neighbours_are_not_covered` (`rich_text.rs`), `only_a_mention_starting_the_message_gets_a_colon_like_element` (`composer.rs`); two existing tests now expect "@Name: " at the start. Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 419, core lib 227) pass.

### Composer focus with bars above it (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- Typing `@a` opened the suggestion list and the editor lost its focus. Same cause as the scroll reset: `main_view::room_pane` pushed the bars (link and command bars, send error, notice, typing line, suggestion list, reply/edit bar) into the same column as the editor, so the editor's index changed whenever one came or went and `Tree::diff` (iced_core 0.14.0 `widget/tree.rs:63-67`) made it a new, unfocused state. Likewise the timeline became a `stack` only while the reaction picker was open, which reset its scroll.
- Fix: `room_column` is always `[title, timeline, bars column, editor]` (`Column::from_vec`, which keeps an empty bars column; `push` would drop a void child, iced_widget 0.14.2 `column.rs:140-154`), and `timeline_layer` is always `stack([timeline, picker or space()])`.
- Test: `the_editor_and_the_timeline_keep_their_place_when_bars_come_and_go` (`main_view.rs`): the tags of the column's children and of the timeline layer stay the same as bars and the picker come and go; seen failing with the helpers written the old way. Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 420, core lib 227) pass.

### Per-room drafts (human request at the checkpoint; option picked: saved to disk)
- The Q24 scope cut is lifted. Storage: matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `Room::{save,load,clear}_composer_draft` (`room/mod.rs:4083-4114`), so drafts live in the session's SQLite state store opened with the session passphrase (`client.rs:209`) and go with `remove_session_store_dir` at logout. `ComposerDraft`'s derived `Debug` prints the text: the core never logs it.
- Core API (additive): `api::Draft { text, mentions, reply_to: Option<EventId> }` (redacted `Debug`), `Command::SaveDraft { room_id, draft: Option<Draft> }` (handled inline in `handle`, not in the action set, so a save before `Shutdown` completes), `CoreEvent::DraftLoaded { room_id, draft }` (sent by `forward_timeline` right after the first `Reset`, so a reply can find its item). New `drafts.rs`: `to_sdk` / `from_sdk`; the text is the markdown source, and the HTML copy links each picked mention (escaped text, first free occurrence) so mentions survive; a stored `Edit` draft is never restored.
- App: `Composer::draft_parts` (during an edit, the set-aside draft) and `load_draft` (only into an empty composer in normal mode). `App::save_draft` maps the reply item to its event; it runs before `SelectRoom`, before `Shutdown`, after a send, and on `Message::DraftDue(seq)` 1 s (`DRAFT_SAVE_DELAY`) after the last composer change. It saves only when the composer was touched since the room was opened (`draft_dirty`), and `SelectRoom` bumps `draft_seq`, so the next room's draft is never wiped before it arrives. A reply whose message is not loaded comes back as a plain draft.
- Tests: `drafts.rs` +5 (written with the module, not red first); `state.rs` +10, red first (save before select, clear after emptying, untouched composer saves nothing, stale timer, load with reply and mentions, never over typed text, send clears, edit keeps the set-aside draft, save before shutdown, debounce); ignored live `a_draft_is_kept_per_room_and_cleared_on_request` (`timeline_live.rs`), passed alone against the local server. Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 430, core lib 232), `check-core-api.sh`, `check-app-deps.sh` pass. The human checked it in the real app, including a restart and a kill.

### Emotes shown as "* Name text" (human-reported at the checkpoint)
- Sending was right (an `m.emote`); the pane ignored `MessageDetails::kind`, so emotes looked like plain messages. `timeline_pane::with_emote_prefix` puts "* name " (the header's sanitised `sender_label`) at the start of the first paragraph, or on its own line before a list or quote; the plain-body path prefixes the text. Notices (`m.notice`) are still drawn like text (Element greys them); not changed.
- Test (red first): `an_emote_starts_with_a_star_and_the_name_like_element`. Checks on the committed tree alone: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 431, core lib 232) pass.

### `/rainbow`, `/rainbowme` and the usage line (human request at the checkpoint)
- Element v1.12.30 `SlashCommands.tsx:637-655` and `utils/colour.ts` (`textToHtmlRainbow`, `labToRGB`). Core API (additive): `TextFormat::Rainbow`; `outgoing::rainbow_html` is a line-for-line port (UTF-16 length for the hue step, as JavaScript's `str.length`). Two deliberate differences: characters are HTML-escaped (Element inserts them raw), and without a grapheme table (no new dependency) `rainbow_chunks` keeps combining marks, variation selectors, emoji modifiers, tags and ZWJ sequences with the character before them, where Element uses lodash `split`. App: `Parsed::{Rainbow, RainbowEmote}`.
- A known command without its text now names itself: `Outgoing::CommandError(name)`, and `state::command_error_text` gives Element's usage line "Command error: Usage: /<name> <message>" (`Command::getUsage`, `command.ts:122`), except `/me`, which keeps "Unable to handle slash command.". `/topic` replaces `/rainbow` as the unknown-command example.
- Tests (red first): core `rainbow_html_matches_elements_snapshot` (byte-equal to element-web `slash-commands/__snapshots__/rainbow.test.ts.snap`), `rainbow_format_sends_the_text_and_coloured_html` (also after sanitising, and escaping), `rainbow_keeps_combined_characters_together`; app composer assertions for `/rainbow`, `/rainbowme` and their errors, `a_command_missing_its_text_shows_elements_usage_line`. Checks on the committed tree alone: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 432, core lib 235), both boundary scripts pass.

### Readable sender text colours (human request at the checkpoint; option picked: readable colours)
- Replaces the plan's choice to ignore colours (plan line 167, rejected over contrast in both themes). Core API (additive): `TextStyle.color: Option<[u8; 3]>`; `rich_text` keeps `data-mx-color` on `span` or `font` and the older `font color`, only as a strict `#rrggbb` (`parse_hex_color`); CSS `style` colours stay ignored; nested elements inherit.
- App: `theme::readable_on(colour, background)` keeps a colour at `MIN_TEXT_CONTRAST` (4.5:1, the bar the app's own colours meet) or above as sent, else mixes it toward white on a dark background or black on a light one in 5 % steps until it reaches it; `rich_text::to_span` applies it to text, and links and pills keep their own colours.
- Tests (red first): core `text_colours_are_kept_like_element`, `only_six_digit_hex_colours_are_kept`, `elements_rainbow_arrives_in_colour`; `unknown_elements_are_unwrapped_and_colours_ignored` became `…_only_matrix_colours_kept` (the decision changed); app `sender_colours_are_made_readable_on_both_themes` (a hue sweep plus white, black and the background itself, on both palettes), `sender_colours_are_drawn_readably_but_not_on_links_or_pills`. Checks: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 434, core lib 238), both boundary scripts pass.

### Full ignored run after the checkpoint fixes (2026-10-06)
- `create-test-users.sh`, then `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` (the human agreed to lose the seeded room). First run: 2 failures in `timeline_live.rs`. `a_draft_is_kept_per_room_and_cleared_on_request` was a bug in the test (it waited for the second room, then `open_room` started a fresh list and waited for an update that had already gone by when both rooms came in one batch); it now waits for both rooms in one pass. `typing_notices_reach_the_other_user` failed at line 332 ("carol never saw alice typing"), its third first-run failure (Task 7, Task 13, now), each time passing on the next run.
- Second run, `--no-fail-fast` after `create-test-users.sh` again: every ignored test passed (100 s with build). Then `docker compose --profile element stop`.
- Plan gate on the tip: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app lib 434, core lib 238), `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok), `check-core-api.sh`, `check-app-deps.sh` pass. Before this, the checkpoint fix commits ran the AGENTS.md gate (fmt, clippy, workspace tests) and the boundary scripts, but not `cargo deny`, the `--all-features --locked` forms or the full ignored suite.
- Watch `typing_notices_reach_the_other_user`: it has never failed twice in a row, but a second consecutive failure is the rule 8 stop.

## Phase 3 open issues (summary)
Fixes the human may want before or after the checkpoint, from the task notes and review ledger:
- **IME input not checked (deferred by the human, 2026-10-06):** composing with an input method (ibus/fcitx on Linux, the macOS and Windows IMEs) is untested on screen: Enter must commit the candidate without sending, the candidate window must sit near the caret, and Enter after commit sends. To check in a later phase.
- **Editor buffer not zeroized (Q17):** iced's `text_editor::Content` and the `String` from `Content::text()` keep message text in memory (extends the Phase 2 password issue).
- **Residual body-styling spoof (Q20):** a message body can contain bold text that looks like a sender name; bodies are sanitised and confined to the indented content column below a bold header, but the risk is shared with every client. Pill labels are sender-chosen and can differ from their targets (links always show the host before opening); a mention label inside code shows raw link markup to recipients (the fix needs pulldown-cmark as a direct dependency, i.e. approval).
- **Upstream exposure (Task 4 re-audit):** the core's own parse of a `formatted_body` is fixed (`ParsedHtml` tears the ruma-html tree down iteratively; `MAX_TAGS` unchanged, measured: `"<table><td>".repeat(32000)` in a debug build on a 2 MiB thread is fine, 16x the cap). The SDK also parses the same `formatted_body` in `matrix-sdk-ui` `content/message.rs` with the recursive drop and showed the same debug abort; that is upstream code the app cannot fix.
- **Raw HTML passthrough on send (Q10):** pulldown-cmark passes raw inline HTML through; the core sanitises the generated HTML after the fact (ruma-html strict config plus removal of img, script and style, and a 1,500-tag guard) and receivers sanitise again (Q4/Q5). Tested with `<img onerror>`, `<script>`, `javascript:` links and tag floods.
- **Phase 2 `text_safety` ranges:** still hand-written by the human's decision (Unicode crate rejected, Q1). The known gaps (unlisted combining marks, joiners and selectors after any base, up to 8 tag characters after U+1F3F4, invisible-only names) now apply to message bodies, names, pills and reaction keys too.
- **Name ambiguity:** room-history actor and target names, reply previews and typing names carry no core ambiguity flag, so a duplicate display name can impersonate (needs a core change; typing shows the ID only for twins inside the typing list).
- **Copy** writes the raw plain body to the clipboard (hidden control characters, bidi: pastejacking); the reply quote bar shows unrevealed spoiler text from the plain body; there is no app-side cap on reaction chips (the core caps at 24 groups, in SDK order).
- **Scope cuts (Q24):** media rendering and sending; threads; per-room drafts (added at the checkpoint at the human's request, kept in the session store); edit history; collapsing state-event runs; link previews; custom emoji; slash commands other than `/me`, `/plain` and `/html` (those two added at the checkpoint, at the human's request; any other `/command` asks before sending, as in Element); user profiles from pills; E2EE flows (Phase 4); joining rooms from pills (Phase 5); private receipts and typing-off settings; i18n.
- **Behaviour gaps:** a `PaginateBackwards` dropped by the core is re-asked only after a 100 px scroll or the next `Idle` (short content with no scroll can stall); the member search loads and sorts the whole member list per query; `rank_members` matches the server part of user IDs; `deselect()` (room vanished, logout) sends no `SetTyping(false)` or `fully_read`; a failed send loses the typed text; `set_room_subscriptions` is never narrowed when the selection changes (check the SDK replaces the set).
- **Test notes:** run `scripts/create-test-users.sh` before each ignored run (accounts accumulating rooms made `timeline_items.rs` time out); `typing_notices_reach_the_other_user` (`timeline_live.rs`) failed once in Task 13's first ignored run and once as a timeout in Task 7, passing on rerun both times. Several view tests were written after the views (the pane, `main_view`, Task 13's row test); rendering itself is untestable here and is on the checkpoint.
- **Local Element Web (checkpoint helper):** `docker compose --profile element up -d` serves Element Web v1.12.30 at http://localhost:8080, preconfigured for the local Synapse (`dev/element/config.json`). It is behind a profile, so the CI `docker compose up -d --wait` does not start it. Needed because browsers block app.element.io from calling `http://localhost` ("does not appear to be a valid homeserver").

- **`timeline_items` flake fixed (phase4.1 follow-up).** Cause: a test race, not Task 1 code. In `membership_and_profile_changes_become_state_items`, carol joined and renamed herself right after `SelectRoom`, before the room subscription took effect. The sync response then carried only the latest events and was limited, so the SDK cleared the timeline; the failing run showed only a late `DisplayName` item (invite and join gone), and the test waited its 30 s. Carol's display name is also account-wide, so `notices_and_emotes...` renaming her in parallel could add or overwrite name events in the same room. It depends on timing, which is why it passed alone or after `e2ee_rooms`; the Phase 3 "accumulating rooms" explanation was not the cause (it also failed right after `create-test-users.sh`). Fix, test only, assertions unchanged: the test now waits for the invite, then joins and waits for the join, then renames and waits for the name; and the two tests that rename carol hold a per-binary lock (`CAROL_PROFILE`).

NotFound" in `avatars`/`bridge`; `cargo clean -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app` fixed it. Not a repo problem.
