## Phase 4 / Task 4 notes
- TDD: the unit tests in `crates/core/src/security.rs` (`status_table`, `uia_password_carries_user_password_and_session`, `reset_outcome_branches`) were written first against `todo!()` bodies and failed (3 failed), then the code. The integration tests were written after the code; the status test was seen red by turning `start_security` into a no-op for one run (timed out waiting for `Security`), then restored. Several failures on the way were real findings (below).
- New private `crates/core/src/security.rs` (SDK types stay inside; no logging of passwords, UIA sessions or URLs):
  - `compute_status(&StatusInputs)` (pure, table-tested incl. identity present but no private keys: `MissingKeys`; identity unknown: `Unknown`). The forwarder (`forward_status`) merges `recovery().state_stream()`, `verification_state()`, `devices_stream()` and `user_identities_stream()` plus a `Notify` that actions use to ask for a recomputation, and emits `Security` once at start and then only when the status changed. `identity_exists` is only "false" after the server was asked (`has_devices_to_verify_against` runs the initial key query); on a failed query it stays `Unknown`.
  - `BootstrapCrossSigning`: `bootstrap_cross_signing_if_needed(None)`; a UIA answer gives `ReauthRequired { BootstrapCrossSigning }` without a password, else one retry through `bootstrap_cross_signing(Some(AuthData))` with the session of the first answer (the SDK's own documented retry). `ResetIdentity`: `recovery().reset_identity()` then `reset_outcome(..)` (Done / NeedPassword / Retry / Approve(url) / Unsupported). OAuth: the URL goes through `oauth::check_auth_url` (https or loopback only, no userinfo), then `OpenAccountManagement(url)` and `handle.reset(None)` polls (2 minutes in the SDK); `CancelIdentityReset` calls `handle.cancel()` and the action ends with `ActionFailed { Cancelled }`. A rejected password (`UiaaInfo.auth_error` set in the UIA answer) gives `ActionFailed { CrossSigning, ReauthFailed }`; anything else a fixed `Internal` message. A flow list with no pure `m.login.password` flow gives `Unsupported` (fixed `Internal` message), instead of asking for a password that cannot help.
  - One identity operation at a time (`IdentityOp` permit): a second concurrent `BootstrapCrossSigning` or `ResetIdentity` gets `ActionFailed { Busy }`. Tested through `SecurityTasks::spawn_identity` with a blocking fake operation (second command while the first runs gets `Busy`; `CancelIdentityReset` while one runs sets the flag the operation sees and the action ends with `ActionFailed { Cancelled }`; a cancel with nothing running does not hit the next operation); the real `handle.cancel()` path stays on the human list. (Audit High, fixed in the follow-up: the permit was a `|_permit| async move` argument that the future never captured, so it was dropped at once and neither Busy nor cancel worked; `spawn_identity` now moves the permit into the spawned task and the work closure takes no permit.)
  - `Active.security: SecurityTasks` holds the forwarder `JoinHandle` and a `JoinSet` for the actions (so `handle` never awaits the network); `Active::stop_tasks` stops it first (before avatars, selection and the sync service), so logout still deletes the store safely. `Engine::start_security` runs after login (password and OAuth) and restore. Without a session the three commands answer `ActionFailed { NotLoggedIn }` (empty `RoomId`, as for the Task 2 stub); the other Phase 4 commands keep the stub.
- Finding 1 (SDK race, handled): `reset_identity()` makes the new private keys at once and uploads them only when `handle.reset(..)` runs. A `/keys/query` that returns the old identity in between makes the SDK drop the new private keys (`check_private_identity` / `clear_if_differs`, matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 identities/manager.rs:464, olm/signing/mod.rs:351), which left the device `Verified` but `cross_signing: MissingKeys` after a successful reset (right after login the first sync queries the own user). Fix: `reset_task` first runs `request_user_identity(me)` so nothing is pending, and `finish` runs it again after every reset, success or failure (after a failed reset it also drops the new keys the server never accepted; this holds for reset only, not for a first bootstrap, whose local identity a keys query does not drop: see the review follow-up). Residual: an interrupted reset (ReauthRequired, wrong password) shows a short `Complete` before the next keys query corrects it; and the SDK deletes the backup and turns 4S off before the UIA step, so a `ResetIdentity { password: None }` is not free (the app should ask for the password before sending it, Task 10).
- Finding 2 (plan wording): there is no success event for `BootstrapCrossSigning` / `ResetIdentity` (the Q7 API has none); success is the `Security` change. The "bootstrap is a no-op" test therefore watches 5 s for `ReauthRequired` / `ActionFailed` and checks that the server's master key is unchanged.
- Synapse v1.151.0 first-upload rule (read through `docker compose exec synapse` from `/usr/local/lib/python3.13/site-packages/synapse/rest/client/keys.py`, `SigningKeyUploadServlet.on_POST`): lines 510-515 resending the same keys answers 200 without UIA; lines 517-521 "The keys are different; is x-signing set up? If no, then this is first-time setup, and that is allowed without UIA, per MSC3967"; only when cross-signing is already set up is UIA required (lines 521-603: `m.oauth` flows under MSC3861/MAS, else `validate_user_via_ui_auth` with `can_skip_ui_auth=False`). So a restored pre-Phase-4 session with no identity bootstraps with `None` auth, as Q4 assumes, and no Q13 password prompt is needed for it; a replacement always needs UIA. Fixed accounts can't produce a never-had-an-identity account, so that first-time path is not exercised by a test here; CI's fresh volume runs the first-time bootstrap with the login password on every run (the SDK passes it from `login_username`, matrix-sdk-0.19.1 src/authentication/matrix/mod.rs:798-813).
- Test helpers (`crates/core/tests/common/mod.rs`): `expect_security`, `reset_identity_on(core, n)` (ResetIdentity with the account password, waits for `Security { Complete, Verified, recovery Disabled }`), `first_device(core, n)` (login + `reset_identity_on`). The `EncryptedPair` fixture and its helpers (`hex`, `outgoing`, `login_and_run`, `pump`, `message_with`) moved from `e2ee_rooms.rs` to `common` unchanged (the Task 3 review suggestion), so the shield test here reuses it.
- Integration tests (`crates/core/tests/e2ee_identity.rs`, verbatim ignore reason, `e2ee_serial` first, alice reset to B0): `status_on_a_fresh_baseline_account` (`MissingKeys`, `Unverified`, `identity_exists`, `Disabled`); `reset_needs_the_password_and_replaces_the_identity` (None gives `ReauthRequired { ResetIdentity }`, a wrong password `ReauthFailed`, the right one a new master key on the server, `Complete`/`Verified`/`Disabled`, and a backup on the server; polled, because the status can turn complete before the upload ends); `bootstrap_is_a_no_op_when_an_identity_exists`; `a_restored_session_keeps_the_complete_identity` (`first_device`, `Shutdown`, `RestoreSession`: `Complete` and `Verified`); `messages_after_a_reset_carry_no_shield` (in an encrypted room with carol; carol learns alice's new keys through sync, so alice sends up to 6 messages 3 s apart until one arrives with `shield == None`; failing means the shield never went away).
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (core lib 255 tests incl. 5 new in `security` and 1 new engine test; the existing stub test now skips the three commands that have handlers), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. Ignored run (`create-test-users.sh`, then `--ignored --skip keyring`): all suites ok in 3m22 including build; `e2ee_identity` ignored run 44-47 s, run in a row three times (5 passed each time, no `create-test-users.sh` between); `e2ee_rooms` (6 passed) also re-run after the fixture move, 52 s. `docker compose stop` done.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/` (matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `src/` unless noted): `Encryption::verification_state` encryption/mod.rs:1184 (`VerificationState` :315, updated by `update_verification_state` :2222 and after a keys query with an own device :2209); `Subscriber::get` eyeball-0.8.8 src/subscriber.rs:72 (`Stream` impl :197); `Recovery::state` encryption/recovery/mod.rs:144, `state_stream` :176, `RecoveryState` recovery/types.rs:87, `Recovery::reset_identity` mod.rs:438 (returns `RecoveryError`, types.rs:32), `IdentityResetHandle` :777 with `auth_type` :785, `reset` :791, `cancel` :802; `Encryption::cross_signing_status` encryption/mod.rs:1121 and `CrossSigningStatus::is_complete` matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 src/olm/signing/mod.rs:117 (re-exported, encryption/mod.rs:124); `has_devices_to_verify_against` :1132; `get_user_identity` :1349; `request_user_identity` :1397; `devices_stream` :1438; `user_identities_stream` :1476; `bootstrap_cross_signing` :1527; `bootstrap_cross_signing_if_needed` :1696; `CrossSigningResetHandle::auth_type` :363, `auth` :370, `cancel` :425; `CrossSigningResetAuthType` :433; `OAuthCrossSigningResetInfo` (public `approval_url`, `session`) :458; `Error::as_uiaa_response` error.rs:443; ruma-client-api-0.25.0: `uiaa::UiaaInfo` (`flows`, `session`, `auth_error`) src/uiaa.rs:80, `AuthFlow` :175, `Password::new` src/uiaa/auth_data.rs:219 (public `session`), `UserIdentifier::Matrix` :438, `MatrixUserIdentifier` :563.
- Audit follow-up (Medium, fixed): the password retry in `bootstrap_task` first calls `request_user_identity(me)` and goes on only when the answer is a confirmed `None` (`bootstrap_retry_allowed`: an existing identity, or a failed query, stops with a fixed `Internal` message, so the app can offer Confirm-identity or Reset); API confirmed at matrix-sdk-0.19.1 encryption/mod.rs:1397. Unit-tested through the pure helper only: an integration test would need an account whose identity the client has not seen yet, which the fixed accounts cannot give (the first `bootstrap_cross_signing_if_needed` already queries keys). (Low, fixed): a rejected approval URL is `Internal` with a fixed message (`approval_url`), no longer `Login`. (Low, recorded for Task 10): the UIA password is copied into ruma's non-zeroized `uiaa::Password`; a failed or password-less reset has already deleted the backup.
- Known failing ignored test: `e2ee_identity::a_restored_session_keeps_the_complete_identity` currently times out in `first_device` (waiting for `Security { Complete, Verified, Disabled }` after the reset; 3 of 3 runs, also on eb9667c before the audit fixes). It passed earlier in this task. FIXED (phase4.4 follow-up): the failure was intermittent (about 1 run in 6 alone, not a state build-up; the log showed Complete then MissingKeys again). Cause: the status forwarder's `read_status` calls `has_devices_to_verify_against`, which queries the server for the own keys; such a query answered with the old identity while a reset had made new private keys but not yet uploaded the public ones made the SDK drop the new private keys. Fix: `IdentityOp::keys_gate` in `crates/core/src/security.rs`, held by the forwarder while it reads and by bootstrap/reset for the whole operation, so no own-keys query of ours runs in that window. Runs: 25/25 single-test passes, 3/3 full `e2ee_identity` ignored runs, `e2ee_rooms` 6/6, workspace gate green. Residual risk: a sync-driven keys query in that window is not covered by the gate. Closed by the phase4.5 follow-up (sync paused, see "Reset race fix"); the `reset_end` upload check there also now needs a complete private identity. The other four `e2ee_identity` tests and `e2ee_rooms` pass.
- Review follow-up (Critical, M2, L1; phase4.4): (Critical) `OlmMachine::bootstrap_cross_signing` saves the new public identity and the private keys locally before the upload (matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 `machine/mod.rs:808-835`); a keys query that finds no own identity changes nothing locally (`identities/manager.rs:464`, `olm/signing/mod.rs:351`), so `get_user_identity` / `request_user_identity` keep returning it (matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `encryption/mod.rs:1349`, `:1397`), and `bootstrap_cross_signing_if_needed` sees it and returns Ok without uploading (`:1696-1708`). So after a first upload refused with UIA the old code (a) never retried the upload, (b) showed `Complete` for an identity the server lacks, (c) always refused its own retry guard, (d) skipped `finish`. Fix, stateless on purpose (it also survives a restart, an in-memory "pending" flag would not): the truth about the server comes from a raw `get_keys::v3::Request` for the own user sent with `client.send` (ruma-client-api-0.25.0 `keys/get_keys.rs:34-67`, `master_keys`; the answer is not fed to the Olm machine, so it changes nothing locally), `server_master_key` / `server_has_identity`. `bootstrap_plan(server, local)`: server has one -> `AlreadySetUp` (only syncs it in, never uploads); none/none -> `FirstAttempt` (`bootstrap_cross_signing_if_needed(None)`); none/local identity -> `UploadLocal` (the keys of the failed attempt are uploaded directly with `bootstrap_cross_signing(None)`: `reset` is false there, `machine/mod.rs:834-835` reuses the local keys); server unreachable -> refuse, fixed `Internal` message. On UIA with a password the retry guard asks the server again (`bootstrap_retry_allowed`, now fed by the raw query, so it no longer refuses the legitimate retry and still refuses when an identity appeared), then `bootstrap_cross_signing(Some(auth))`. `bootstrap` now runs `finish` also after an error. Status: `identity_known(local, queried, server)` (pure): with a local identity only the server's answer counts, so a pending upload reads `NotSetUp` (server none) or `Unknown` (server unreachable), never `Complete`; the raw query runs only until the server confirmed an identity once (`IdentityOp::server_identity_seen`, an identity never disappears). The sequence is tested through a `BootstrapApi` seam with a scripted fake (failed first attempt -> ReauthRequired and local identity left; next request with the password goes straight to the upload; server identity seen late -> refused, nothing uploaded; unreachable server -> nothing uploaded) plus tables for `bootstrap_plan` and `identity_known`; the mutation "none/local -> FirstAttempt" fails two of them. No integration test of the first-upload UIA path (the local Synapse needs no UIA there, see above). (M2) `ResetIdentity { password: None }` on a password/UIA session now answers `ReauthRequired { ResetIdentity }` before `reset_identity()` is called (`reset_needs_password_first`, session kind from `Client::auth_api`, matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `client/mod.rs:876`; OAuth sessions are exempt), because `reset_identity` deletes the backup and disables 4S first (`encryption/recovery/mod.rs:438-443`). The integration test now starts from B1 (a backup exists), and after the None attempt asserts the backup and master key are unchanged (seen red with the guard off: "a password-less reset deleted the backup"). Residual for Task 10 and the checkpoint: a WRONG password still destroys the backup and 4S (same SDK order), then the reset fails with `ReauthFailed`. (L1) The OAuth cancel races are fixed: a cancel seen after the handle is stored and before `OpenAccountManagement` returns `Cancelled` without sending the URL; after `handle.reset(None)`, `reset_end(ok, cancelled, uploaded)` reports success when the server holds the new master key even if a cancel raced. Recorded: after `cancel`, the SDK's `CrossSigningResetHandle::auth` returns `Ok(())` (`encryption/mod.rs:386-389`) and `IdentityResetHandle::reset` then still runs `enable_backup()` (`recovery/mod.rs:791-799`), so `Ok` alone cannot tell a cancel from an approval; the core compares the server's master key with the local one (only when a cancel was seen). Not testable offline (the real path is on the human list). (Warnings) `keys_gate` is held for the whole OAuth approval wait (up to 2 minutes), so `Security` events stall then; the app must not read a missing `Security` event as "unchanged". The engine unit test for background identity commands now expects `NotLoggedIn` (its client never logged in, and bootstrap checks the user ID first). Checks: fmt, clippy `--workspace --all-targets --all-features -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (core lib 268 tests), `check-app-deps.sh`, `check-core-api.sh` pass; `e2ee_identity` ignored run 3 times in a row (5 passed each, 46 s, 43 s, 43 s), `e2ee_rooms` 6 passed (40 s); `docker compose stop` done.
- Open issues: (1) the `ruma` UIA `Password` holds a plain `String` copy of the password while the call runs (our `Password` is zeroized, the SDK's copy is not). (2) Interrupted reset leaves the SDK's deleted backup and disabled 4S behind (see Finding 1); the app must collect the password first. (3) OAuth approval and cancel are covered by the pure `reset_outcome` test only; the real path needs a matrix.org-style account (human checkpoint). (4) `has_devices_to_verify_against` counts the own, just-signed device after a reset, so it is `true` on a first device; Task 10 should not treat it as "another device exists" without checking.

## Phase 4 / Task 5 notes
- TDD: the unit tests (`recovery_errors_map_to_fixed_messages`, `enable_progress_never_forwards_the_key`, `enabling_and_changing_the_key_are_guarded_by_the_recovery_state`) were written first against `todo!()` stubs and failed (3 failed), then the code. The integration test `a_returning_user_gets_the_backup_message_and_recovers_with_the_key` was run red with `crates/core/src` stashed (it got `Internal("not implemented yet")`), then green.
- `crates/core/src/security.rs` (private; no key, passphrase or message body is logged or printed):
  - `EnableRecovery`: `enable().wait_for_backups_to_upload()` in the security `JoinSet` under the identity permit. `subscribe_to_progress` is forwarded through the pure `map_progress` as `RecoveryProgress`; `EnableProgress::Done { recovery_key }` maps to `None` and no `EnableProgress` is ever formatted. The returned key becomes `RecoveryKeyReady(RecoveryKey)` once, sent after the permit is freed (so a front end that reacts to it can start the next operation). Status `Enabled` can arrive before or after `RecoveryKeyReady`; the tests accept either order.
  - `Recover { key }`: `recover(key.expose())` as typed (the app trims), under the permit and the `keys_gate` for the whole call (it imports the private keys and signs the device), then `Recovered`. `ChangeRecoveryKey`: `reset_key()`, then `RecoveryKeyReady`. All three call `finish` (own-identity query and a status refresh), also after a failure.
  - Errors have their own `ActionKind::Recovery` (`spawn_identity` now takes the action kind; `report` no longer hard-codes `CrossSigning`, so a `Busy` for a recovery action says `Recovery`). `spawn_identity` work returns `Result<Option<CoreEvent>, Stop>`: the success event goes out after the permit drops.
  - `map_recovery_error` (pure, unit-tested; the SDK's texts can quote input, so none is forwarded): `BackupExistsOnServer` gives "a key backup already exists; enter your recovery key"; any `SecretStorageError::SecretStorageKey(_)` (does not decode, parity, MAC) gives "that recovery key or passphrase is not correct"; `MissingKeyInfo` gives "recovery is not set up on this account"; anything else keeps the caller's fixed message.
  - Destructive-call guards, not in the plan, from the Task 4 lessons (`enable()` over an existing secret store, and `reset_key()` on a device lacking secrets, would replace the key with one that lacks them; both are local-state checks, pure and unit-tested): `enable_precheck` lets only `Disabled` through (`Incomplete` gives the "backup already exists, enter your recovery key" message without calling the SDK, `Enabled` "recovery is already set up; change the key instead", `Unknown` "the recovery state is not known yet"); `change_key_precheck` lets only `Enabled` through. The SDK's own server check (`BackupExistsOnServer`) stays the authority for the `Disabled` case and is tested for real (see below).
  - `engine.rs`: the three commands dispatch to `SecurityTasks`; without a session they answer `ActionFailed { Recovery, NotLoggedIn }`. The Task 4 stub test now skips them; a new engine test checks they run in the background and answer `ActionFailed { Recovery, Internal }` with an empty `RoomId` and no secret.
- Integration tests, `crates/core/tests/e2ee_recovery.rs` (verbatim ignore reason; `e2ee_serial` first), 7 tests: set up then restore on a fresh store (roadmap acceptance); returning user + `BackupExistsOnServer` on purpose (`seed_recovery(1, None)`, a B1 core is `Unverified`/`Incomplete`, `EnableRecovery` gives the fixed message, `Recover { K0 }` gives `Recovered` and `Verified`/`Enabled`/`Complete`); a backup the device is not connected to (recovery `Disabled`, a raw device made a backup with no 4S: the real SDK `BackupExistsOnServer` path, the backup version on the server is unchanged); wrong key (one changed character, and a non-key) gives the fixed message with `recovery` staying `Incomplete`, then a passphrase from `seed_recovery(1, Some(..))` recovers; change key (new key differs; a fresh store recovers with the new one, another fails with the old one); change key refused on an `Incomplete` device (server backup unchanged, the old key still works); reset clears recovery (`Disabled`, a new backup version, 4S off on the server). The spaced key from `enable` is accepted as is (first test and the returning-user test).
  - Observed UTD reason before recovery on a fresh store: `HistoricalMessageAndDeviceIsUnverified` (3 of 3 messages, every run). After `Recover`, the same item IDs become `Message`s with the original bodies and `shield == Grey(AuthenticityNotGuaranteed)` within the 60 s window (about 2-5 s in practice).
  - `tests/common/mod.rs`: `pump` now calls `pump_with` (explicit timeout and a callback for the other events, so a test can follow `Security` and `Recovered` while it keeps the timeline up to date) and `try_pump_with` (returns false on a timeout). A fresh store shows only the newest event first, so the restore test sends `PaginateBackwards` until all three placeholders show (it failed 2 of 3 full runs before that, passing alone).
- Finding (Task 4 code, NOT fixed, rule 8; needs the human): the SDK's own sync-driven own-user `/keys/query` can still drop the new private keys of a reset, because the keys gate cannot cover the SDK's sync requests. The trace (`matrix_sdk_crypto=debug`) shows: "Creating new cross signing identity", the first `device_signing/upload` answered 401, a sync-driven `/keys/query` for the own user created right after, answered with the OLD identity, "Removed some or all of our private ..." (identities/manager.rs:464), then the password retry uploads fine. Result: device `Verified` on the server but `cross_signing: MissingKeys` locally. Two cases seen: (1) a reset in the first seconds after login (`first_device`, so `e2ee_identity::a_restored_session_keeps_the_complete_identity`): failed about half the runs on the Task 4 commit itself (3 of 6 with `crates/core/src` stashed to HEAD), so this was not caused by Task 5; (2) a reset on an account whose recovery was just enabled: 3 of 3 runs, also with a 3 s wait. Handling here: `first_device` now waits for `SyncState::Running` and 3 s more before it resets (8 of 8 passes where it was 4 of 8 failing; a fixed wait, not a fix), and the Task 5 reset test asserts only what the plan asks (recovery `Disabled`, a new backup) and not `Complete`. A product fix would pause sync during a reset or redo a reset that ends `MissingKeys`; both touch Task 4 and the engine, so it is left to the human. **RESOLVED (phase4.5 follow-up, see "Reset race fix" below):** the sync is paused during bootstrap/reset; the fixed 3 s wait is gone and the reset test asserts `Complete`.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (core lib 275 tests incl. 6 new here), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. Ignored run (`create-test-users.sh`, then `--ignored --skip keyring`): every suite ok in 4m30 including build; `e2ee_recovery` 78-84 s for its 7 tests (per-test reset cost about 3-6 s). Repeat runs of `e2ee_recovery` without `create-test-users.sh` between: 7 passed in each of 7 runs after the pagination fix (including right after the full ignored run); `e2ee_identity` repeat 5 passed.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/` (matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `src/encryption/` unless noted): `Recovery::state` recovery/mod.rs:144, `enable` :235, `reset_key` :359, `reset_identity` :438, `recover` :489, `enable_backup` :261; `Enable::subscribe_to_progress` recovery/futures.rs:53 (the current value comes first, `ChannelObservable::subscribe` utils/mod.rs:79), `wait_for_backups_to_upload` :66, `IntoFuture for Enable` :82 (fails with `BackupExistsOnServer` only when backups are not enabled locally and one exists, :90-97), `IntoFuture for Reset` :181; `RecoveryError` recovery/types.rs:32 (`BackupExistsOnServer` :38), `EnableProgress` :51 (`ZeroizeOnDrop`, `Done { recovery_key }`), `RecoveryState` :87; `SecretStorageError` secret_storage/mod.rs:124 (`SecretStorageKey(DecodeError)` :136, `MissingKeyInfo` :144), `open_secret_store` :263 (a wrong key or passphrase fails in `SecretStorageKey::from_account_data`, :281); matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 `secret_storage.rs` `DecodeError` :69 (`Mac` :87), `store/types.rs` `RoomKeyCounts` :410 (`total`, `backed_up`); ruma-client-api-0.25.0 `backup/get_latest_backup_info.rs` `Request` :38 (test helper only); `wait_for_e2ee_initialization_tasks` mod.rs:2176 (test helper only).
- Open issues: (1) the SDK copies the typed key into plain `String`s while `recover` runs (our `RecoveryKey` is zeroized, the SDK's copy is not), and `enable`/`reset_key` return the new key as a plain `String` that `RecoveryKey::from` takes over without a copy. (2) The reset race above (fixed, see "Reset race fix"). (3) `RecoveryProgress::BackingUp` counts come from the SDK per upload step and can repeat; the app should show the last one. Human items: added to the checkpoint list (restore with the Element-made key and passphrase is already there; the new ones are below).
- Reset race fix (phase4.5 follow-up). Cause: the sync service sends the SDK's `/keys/query` next to every sync request (matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `sliding_sync/mod.rs:633`, `send_outgoing_requests` in a task per request; `encryption/mod.rs:857-889`, `send_outgoing_request` -> `keys_query` :566). `OlmMachine::bootstrap_cross_signing(reset)` saves the new private keys at once (matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 `machine/mod.rs:808-835`) and the public ones reach the server later (`encryption/mod.rs:1593-1640`; for OAuth only after approval, up to 2 minutes). A query that was answered with the old identity and is processed after that goes through `check_private_identity` / `clear_if_differs` (`identities/manager.rs:462-470`, `olm/signing/mod.rs:351`) and drops the new keys for good. The own-user query is dirty after every sync that reports our own device-list change (login, key and signature uploads, enabling recovery), which is why it hit right after login and right after enabling recovery. The `keys_gate` cannot cover the SDK's own requests. Fix: `SyncPause` (`crates/core/src/sync.rs`) stops the `SyncService` for the whole `bootstrap` and `reset` (also the OAuth wait) and starts it again afterwards. `SyncService::stop` returns only after the supervisor has ended both sync tasks (matrix-sdk-ui-0.19.1 `sync_service.rs:657-668`, `shutdown` :318-331, supervisor loop :246-330), so no query is in flight when the new keys are made. The supervisor's `restart` takes the pause gate (`ServiceControl` in `engine.rs`), so a sync failure during the pause cannot bring the sync back early; `Active::stop_tasks` aborts the security tasks first, which drops the gate (no deadlock on logout). Before the pause the SDK's one-shot setup task is awaited (`wait_for_e2ee_initialization_tasks`, `encryption/mod.rs:2176`; with `auto_enable_cross_signing` it bootstraps and queries keys itself). The own-identity query of `finish` now runs while still paused, so a failed or cancelled reset drops its never-uploaded keys at a safe time. After a reset that reported success the core checks `cross_signing_status().is_complete()` and else answers `ActionFailed` with a fixed message (`reset_complete`; this also catches another device changing the identity meanwhile). `reset_end`'s upload check (cancelled OAuth reset) now needs complete private keys as well as server master == local master, so a sync-overwritten public identity cannot make a cancelled reset look like a success. `keys_gate` and the identity permit are unchanged. Rejected: exporting and re-importing the private keys (the clear can happen before the export, inside `reset_identity`) and redoing the reset (new UIA or OAuth approval, and it could replace an identity another device changed). Side effects: the app sees `SyncState::Idle` then `Running` during a reset; no events arrive during it. Tests: `first_device` no longer waits (no sleep, no wait for `Running`); new `e2ee_identity::a_reset_right_after_login_keeps_the_new_private_keys` (two resets in a row, then 5 s watching that no `Security` shows an incomplete identity); the Task 5 reset test (reset right after enabling recovery) now asserts `cross_signing: Complete` and the same 5 s watch; a unit test for `reset_complete`. Runs: `e2ee_identity` ignored run 5 times in a row (6 passed each, 57-62 s), `e2ee_recovery` 5 times (7 passed each, 76-77 s), `e2ee_rooms` once (6 passed, 37 s). Full ignored run (`--ignored --skip keyring`) all ok once, workspace gate and both scripts pass.
- Review fixes (phase4.5, second follow-up). (1) The restore test now records the timeline diffs after `Recover` (`pump_recording` in `tests/common/mod.rs`) and asserts at least one `Set` to a `Message` per item ID and no `Remove`/`Insert`/`Reset`/`Pop*`/`Truncate`/`Clear` touching them; the SDK does emit `Set` (test passes, so the criterion is not weakened). (2) `SyncPause` is generic over a small `PauseTarget` trait (production: `SdkTarget` = `SyncService` + `Client`; tests: a fake). `Paused` restores on drop too: a panic or cancellation of the identity work spawns the restore (start the sync, switch the queue on, then open the gate), unless `SyncPause::begin_ending` was called (`Active::stop_tasks` calls it before `security.stop()`, so an abort at logout does not restart anything). (3) The pause records whether the service ran (`SyncService::state().get()` in Running or Offline, the states `stop` acts on) and starts it again only then. (4) The pause disables the send queue and restores its previous state (a queue that was already off stays off; the supervisor's own re-enable on resume, `forward_sync_state`, is gated behind the same lock and not fought). (5) `pause()` runs `set_enabled(false)` + `stop()` in its own spawned task and awaits the handle, so aborting the caller cannot cut `SyncService::stop` short. Unit tests (`sync::tests`): stops and restores both, not-running stays stopped, a disabled queue stays disabled, restart after a panic (and the gate opens), no restart when ending, stop survives an abort of the caller. Seen failing first (4 of 6) on the old code. APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`: `SendQueue::set_enabled` matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `src/send_queue/mod.rs:286` (sets the global flag, wakes rooms, `:286-299`), `SendQueue::is_enabled` :303, `Client::send_queue` :389; the send path's key query `room/futures.rs:533` -> `room/mod.rs:2557-2581`; `SyncService::state` matrix-sdk-ui-0.19.1 `src/sync_service.rs:617`, `start` :629, `stop` :657; `Subscriber::get` eyeball-0.8.8 `src/subscriber.rs:72`. Residual: the disabled queue stops the SDK's room send path, but a send already inside `query_keys_for_untracked_or_dirty_users` when the pause starts, or any other direct SDK call that queries keys (a manual `request_user_identity`, verification), is not covered; not testable without a network. Runs: workspace gate and both boundary scripts pass; `e2ee_recovery` ignored run 3 times in a row (7 passed each, 83-94 s), `e2ee_identity` 3 times (6 passed each, 58 s), `e2ee_rooms` once (6 passed, 52 s). Not fixed, recorded: the enable/change-key prechecks read the cached `recovery.state()`, which can be stale (consider a server re-check later); `ChangeRecoveryKey` does not re-upload other clients' secrets under the old default key (UI wording for Task 10).

## Phase 4 / Task 6 notes
- TDD: unit tests first against `todo!()` stubs (red), then the code: `map_cancel_code`, `map_request_state`, `map_sas_state`, `Slot` (one flow at a time, `Claim::{Taken, Same, Busy}`), `retry_lookup` (red by mutation), `map_user_trust`; engine test for the verification commands. Integration tests `crates/core/tests/e2ee_verification.rs` (5, verbatim ignore reason, `e2ee_serial`): self-verification with history decryption, DM user verification (both users `Verified`), mismatch, busy (outgoing `Busy`), second incoming request from another user cancelled.
- `crates/core/src/verification.rs` (private; no emoji, key or device name is logged): handlers for to-device and in-room requests (kept as drop guards in `SecurityTasks.verification`, ended in `Active::stop_tasks`); one driver task per flow follows `VerificationRequest::changes()` then `SasVerification::changes()` (subscribe first, then read the state) into `VerificationRequested` (also for outgoing requests) and `Verification` events; the slot is freed before the final event. The requester starts SAS on `Ready`; the other side accepts the SAS start. `CancelVerification` cancels the SAS when there is one, else the request. `StartSas` and `ConfirmQrScanned` still answer "not implemented yet" (Task 7).
- `WatchUserTrust` (Task 8 scope, pulled into Task 6 because the DM test needs it; accepted by the human): minimal version in `security.rs` (`watch_user_trust`, pure `map_user_trust`), one watched user at a time, fed by `user_identities_stream`, no DM auto-watch. **Task 8 reuses or refines it.**
- SDK behaviours found (evidence in the test runs):
  - The crypto machine cancels BOTH requests when a second one arrives from the same user (matrix-sdk-crypto `verification/machine.rs:164`); the core's busy check therefore matters across different users only.
  - An in-room request handler can run before the machine has registered the request; the core retries the lookup (30 x 100 ms).
  - A request from a device the machine does not know yet is dropped by the SDK ("Could not retrieve the device data for the incoming verification request, ignoring it"). Two accounts that share only a brand-new DM hit this on first contact (this was the cause of the first-run-after-prune failures: after `create-test-users.sh` they share no other room, so nothing had queried the peer's keys). The tests do a `WatchUserTrust` (a keys query) first, as the app will on opening a DM (Q10, Task 8). Real first-contact requests from unknown devices can still be lost: open issue.
  - An instant automatic cancel can overtake the requester's own send response (which registers the flow ID), so the requester never sees it; the busy cancel waits 1 s.
  - The signature upload after a user verification is sent only with the next sliding-sync request (`sliding_sync/mod.rs:633`), up to a 30 s long poll later, so the peer's `UserTrust::Verified` can lag by up to 30 s (the test waits 75 s).
- Test lessons: waiting on a core's events with `expect_event` drops its timeline diffs and status events; the self-verification test follows core 2 through `pump_with`/`pump_recording`. It also waits until the room key is in the server backup before the second device logs in.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/` (matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `src/encryption/` unless noted): `VerificationRequest` verification/requests.rs (`accept` :148, `start_sas` :199, `cancel` :207, `changes` :250, `state` :260, `we_started`, `is_self_verification`), `VerificationRequestState` :35, `SasVerification` verification/sas.rs (`accept` :32, `confirm` :81, `mismatch` :97, `cancel` :106, `changes` :320, `state` :328, `we_started`), `SasState` and `EmojiShortAuthString` matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 `verification/sas/mod.rs:99/:118`, `CancelInfo` `verification/mod.rs:317`, `Encryption::get_verification_request` encryption/mod.rs:1207, `request_user_identity` :1397, `UserIdentity::request_verification` identities/users.rs:187, `Device::request_verification` identities/devices.rs, `Client::add_event_handler` client/mod.rs:1073 and `event_handler_drop_guard` :1324, `CancelCode` ruma-events-0.35.0 `key/verification/cancel.rs:72`, `ToDeviceKeyVerificationRequestEventContent` `key/verification/request.rs`. eyeball `subscribe()` does not yield the current value (so state is read after subscribing).
- Open issues: first-contact requests from unknown devices are dropped by the SDK (above); signature upload latency up to 30 s; the verification lookup retry and the 1 s busy-cancel delay are workarounds for SDK timing; `request_user_identity` for a request can run during an identity reset (not covered by `keys_gate`); flows are not resumed after a restart.

## Phase 4 / Task 7 notes
- Dependency (rule 3, the one allowed change): `crates/core/Cargo.toml` matrix-sdk keeps `=0.19.1` and gains only the `qrcode` feature. `Cargo.lock` gains exactly two packages, `matrix-sdk-qrcode 0.19.1` and `qrcode 0.14.1` (plus the dependency lines naming them under matrix-sdk-base/matrix-sdk-crypto); no existing version changed (checked with `git diff Cargo.lock`). `cargo tree -p twicecat-core -e features -i matrix-sdk` lists `qrcode`; `cargo deny check` passes with `deny.toml` unchanged. `crates/app/Cargo.toml` unchanged.
- `verification.rs`: once a request is `Ready` (incoming accepted, outgoing ready), `generate_qr_code()` runs once. `Some` gives `ShowQr(QrImage)` and the driver follows `QrVerification::changes()`; `None` (or an error, reported as `ActionFailed`) keeps Task 6 (outgoing: `start_sas()`; incoming: stays `Ready`). When SAS takes over (`StartSas`, or the peer starts it) the QR stream is dropped and the SAS flow is followed. `StartSas` calls `start_sas()` only while a QR flow is open, else `NotAllowed`. `ConfirmQrScanned` calls `QrVerification::confirm()` only in `QrVerificationState::Scanned`, else `NotAllowed`. `CancelVerification` cancels the QR flow when there is one. The `act` helper now returns `CoreError` (not only a fixed text). `engine.rs`: `StartSas` and `ConfirmQrScanned` are wired; only the device commands are stubs now.
- Pure helpers (table-tested): `map_qr_state`, `qr_image(size, modules)` (rejects a non-square count, 0 and a size over `u16`), `qr_image_of` (width plus `to_colors()` through `Color::select(true, false)`, so the core never names a qrcode type and adds no dependency). Unit test with spec bytes (`MATRIX`, 0x02, mode 0x02, flow ID, two 32-byte keys, secret) through `QrVerificationData::from_bytes` and `to_qr_code` gives a 45 x 45 image with the three 7 x 7 finder patterns.
- Integration tests (`e2ee_verification.rs`, alice only, a raw client `twicecat-test-qr` advertising `QrCodeScanV1`, syncing in a background loop of `sync_once` because `Client::sync` in `tokio::spawn` hits compiler error E0275): `a_qr_code_is_shown_to_a_peer_that_can_scan` (size 45; the peer sees `QrCodeShowV1`, not `QrCodeScanV1`), `compare_emoji_instead_of_the_qr_code_verifies_the_peer` (identical emoji, both confirm, core `Done`, the peer's device verified), `cancelling_while_the_qr_code_shows_cancels_the_peer_request` (peer sees `m.user`), `qr_confirmation_and_sas_need_the_right_phase` (`ConfirmQrScanned` with no scan gives `NotAllowed`). The Task 6 tests now fail if a `ShowQr` ever reaches them (Twicecat to Twicecat stays SAS). As in Task 6, each side knows the other's keys first (`WatchUserTrust` on the core after the peer's first sync; the peer queries the server).
- TDD: the four integration tests were run first against the Task 6 code (stashed `verification.rs`/`engine.rs`) and all failed (timeouts); then the code made them pass. The unit tests were written together with the code (not seen red first).
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (core lib 291, app lib 434), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored `e2ee_verification` after `create-test-users.sh`: 9 passed in 91 s, repeat run: 9 passed in 94 s. Full ignored run (`-p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --ignored --skip keyring`): all pass, 6 min 22 s including build; `docker compose stop` afterwards.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`: matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `encryption/verification/qrcode.rs` (`QrVerification::to_qr_code` :85, `confirm` :99, `cancel` :108, `changes` :210, `state` :218), `verification/mod.rs` (`Verification::qr` :71, re-exports of `QrVerification`, `QrVerificationState`, `QrVerificationData` :42-48), `verification/requests.rs` (`generate_qr_code` :172, `start_sas` :199, `their_supported_methods` :133, `VerificationRequestState::Ready` fields), `identities/devices.rs` (`request_verification_with_methods` :191, `is_verified` :421), `encryption/mod.rs` (`get_own_device` :1269, `get_user_devices` :1301, `request_user_identity` :1397), `client/mod.rs` (`sync_once` :3182); matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 `verification/qrcode.rs` (`QrVerificationState` :97-130), `verification/mod.rs` (`CancelInfo::cancel_code` :330); matrix-sdk-qrcode-0.19.1 `types.rs` (`from_bytes` :134, `to_qr_code` :163), `utils.rs` (one byte segment, version 7, EC level L); qrcode-0.14.1 (`QrCode::width` lib.rs:173, `to_colors` :218, `Color::select` types.rs:73).

## Phase 4 / Task 8 notes
- `crates/core/src/security.rs` (private): pure `merge_devices(server, crypto_trust, current)` (current first, then `last_seen_ms` descending, never-seen last, ties by device ID; a device the store has no keys for is `NoKeys`; an empty or blank name is `None`; the last-seen IP is never read), `clean_device_name` (trim, cap at 64 characters, empty is `NotAllowed`), `sign_out_precheck` (the current device or an empty list is `NotAllowed`), `account_management_target(metadata, Option<&DeviceId>)` (`DeviceDelete` URL for one device; with several selected, `None` gives the `DevicesList` page, a small extension of the plan's signature), `dm_peer(targets, own)` and `dm_peer_of(room, own)` (exactly one other user; a non-user target gives no badge), and `delete_flow` over a small `DeleteApi` trait (the Q13 sequence: no auth first, UIA answer without a password gives `ReauthRequired { DeleteDevices }`, with one a single retry with the answer's session, a refused password gives `ReauthFailed`; no SDK text and no password in any error).
- `ListDevices` starts a watcher on the first call of a session (`devices_stream`, subscribed before the first read); it re-sends `Devices` when the list changed. Later calls answer once on their own. A successful rename or sign-out also sends the new `Devices` (the server-side changes do not appear in the crypto-store stream). `RenameDevice` and `SignOutDevices` run in the task set, so `handle` never waits on the network. On an OAuth session `SignOutDevices` emits `OpenAccountManagement` (URL checked by `check_auth_url`), or `ActionFailed` "manage your devices on your account page" when the server gives no usable URL.
- `WatchUserTrust` (the Task 6 version, refined, not duplicated): failures now go through one `spawn_failed` helper (still `ActionKind::Devices`, no new kind). Stale `UserTrust`: the old watcher is aborted but not awaited, so one event it had queued can arrive after the new watcher's first. Harmless and documented on the method: every `UserTrust` carries its `user_id`, and consumers match on it (Task 12's header must ignore a user who is not the selected DM's peer; this is already in its criteria). `SelectRoom` of a DM with one other direct target now watches that user (`engine.rs`, after the selection is stored); the watcher's `request_user_identity` is the keys query a later verification request needs.
- Removed: `security_stub` and `security_action` (no command is stubbed any more) and their "not implemented yet" test; added an engine test that renaming to a blank name is refused with no request.
- Unit tests (all in `security::device_tests`, written first, seen failing as compile errors, then the code): ordering, `NoKeys` and missing names, name trimming and cap (multibyte), sign-out refusal, account-page URL with a hand-built metadata JSON (with and without `account_management_uri`), `dm_peer`, and five `delete_flow` cases with a fake (success without UIA, asks for the password, retry carries password and session, refused password with no secret in the error, flow without a password stage). `map_user_trust` keeps its Task 6 table test.
- Integration tests (`tests/e2ee_devices.rs`, alice and carol, `e2ee_serial`, `reset_e2ee_account` first): list and rename (exactly 2 devices, own first and current; core 2 renames itself with spaces around the name, core 1's next list shows it; a blank name is refused), trust in the list (`Unverified` before and `Verified` after the Task 6 self-verification; the list is asked again every 2 s because the signature reaches core 1 through its sync), sign out another device (no password gives `ReauthRequired`, a wrong password gives `ReauthFailed` with the password absent from the event, the right one gives `SessionExpired` on core 2 within 30 s and a one-device list on core 1), current device refused (`NotAllowed`), and one extra test that `SelectRoom` of a DM reports the peer's `UserTrust` with no explicit `WatchUserTrust`. The integration tests were written after the code and not seen red first (the old handlers were stubs that answered "not implemented yet", which these tests cannot pass).
- Checks: fmt, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (core lib 303, app lib 434), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh` and `cargo deny check` pass. Ignored `e2ee_devices` right after `create-test-users.sh`: 5 passed, 37 s; repeat run without it: 5 passed, 36 s. Full ignored run (`cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`, after `create-test-users.sh`): all passed in 7 min 10 s, `e2ee_devices` 5/5 (40 s) among them.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`: matrix-sdk-0.19.1 `Client::devices` client/mod.rs:2972, `delete_devices` :3030, `rename_device` :3050, `Client::oauth` :938, `auth_api` :876, `OAuth::server_metadata` authentication/oauth/mod.rs:511, `Encryption::get_user_devices` encryption/mod.rs:1301, `devices_stream` :1438, `Device::is_verified` encryption/identities/devices.rs:421 (`Deref` to `DeviceData::device_id`, matrix-sdk-crypto-0.19.1 identities/device.rs:655), `UserDevices::devices` :577, `HttpError::as_uiaa_response` error.rs:135; matrix-sdk-base-0.19.1 `Room::direct_targets` room/mod.rs:335; ruma-events-0.35.0 `OwnedDirectUserIdentifier::as_user_id` direct.rs:32; ruma-client-api-0.25.0 `get_authorization_server_metadata::v1::{AuthorizationServerMetadata, AccountManagementActionData, DeviceDeleteData}` and `account_management_url_with_action` (discovery/get_authorization_server_metadata.rs:284, `DeviceDeleteData` :574), `device::Device` (device.rs:15, `last_seen_ip` is not read).
- Open issues: a device the store has not learned yet is `NoKeys` until the sync's keys query (the list is not asked to query keys, to stay clear of the Task 4 keys race; the stream re-sends then); the server-side name or deletion by another client does not re-send `Devices` by itself (the Sessions screen asks again when it opens); OAuth device sign-out for several devices opens the devices list page, not one URL per device; the OAuth path is covered by the pure URL test only (no OAuth homeserver), see the human list; device names are server text and must go through the sanitizer in the app (Task 13).

## Phase 4 / Task 11 notes (verification dialog in the app)
- Step done: new `crates/app/src/verification.rs` (pure `VerificationModel`, `Phase`, `qr_rgba`, `VerificationMessage` and the `App` handlers) and `crates/app/src/screens/verification.rs` (the modal; `layer` wraps both the Main and the Loading view as a second fixed stack layer, above the security screens). `state.rs` gains `Message::Verification`, `App.verification` and routes `VerificationRequested`, `Verification` and `ActionFailed { Verification }`. "Use another device" already sent `RequestSelfVerification` (Task 10); a request now clears `asked_other_device`, so the identity screen can ask again after the dialog.
- Ledger notes honoured:
  - the QR image is built on `ShowQr` and dropped on every phase except `ShowQr` and `QrScanned` (tests: every way out, handle id stable across unrelated updates and a `Security` event); the model keeps no copy of the grid, and its `Debug` shows the phase name and whether an image exists;
  - an incoming request with no QR code stays `Ready` and shows the waiting card (nothing waits for a `Waiting` phase);
  - an `ActionFailed { Verification }` while a flow runs is not shown and does not end the flow, it only re-enables the buttons (the core emits it for a QR code that cannot be drawn and then falls back to SAS, and ends a broken flow with `Cancelled`); with no flow it becomes a one-line notice and frees "Use another device";
  - peer and device names, emoji symbols and descriptions go through `text_safety` (`name_or_placeholder`, `one_line_capped`: bidi and control characters become U+FFFD, zero-width ones are dropped, 64 and 32 characters), and tests use an RLM-override and a newline;
  - routing by `user_id` matters for `UserTrust` (Task 12); the dialog is keyed by the flow ID and shows the user ID of a peer that is not this account (the event carries no display name).
- One command per press: a button that already sent in this phase is ignored until a phase arrives (or a failure); buttons only act in their phase (`Accept` only for an incoming request, `Compare emoji instead` only on `ShowQr`, `Yes` only on `QrScanned`, `They match` only on emoji or numbers); **Decline**, **Cancel** and **No** all send `CancelVerification`; **Close** acts only on a finished flow and sends nothing.
- A second `VerificationRequested` while a flow runs is ignored (the core has one slot); one that arrives while a finished (Done or Cancelled) card is open no longer replaces it (a click meant for Close could land on Accept): it is queued inside the model (`queued`), follows its own phase events, and shows after Close unless it already ended. Fix round (human-requested review): a user ID is never cut from the end; the title name is cut in the middle (`elide_middle`, 64 characters, start and end kept) and the card shows the full cleaned user ID on its own wrapping line for another user (`VerificationModel::user_id`, up to 255 characters). Tests: a 200-character server name keeps its end in the name, the full ID is kept, queue and drop-if-ended in `state.rs`.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_core-0.14.0 `image.rs` (`Handle::from_rgba :147`, `Handle::id :161`, `Handle::Rgba` Debug prints id and size only :190), iced_widget-0.14.2 `image.rs` (`Image::new :74`, `width :91`, `height :97`, `filter_method :124`, `FilterMethod::Nearest :236`, re-exported at :33). No spinner widget exists in iced 0.14, so waiting is a text line.
- Tests (unit, written first and seen failing to compile): model transitions table (incoming to Done, QR to Done, QR to emoji, Cancelled from every phase, stale ID, ended flow final), `qr_rgba` (size, white quiet zone, black module (0,0) over its block, black and white only, short grid), QR handle lifetime, redacted `Debug` (model and app), name and emoji sanitising, titles of the plan, every phase builds a card, and in `state.rs` one command per button including `StartSas` and `ConfirmQrScanned`, stale IDs, failure handling, logout. App lib: 497 tests.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked`. Not run: the ignored suites (no core code changed).
- Open issues: none beyond the human items above. Not rendered by the agent (rule 9).

The base crate has no notable-update reason for the encryption state (`RoomInfoNotableUpdateReasons`, matrix-sdk-base room_info.rs:1469-1505), so a room that turns encrypted may get its lock only with its next `Set` (new message, read receipt, name change). A user ID is not cut anywhere new (no peer text is drawn in the header; the DM title is the room name through `display_name`). The UTD, shield and badge texts are static.
