## Phase 4 / Task 13 notes (Sessions screen, re-authentication prompt, logout warning in the app)
- Step done:
  - `crates/app/src/sessions.rs`: pure `SessionsModel` (devices, rename field, sign-out confirmation, `pending` device IDs, `ReauthPrompt`, account-page `Approval`, notice, logout warning), `SessionsMessage`, pure helpers `logout_needs_warning`, `device_title`, `device_id_line`, `trust_label`, `can_verify`, `last_active_text` (uses `dates.rs` labels), `dedup_ids`, and the `App` handlers.
  - `crates/app/src/screens/sessions.rs`: the page (Refresh, Close, rows, "Sign out of all other sessions"), the password card and the logout-warning card, layered over the main view; `main_view` status bar gains **Sessions**.
  - `state.rs`: `Message::Sessions`, `App.sessions`; routes `Devices`, `ActionFailed { Devices | SignOutDevices }` (a `Devices` failure never ends a sign-out; `SignOutDevices` does, since d00f1fb), `ReauthRequired { DeleteDevices }` and `OpenAccountManagement` (to the security screens while a reset runs, otherwise to Sessions); `LogoutRequested` asks first when `logout_needs_warning`; `drop_secrets` clears the sessions password prompt; `back_to_login` resets the model.
- Ledger notes honoured:
  - Task 8 (no re-emit after renames and deletions by other clients): opening the screen and **Refresh** send `ListDevices`. An unseen device may list as `NoKeys` until the sync keys query; the badge says "No encryption keys" and offers no Verify (Refresh later).
  - Task 8 (OAuth): several devices open the DevicesList page; the app just opens the URL it is given (after the checks below).
  - Task 8 (names attacker-controlled): every name goes through `text_safety` (`name_or_placeholder`, 64 chars); a blank name shows the device ID; the ID is always shown too (elided in the middle, never cut from the end); the rename field starts with the sanitized name and caps input at 64 characters.
  - Task 8 (`uia_password` plain String): the app keeps the typed password only in the prompt's `Password` (zeroized); on Continue it moves into the command and the field is left empty; `pending` holds device IDs only; Cancel, close, logout and a finished sign-out drop the prompt.
  - Task 8 (no dedup or cap in `sign_out_devices`): the app dedups (`dedup_ids`), drops this device and unknown IDs, and keeps at most 500 devices from the list.
  - Task 4/5 (`ReauthRequired(DeleteDevices)` ignored): now handled only while a sign-out of the user's is pending; a wrong password (`ReauthFailed`) keeps the prompt, empties the field and shows "Wrong password"; any other failure ends the sign-out with a one-line notice.
  - Task 10 (OAuth URLs): the account-page URL goes through `check_browser_url`, must also pass `check_link_url` as `https`, and shows a bar naming the host; the browser starts only inside a `Task` after **Open**. A refused address gives a notice and nothing opens.
  - Task 11: **Verify** (unverified other devices only) sends `RequestDeviceVerification`; the dialog opens from the core's events as for any request. No user or device ID is cut from the end.
- Decisions to confirm:
  - `logout_needs_warning(status, other_devices: Option<usize>)`: warns when recovery is `Disabled` or `Incomplete` (not `Enabled`) and there is no other session; an unknown list (Sessions never opened) warns too, to be safe. A recovery state still `Unknown` does not warn. The list is the last one the core sent, so it can be stale.
  - The password prompt is the Sessions screen's own card (the security screens' prompt stays for reset and bootstrap).
  - After an OAuth sign-out the app does not know the outcome: the bar tells the user to press Refresh after approving.
- TDD: pure helpers and the `update` tests were seen failing on assertions against stubs. Two existing logout tests (Task 10) now set recovery `Enabled` first, because logout with recovery off warns. No view test beyond a smoke test that builds every Sessions state.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget-0.14.2 `helpers.rs:561` (`stack(children)`), `helpers.rs:1444` (`text_input`), `text_input.rs:157,163,184,201` (`id`, `secure`, `on_input_maybe`, `on_submit_maybe`); the rest is already used elsewhere in the crate.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 555), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`: pass for the first build (71f9702). Correction: the later round d00f1fb did change core code (`ActionKind::SignOutDevices` and its failure paths), so the ignored suites were not "unneeded": the controller ran the ignored `e2ee_devices` suite (5 tests, two assertions updated) against the local homeserver after d00f1fb, green in about 41 s; the full ignored suite ran in Task 14 (see "Phase 4 wrap-up").
- Review fix (Low, security): a sign-out and an identity reset no longer overlap. While a reset runs (`Busy::Resetting`), `SignOutAsk`, `SignOutAllAsk` and `SignOutConfirmed` send nothing and show a one-line notice (confirmation dropped); while a sign-out is pending, `ResetRequested` and `ResetConfirmed` send nothing and show a one-line notice (`refuse_sign_out_during_reset` in `sessions.rs`, `refuse_reset_during_sign_out` in `security.rs`). So an OAuth account page URL can only belong to the flow that is running. Two `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first; the visible notices are plain text, no extra human check.
- Review fix (Medium, security): closing the Sessions screen no longer forgets a sign-out the core is still running. `Close` drops the password prompt, confirmation and approval bar but keeps `pending` until the core answers (`Devices` without the targets, `ActionFailed { SignOutDevices }` (first built as `ActionFailed { Devices }`, replaced by `ActionKind::SignOutDevices` in d00f1fb), `ReauthRequired`, `OpenAccountManagement`, logout), so `ResetRequested`/`ResetConfirmed` stay refused. `OpenAccountManagement` goes to the Sessions approval bar whenever a sign-out is pending (even if `Busy::Resetting`), never to the reset's browser path; the bar waits for the user's click and stays until the screen is opened. A `ReauthRequired` with the screen closed ends the sign-out quietly (no prompt appears on its own; the user starts it again). Three `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first.
- Review fix (Low, superseded by the next note): a sign-out used to end on a list of core error values copied from private constants.
- Review fix (Low): `submit_reauth` for a `ResetIdentity` prompt checks `refuse_reset_during_sign_out` first (sends nothing, notice); `refuse_sign_out_during_reset` also treats an open `ResetIdentity` password prompt as a reset in progress. Four `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first.
- Review fix (human-approved additive core change): `ActionKind::SignOutDevices`. Every failure of the `SignOutDevices` command path (not logged in, precheck, delete flow, the post-delete device reload, the OAuth account-page message) uses it; error values are unchanged. The app ends a pending sign-out on any `ActionFailed { SignOutDevices, .. }` (a `ReauthFailed` with the password prompt open still keeps the prompt) and shows a one-line notice; `ActionFailed { Devices, .. }` (list, rename, trust watch) never ends it. The copied text constants and the error-value matching are gone, which closes the gap where a failed device reload after a successful delete left `pending` set. Reopening Sessions or Refresh sends `ListDevices`, and a `Devices` answer without the targets still ends the sign-out. Core: unit test of the kind for a sign-out that cannot start; the ignored `e2ee_devices.rs` assertions (wrong password, current device) now expect the new kind. Tests in `state.rs` written first and seen failing on assertions.
- Open issues: the Sessions row has no spinner while a sign-out waits (text only).

## Phase 4 / Task 15 notes (change the recovery key from the Sessions page)
- Added by the human on 2026-10-07 at the checkpoint (Q5 "Change key" had no screen); plan written as Task 15 in `docs/plans/phase4-e2ee.md` and approved the same day. App only: no core, API, dependency or bridge change. The core's `ChangeRecoveryKey` (Task 5, `Recovery::reset_key()`, matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `encryption/recovery/mod.rs:359`) and its integration test (new key differs, opens a fresh store, old key fails) are reused as they are.
- `crates/app/src/sessions.rs`: `RecoveryAction` (`ChangeKey`, `SetUp`, `EnterKey`, with `label` and `message`) and the pure `recovery_line(RecoveryStatus)`: Enabled "Recovery is on." + **Change recovery key**; Disabled "Recovery is off." + **Set up recovery**; Incomplete "This device isn't connected to your recovery." + **Enter recovery key**; Unknown: no line. `screens/sessions.rs` draws it as a bordered row above the device list (shown while the list loads too); the button is off while any security `Busy` is set.
- `crates/app/src/security.rs`: `Overlay::ChangeKeyConfirm`, `Busy::ChangingKey`, `SecurityMessage::{ChangeKeyRequested, ChangeKeyConfirmed, ChangeKeyCancelled}`, `RecoverySetup.replaces_old` and `key_title` ("Your new recovery key" after a change, "Your recovery key" after set-up). Requested opens the card only when recovery is `Enabled`, nothing is busy and no key screen is open; Confirmed sends one `ChangeRecoveryKey` and keeps the card with "Changing your recovery key…"; Cancel is ignored once sent. `RecoveryKeyReady` during `ChangingKey` closes the card and opens the show-once screen (Done gate, Copy, 60 s clipboard clear and the drop on Done, logout and close unchanged). `ActionFailed { Recovery }` during `ChangingKey` clears busy, closes the card and puts the core's fixed message on the Sessions page notice (the main view's notice bar is under the page).
- `screens/security.rs`: `CHANGE_KEY_WARNING` (the plan's draft wording) and `CHANGE_KEY_WAIT`; the card uses the existing `wide` and `secondary` buttons.
- TDD: the 7 `state.rs` tests and the `sessions.rs` test were written first and failed to compile (missing variants, `key_title`, `recovery_line`), then passed: asks first and Cancel sends nothing; one command and nothing while busy (also **Set up recovery**); refused unless recovery is on (Disabled, Incomplete, Unknown); new key screen titled as new, Done needs the box, back on the Sessions page; set-up keeps the first title; failure on the Sessions page; the card, the wait and the new key screen fill a 1000 x 700 window, and the new key screen's `Debug` holds none of the sweep sentinels.
- No integration test (as planned): the E2EE reset helpers live in `crates/core/tests/common`, which app tests cannot reach.
- APIs: no new matrix-sdk or iced API; the views use only widgets and methods already used in the same files (`button::on_press_maybe`, `container::bordered_box`, `row`, `text`, `space`).
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (935 passed, 0 failed; app lib 577), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. No ignored run: no core or bridge code changed.
- New human items (checkpoint list and checklist page): the Recovery line in each state and both themes; the warning wording and the "Your new recovery key" screen; a changed key restores in Element Web (as bob) and the old one is refused.

## Phase 4 security review (Task 14, plan Q15, Q16 (d))
Written by the agent from the code and the tests; the human accepts or rejects the residuals (last item of the checkpoint).

### Where each secret lives
| Secret | Where | Protection |
|---|---|---|
| Access token, refresh token (if any), the SQLite store passphrase | The OS keychain, one `SessionRecord` JSON entry per account (service `io.github.ga_lep.Twicecat`); `SessionRecord::to_bytes` is the only serializer | OS keychain; `Debug` of the record prints `<redacted>`; in the core only `store_passphrase` is `Zeroizing`, and the `to_bytes` output; the tokens are plain `String`s inside the SDK types (`SessionAuth::Matrix(MatrixSession)`, `SessionAuth::OAuth { user: UserSession }`), `SessionRecord` and `SessionAuth` are `Clone`, and the live SDK `Client` keeps its own copies |
| Room keys, Olm/Megolm sessions, cross-signing private keys, backup key, message events | The SDK's SQLite store under `<data>/sessions/<session id>/` (main, `-wal`, `-shm`) | Values encrypted with the store cipher derived from the keychain passphrase; keys of the tables are hashed |
| User ID of the last account | `<data>/last_account` | Plain text on purpose (it is not a secret); the data root holds only this file and `sessions/` (unit test in `paths.rs`) |
| Recovery key (new or typed), recovery passphrase | Never stored by Twicecat or the SDK. The recovery key encrypts secret storage (4S: the backup key and the cross-signing keys), which lives on the server; the key itself is not kept. The core hands a new key to the app once (`RecoveryKeyReady`) | App: `RecoveryKey` wrapper (`Zeroizing`, redacted `Debug`); the show-once screen drops it on **Done**, logout and close; a copy on the clipboard is cleared after 60 s, on logout and on close if it still holds the key |
| Account password (login, UIA for reset, bootstrap and sign-out of devices) | Never stored. Held in the typed field only until submit | `Password` wrapper (`Zeroizing`, redacted `Debug`); moved into the command on submit; every prompt is dropped on Cancel, close, logout |
| QR code (one-time verification secret) | On screen only while the QR phases last | The core prints `QrImage` as its size only; the app builds the image on `ShowQr` and drops it on every other phase |
| SAS emoji and numbers | On screen during the comparison | Not secret after the flow; the app's `Debug` of the dialog prints the phase name only |

### Evidence
- **Log capture (Q16 a):** `crates/core/tests/log_hygiene.rs` (ignored, own binary). One flow (alice and carol after `reset_e2ee_account`: password login, sync, encrypted DM with sentinel bodies both ways, identity reset, recovery enable, second device that recovers with the key, SAS between the two alice devices, device rename, logout) under a global `tracing_subscriber` at filter `info` and, in a second buffer, `DEFAULT_FILTER`. 12 sentinel values (3 access tokens, 3 store passphrases, the passwords, the recovery key, the message bodies) were searched in both buffers: none found. A self-check (a logged sentinel and a scratch-file sentinel must be found, an unrelated one not) was seen red.
- **Data at rest (Q16 b):** the same test, before logout, reads every file under the data root (39 files, `-wal` and `-shm` included) and searches the same sentinels: none found. The scan covers the literal values the test knows, not derived forms (base64, URL-encoded).
- **Keychain-only audit (Q16 c):** unit tests in `paths.rs` (the data root holds only `last_account` and `sessions/<id>`) and `session.rs` (`SessionRecord` `Debug` and error strings carry no sentinel; `Serialize` and `serde_json::to_*` appear in no other source file, a tripwire only). These pinned existing behaviour (not seen red).
- **App redaction sweep (Task 14):** `state.rs` test `the_redaction_sweep_finds_no_secret_in_any_state_or_message` builds an `App` in the show-once key screen, a restore input, the reset password prompt, the Sessions password prompt, a verification in `Emojis` and one in `ShowQr`, and the Sessions screen with a rename in progress, with sentinel values, and finds none in `format!("{app:?}")`; the QR modules are not printed. It also checks the `Debug` of `secret_messages()`: `RecoveryKeyReady`, `RestoreInput`, `ClipboardRead`, `ClipboardFlushed`, both `ReauthInput`, the `ShowQr` event, the login `PasswordChanged`, the `OAuthUrl` and `OpenAccountManagement` events (sentinel in the URL `state`), the composer `Paste` and `Insert` actions, `PickerQuery`, `PickerPicked` and `ReactionToggled`; the states cover the login form, the waiting-for-browser screen, and the composer and picker with typed text. Not covered: messages that carry only IDs, flags or an index. The browser-link state check is weak: `App`'s `Debug` does not print `browser_link`, so a planted leak in `BrowserUrl`'s `Debug` was not caught (the message-level `OAuthUrl` check uses `AuthUrl`'s redaction). It passed at once: it pins existing behaviour (the manual `Debug` impls from Tasks 10, 11, 13).
- **App logging tripwire (Task 14):** `crates/app/tests/no_secret_logging.rs` scans the non-test source of `crates/app/src` for `println!`, `print!`, `eprintln!`, `eprint!`, `dbg!`, `write!`, `writeln!`, `trace!`, `debug!`, `info!`, `warn!`, `error!`, `tracing::event!`, `log::`, `panic!`, `assert!`, `assert_eq!`, `assert_ne!`, their `debug_` forms, `unreachable!`, `todo!`, `unimplemented!` and `.expect(`, and fails when their arguments name a secret-bearing type, field or model (list in the file). It passed at once against the existing code (the app has no `tracing` dependency; the only prints are command-line errors in `main.rs`). To show it bites, a planted `eprintln!("{}", setup.key.expose())` in `security.rs` made it fail ("eprintln! with `.expose(`"), and was removed. A second test feeds the scanner planted violations for every macro family and checks that comments and `#[cfg(test)]` items are ignored.
- **Not asserted:** SDK `debug`/`trace` logging (the SDK logs request details at `debug` by design; documented in `logging.rs` as local debugging only), and the pixels and texts iced renders.

### Residuals (to accept or reject)
- **Tokens in memory:** the access and refresh tokens are plain `String`s in memory (SDK types, live `Client`); only the store passphrase and `to_bytes` output are `Zeroizing` in the core, and `SessionRecord`/`SessionAuth` are `Clone`.
- **`RUST_LOG`:** it replaces the default filter of the stderr subscriber installed by `crates/app/src/main.rs` (`logging::init`, core `logging.rs` `build_filter`), so a user can enable SDK `debug`/`trace` request logging; not covered by `log_hygiene`.
- **Scanner limits (`no_secret_logging.rs`):** it is a tripwire for ordinary mistakes, not a guarantee. It misses `format!` into a local that is printed later, direct writes such as `io::stderr().write_all(..)`, and a secret copied into an innocently named local; code under `#[cfg(test)]` is skipped on purpose. It matches names as text, so deliberate bypasses pass: a space before `!`, raw identifiers (`r#password`), whitespace around `.` or `(`, type aliases and qualified calls (`K::expose`, `Option::expect(x, msg)`), `.security` on receivers other than `self`, and unlisted macros (`format_args!`, `write_fmt`, a bare `event!`/`log!`/`span!` after `use`, `#[tracing::instrument]`). Comments, strings, raw strings and character literals are skipped by one shared lexer step.
- **iced buffers (Q15):** `text_input` keeps plain copies of typed text (the password, the recovery key) in its own and the renderer's text and paragraph buffers; they are not zeroized. The same holds for the Phase 3 editor buffer. `Password` and `RecoveryKey` zeroize only the app's own copies. The SDK also keeps plain `String` copies of a typed key during `recover()`, and ruma's `uiaa::Password` holds the password in a plain `String` while a UIA request is built.
- **Clipboard:** after **Copy** the key leaves `Zeroizing` (iced's clipboard takes and returns `String`); OS clipboard managers and their history can keep it after the 60 s clear, which only touches the live clipboard (checkpoint item; no clipboard-manager hint exists in iced or arboard).
- **On-screen secrets:** the recovery key on the show-once screen and the QR code during a flow are visible to anyone who can see the screen; the QR RGBA buffer is not wiped and iced's renderer may cache the image after the handle is dropped. The SAS emoji are printed by the derived `Debug` of the core's `VerificationPhase::Emojis` (they are not logged anywhere, and only matter during the comparison).
- **At rest:** the user ID in `last_account` is plain text; the SQLite table layout, row counts and sizes are visible (matrix-sdk-sqlite encrypts values and hashes keys, it is not whole-file encryption); the store passphrase is as safe as the OS keychain.
- **`RecoveryKey`'s `PartialEq`** is not constant-time (used in tests and for the clipboard comparison only); `QrImage` is not zeroized.
- **Server and other users:** another user's device names and display names are attacker-controlled; the app sanitizes bidi, control and zero-width characters, caps lengths, elides IDs in the middle (never from the end), and shows the full peer ID in DMs and verification requests. Lax shields (`get_shield(false)`) as Element X.
- **Open Phase 3 security items carried over:** the editor buffer is not zeroized (Q17 of Phase 3); name ambiguity (reply previews, history actors and typing names carry no core ambiguity flag, so a duplicate display name can impersonate); **Copy** writes the raw plain body to the clipboard (hidden control and bidi characters, pastejacking); the residual body-styling spoof and mention label spoofing (Phase 3 open issues).

## Phase 4 open issues (summary)
Every Low or Medium finding from the task reviews (`docs/plans/phase4-e2ee.progress.md`), by status. "Fixed" means a later round closed it with the commit named; "open" items stay for the human or a later phase.

### Fixed by later rounds
- **Task 4 first-bootstrap Medium (M1) and password-less reset Medium (M2):** fixed in 6291447 (raw server keys query, `bootstrap_plan`, password-less reset refused on UIA sessions). The identity-permit High and the password-retry Medium: d172d3f. The reset/sync race that made `e2ee_identity` time out: 2d3b383, then 23fbcf6 (`SyncPause`).
- **Task 5 prechecks and reset races:** 23fbcf6 and ae10b8b. Drop-spawned restore: pause made panic-safe (ae10b8b); race with logout still open (see below).
- **Task 6 `WatchUserTrust` keys-query race:** the tests look the peer up first; the app does it on opening a DM (Task 12). Task 8 refined the watcher.
- **Task 9 send stall after a reset:** 313f356 (Security event held until sync and send queue are restored); the SDK-timeline-clear report was retracted (test bug, 83ef109).
- **Task 10 copied key left on the clipboard on logout or close:** a2c94ae and 4e3abc7 (flush on close, restore field emptied).
- **Task 11 peer user ID cut from the end (Medium):** fixed in 3cc2423 (elided in the middle, full ID on its own line); a request behind a finished card is queued.
- **Task 12 DM Verified badge next to an attacker-settable room name, and the app guessing the DM peer from timeline senders (two Mediums):** fixed in 8a38fd5 (`RoomSummary.dm_peer` from the core, full peer ID in the header, `VerifyPeerRequested` checked against it).
- **Task 13 sign-out and identity reset overlap (Medium, and Lows):** 87b7cc3, aeead79, ae3db3f, d00f1fb (`ActionKind::SignOutDevices`; a sign-out ends only on its own failures).
- **Task 13 stale PROGRESS.md notes** (ignored suites "not run", `ActionFailed { Devices }`): corrected in acb0478.

### Also fixed in the app
- **Task 6 and Task 8 attacker-controlled device and peer names (Info/Low):** the app sanitizes bidi, control and zero-width characters (Tasks 11 and 13).
- **Task 11 a new request replacing a finished card under the mouse (Low):** queued behind it (3cc2423).

### Open: Low or Medium findings
Core:
- **Cross-phase (Phase 1 limitation, re-raised in the Task 14 review):** access and refresh tokens are plain `String`s in SDK types and the live `Client`; `SessionRecord`/`SessionAuth` are `Clone`; only `store_passphrase` and the `to_bytes` output are `Zeroizing` (see Residuals).
- **Task 1:** `seed_recovery` passphrase and key not zeroized end to end (test-only); `e2ee_serial` serializes per binary only (a parallel runner such as nextest could overlap resets); a panicking test leaves its device until the next reset; the `fetch_raw_event_type` check in the room smoke test is weak.
- **Task 2:** `RecoveryKey` derived `PartialEq` is not constant-time; `QrImage` grid not zeroized; the CLI `recovery_key_event_prints_no_key` test is weak (does not capture output); non-room actions send `ActionFailed` with an empty `RoomId` (the app routes by action).
- **Task 3:** `RoomSummary.is_encrypted` is false while the SDK state is Unknown (the app draws a lock for `true` only); a room that just turned encrypted may get its lock only on its next list update.
- **Task 4:** a sync-driven keys query during the OAuth wait can make a cancelled reset report success (Task 5's 23fbcf6 made `reset_end` require complete private keys, which may have narrowed this; not re-verified; otherwise compare against the private identity or require a complete cross-signing status); a wrong password, an Unsupported flow or an OAuth cancel still leaves the backup deleted (the app says so); a millisecond-wide check-then-upload window in the bootstrap retry; `keys_gate` stalls `Security` events for up to 2 minutes during the OAuth approval; a sync-driven keys query racing `keys_gate` is not covered by it; `uiaa::Password` is not zeroized; the first-upload-with-UIA path is covered by a fake only; `has_devices_to_verify_against` counts the own device right after a reset.
- **Task 5:** a Drop-spawned restore after a panic can race logout; bootstrap or reset before `start_sync` runs unpaused; an in-flight send's key query is not covered by disabling the queue; an abort during `pause()` leaves sync stopped (benign today); `wait_for_e2ee_initialization_tasks` has no timeout; prechecks read the cached `recovery.state()`; `ChangeRecoveryKey` drops other clients' secrets stored under the old key; sync shows `Idle` and no messages arrive during a reset (up to about 2 minutes with OAuth).
- **Task 6:** a request from a never-seen device can still be lost; a peer's `UserTrust::Verified` can lag about 30 s; every request event (also in-room ones not for us) spawns an uncapped lookup of about 3 s (skip `content.to != me`, add a semaphore); a stranger's request can hold the single slot until declined; test asserts can print message bodies (`e2ee_verification.rs:365`) and device names on failure; `watch_user_trust` aborts the old watcher without awaiting; the self-verification test does not assert core 2's `Unverified` after login, and the mismatch test does not check core 1's trust unchanged.
- **Task 7:** the `map_qr_state` test lacks the `Cancelled` arm; an incoming request with no QR code emits nothing (stays `Ready`) where the plan says `Waiting`; a `generate_qr_code()` error sends `ActionFailed` and then falls back to SAS (spurious notice).
- **Task 8:** an unseen device lists as `NoKeys` until the sync keys query; renames and deletions by other clients are not re-emitted (the app re-asks on Refresh); `watch_devices` keeps the old list on later read errors; `uia_password` copies the password into a plain `String`; `sign_out_devices` does not dedup or cap the ID list (the app does); on an OAuth account several selected devices open the devices-list page instead of a per-device URL (deviation to confirm at the checkpoint); a stale `UserTrust` from an aborted watcher can follow the new one (the app filters by user ID).
- **Task 9:** `settled()` waits the full 10 s holding the sync gate and `paused` when offline or on a sync error, and can return at once on a stale `Running` (the timeout is silent); a status can still go out mid-pause by a narrow gap (promptness only; `keys_gate` protects); no mock test pins "Security held until restore"; `log_hygiene` keeps unzeroized `serde_json::Value` copies (test only).
App:
- **Task 10:** the copied key leaves `Zeroizing` (documented residual above); the approval URL doc comment says "discovered https endpoint" but only the scheme is checked, host not pinned (fix the comment or compare hosts via `login::host_of`); the password prompt for a cross-signing setup shows the reset wait wording; the bridge test's identity-screen assertion is conditional; "backup already exists" is matched by a substring of the core's `Internal` text (a dedicated `CoreError` variant would be sturdier, a core API change); the Change-key screen (Q5) is not built.
- **Task 11:** QR pixels may linger in iced's renderer cache; a request during a running flow is dropped silently and a third request overwrites the queued one; a late failure shows "Could not start the verification"; a failed confirm in a running verification flow is silent (it only re-enables the buttons); the waiting cards show a text line (iced 0.14 has no spinner widget); the queue test does not assert `asked_other_device`, the queued-overwrite deserves a comment.
- **Task 12:** `dm_peer` comes from the account's own `m.direct`, not from membership (an invited third member does not remove the Verified badge); a changed `m.direct` target may show only on the room's next list update; U+1F512 font coverage is unchecked; the header trust map keeps the Phase 3 measured height (plan wording loose).
- **Other Info and suggestions:** Task 4 comment on `server_identity_seen` never clearing; Task 7 doc note that `StartSas` outside a QR flow is `NotAllowed` on purpose; Task 9 a send right after login is not tested separately; Task 10 the `on_status` half of the restore-field clearing has no test; Task 13 the approval bar host is ASCII (non-ASCII refused).
- **Task 13:** the logout warning is skipped while recovery is `Unknown` or the list is stale, and any other listed session (even unverified or `NoKeys`) suppresses it (consider `Unknown` warns, count `Verified` only); `BootstrapCrossSigning` is not fenced against a pending sign-out (harmless today, tag `OpenAccountManagement` with its action); a stale reset URL after a cancelled reset can still be shown for a sign-out; `ReauthRequired` after Close ends a sign-out quietly; after an OAuth sign-out the user must press Refresh; no spinner while a sign-out waits; raw device IDs are sanitized with no length cap before `elide_middle`; `Internal` error text is shown in notices (fixed strings today).

### Scope cuts (Q17), the Synapse rule, the Q3 decision
- **Out of Phase 4 (Q17):** scanning QR codes (no camera) and QR login (MSC4108); identity-change acknowledgement and pinning UI (badge only); turning recovery or backup off (`disable()`); key export/import files; dehydrated devices; history sharing on invite; "send only to verified devices" settings; encrypted state events; the cross-process store lock; creating encrypted rooms from the UI (Phase 5); i18n.
- **Synapse first-upload rule (Task 4):** Synapse v1.151.0 (`keys.py`, `SigningKeyUploadServlet.on_POST`) allows the first upload of cross-signing keys without UIA (MSC3967) and demands UIA for any replacement, so a restored pre-Phase-4 session bootstraps with no password and a reset always needs one. The local fixed accounts cannot produce a never-had-an-identity account, so the first-time path is covered by CI's fresh volume only; a homeserver that demands UIA for a first upload (matrix.org may) is on the checkpoint.
- **Q3 iced widget decision:** matrix-sdk `qrcode` is enabled, iced `qr_code` is NOT enabled; the code is drawn with the existing `image` widget from the SDK's own grid (6 px per module, nearest filter, 288 px, quiet zone). Cargo.lock gained `matrix-sdk-qrcode` 0.19.1 and `qrcode` 0.14.1 only.
- **Per-test reset cost and test durations:** each E2EE test resets alice and carol (about 3 to 6 s per test); `e2ee_recovery` 7 tests take 78 to 84 s, `e2ee_identity` 5 to 7 tests about 45 s, `e2ee_devices` 5 tests about 41 s, `log_hygiene` about 19 s, the app bridge suite about 5 s. Full ignored runs (about 7.5 and 7 minutes) are in "Phase 4 wrap-up".
- **Test-run notes:** the Task 6 peer lookup and the Task 9 post-reset send were the flakes found and fixed in Phase 4. The Task 14 repeat-run failures are the room backlog described in "Phase 4 wrap-up" (not a core bug); run `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` before an ignored run when the fixed accounts have accumulated rooms.

## Phase 4 wrap-up (Task 14)
- Agent work for Phase 4 is done (Tasks 1 to 14); the human checkpoint below stays open and nothing on it is ticked by the agent (rule 9: rendering, real accounts, Element X, OS clipboard managers, keychains).
- Full ignored suite (`cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`, as CI) against the local Synapse, 75 tests, all passed in both runs:
  - run 1, right after `scripts/create-test-users.sh`: 457 s (7 min 37 s, wall clock including an incremental build);
  - run 2, after `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` only (rooms; no `create-test-users.sh`, so devices and identities left by run 1 carry over): 429 s (7 min 9 s). Both are under the 15-minute limit.
- **Decision (human), repeat-run recipe:** the repeat run is `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` (rooms only), then the full ignored suite WITHOUT `create-test-users.sh`. Residual: the repeat does not start from fresh accounts; it reuses the devices and identities of the previous run (the E2EE tests reset their baseline themselves, Q2).
- **Failed repeat attempts and root cause (found by a debugger):**
  - run 2 (earlier attempt): 1 failure in `timeline_actions`; run 3: 7 failures in `timeline_actions`, 2 in `timeline_items` and 1 in `timeline_live` (all `apply_until` 30 s timeouts). The earlier attempt stopped BLOCKED (rule 8) on the repeat.
  - Evidence: joined rooms accumulate on the fixed test accounts, about 50 per full run (alice had 151 and carol 97 before the failed repeat). After the first sync the SDK event cache processes every joined room serially (`handle_joined_room_update`, about 0.1 to 0.13 s each in a debug build), and a new test room's events wait behind that backlog. Measured time to receive in `markdown_round_trip` alone: about 0.37 s with 1 room, 3.2 s with 25, 6.5 s with 50, 12.7 s with 100; the 30 s `apply_until` limit is exceeded at about 150 rooms with parallel tests.
  - Conclusion: not a test race and not a core bug. The pinned SDK has no "event cache caught up" signal, so the cure is pruning rooms (`scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`). Before this repeat, the prune left alice 49, bob 3 and carol 30 rooms to leave.
- Checks for the commit: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`.

## Phase 5 / Task 4 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (335 core unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok). Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh && cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`, then `docker compose stop`): the first run stopped at `typing_notices_reach_the_other_user` (482 s, see the residuals); the rerun with `--no-fail-fast` passed everything in **454 s**. The `media` binary was run again right after, with no reset in between (Phase 4 Q2 repeat run), and passed; it had also passed twice earlier in a row.
- Test-first: `media.rs` and the `item_actions` test were written against `todo!()` stubs / the old signature and failed (17 of 19 media tests, then a compile error for `MediaInput`); then implemented. The engine tests for the no-session and no-room answers of `FetchMedia`/`SaveMedia` were added with the handlers. The ignored tests were written together with the code and run against the homeserver.
- Core (`crates/core/src/media.rs`, private): `map_media(&MessageType)`, `has_caption`, `thumbnail_plan` (Q7 rule: sender thumbnail, else the plain image itself scaled by the server, else the whole encrypted image when its declared size is at most 10 MiB, else no preview), `allowed_full(size)` (512 MiB), `source_permitted` (well-formed MXC; a `*.localhost` server, i.e. the SDK's local media, only for the user's own local echo), `fetch_media`/`save_media` over a `ContentFetcher` trait (fake in tests), `write_atomically`, `MediaTasks` (semaphore of 4, at most 128 pending tasks, then `Busy`; `send_limits`). `timeline.rs`: image, video, audio and file messages are `Message` items (kind `Text`, body is the caption or file name, `details.media` set, `upload` None until Task 5); `item_actions` takes a `MediaInput` and offers edit only with a caption. A sticker is still a placeholder. `engine.rs`: `FetchMedia` and `SaveMedia` find the item in the selected room's timeline (`ItemNotFound` / `NoRoomSelected` / `NotLoggedIn` / `NotAllowed` for a non-media item) and run in `Active.media`, which `stop_tasks` ends before the avatar tasks; `MediaLimits` is sent from `start_security` (login, OAuth and restore) with a 15 s deadline (`None` on error).
- Review notes from earlier tasks applied: the sender's file name is never joined onto a path (`SaveMedia` takes the caller's full path; the part file is `<path>.twicecat-part`, created with `create_new` after removing a stale one, so a symlink is never followed for writing; the destination is replaced by `rename`, which replaces a link itself); `info.size` over 512 MiB is refused before any request and a returned body over the cap is refused too; thumbnails over 10 MiB are refused; errors carry fixed text only (no server text); `MediaBytes`/`MediaContent` redact `Debug` (Task 3).
- Test helper changes (`tests/common/mod.rs`): `send_attachment_raw` now sets image info (width, height, size) for a PNG and file info (size) for other types, and `send_attachment_with(client, ..)` takes the caller's client. The Task 1 note "plain rooms only" was wrong: `Room::send_attachment` calls `latest_encryption_state` (a server request), so an unsynced client encrypts for an encrypted room; the encrypted test proves it.
- Integration tests (`tests/media.rs`, both take `e2ee_serial`, because the encrypted one resets carol's devices and a parallel plain test got its raw session deleted: found on the first run): plain image (info, identical full bytes, a Synapse thumbnail with a PNG/JPEG signature, `ItemNotFound`, `NoRoomSelected`, `MediaLimits` with a positive limit); encrypted image and 300 KiB random file (identical bytes, `m.room.encrypted` on the server, the stored ciphertext differs and is a multiple of 16 bytes, preview of a small encrypted image = the decrypted file, no preview for a file, `SaveMedia` identical and no `.twicecat-part`, a failed save leaves nothing). `log_hygiene.rs`: an encrypted image (fixture with a `tEXt` sentinel) is sent by a raw client and fetched (full and thumbnail) by both cores; the file name and the image sentinel are scanned in the logs and the data roots: no hit.
- Deviation: the raw download of the stored ciphertext uses the authenticated media endpoint (`authenticated_media::get_content::v1`), not the unauthenticated one named in the plan (the deprecated v3 endpoint would need an `allow(deprecated)`); it shows the same thing (the server holds ciphertext).
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `Media::get_media_content` `src/media.rs:431` (decrypts `MediaSource::Encrypted`, reads the whole body, `Duration::MAX` timeout: :818-870), `is_local_uri` :792, `LOCAL_MXC_SERVER_NAME` :63; matrix-sdk-base 0.19.1 `MediaRequestParameters`/`MediaFormat`/`MediaThumbnailSettings::new` `src/media/mod.rs:40-100,135`, `MediaEventContent` :155-223 (image and video `thumbnail_source` falls back to the file's own source, which is why the core reads `info.thumbnail_source` itself); `Client::load_or_fetch_max_upload_size` `src/client/mod.rs:3690`; ruma-events 0.35.0 `ImageMessageEventContent::{filename,caption}` `room/message/image.rs:72,80`, `ImageInfo`/`ThumbnailInfo` `room.rs:96-166`, `VideoInfo` `message/video.rs:100`, `AudioInfo` `message/audio.rs:124`, `FileInfo` `message/file.rs:96`; matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `EventTimelineItem::is_editable` `timeline/event_item/mod.rs:355` (media of the user's own is editable by the SDK, so the caption rule is ours); matrix-sdk `attachment::{AttachmentInfo, BaseImageInfo, BaseFileInfo}` `src/attachment.rs:30-80`; ruma-client-api 0.25.0 `authenticated_media::get_content::v1::Request::from_uri` `authenticated_media/get_content.rs:82`; matrix-sdk `Room::latest_encryption_state` `src/room/mod.rs:1124`, used by `send_attachment` :2754.
- Residuals / open issues (none needs the human to stop):
  - The SDK reads an encrypted body whole and decrypts it into a second buffer; the 512 MiB cap uses the declared size, so a lying `info.size` could make the SDK allocate the real size (Q20 residual; fixed, see the `info.size` fix note under "Phase 5 security notes"). A full file is held in memory (`Vec`, then once more for `MediaBytes` on `FetchMedia`).
  - A fetch or save in flight is not tied to the open room, but it is ended at logout; it is lost if the session ends first. `SaveMedia` of a half-fetched file leaves nothing on disk.
  - `use_cache = true` also caches full files in the SDK media store (encrypted at rest, scanned in `log_hygiene`).
  - `typing_notices_reach_the_other_user` (timeline_live) failed once in the first ignored run ("carol never saw alice typing") and passed when run again alone and in the second full run; the change under review does not touch typing. Not flaky twice, so not a rule-8 stop; watch it.
- New human items: none (the viewer, cards and Save dialog are Tasks 9 and 12).

## Phase 5 / Task 5 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (343 core unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok). Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh && cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`, then `docker compose stop`): passed first time in **529 s**. The `media` binary was run again right after with no reset in between (repeat run) and passed (7 tests, 95 s).
- Test-first: the pure functions (`parse_mime`, `check_size`, `attachment_config`) and the progress table test were written against stubs and failed (6 of 7, then 1), then implemented; one more failure found by the test ("image/" and wildcards parse as valid MIME types in `mime` 0.3, now refused). The ignored tests were written after the code; to check they test something, upload progress was switched off once and `upload_progress_reaches_the_local_echo` failed ("no partial progress in 2 updates"), then it was switched back on.
- Core: new private `upload.rs` (`parse_mime`, `check_size`, `attachment_config(&OutgoingAttachment, size)`, `validate`, `UploadQueue`). `SendAttachment` is handled by `Engine::send_attachment`: unlogged-in gives `NotLoggedIn`; a bad MIME, an empty file name, a bad thumbnail type, a thumbnail over 10 MiB, a malformed reply target, a thread request, or a room that is not joined gives `ActionFailed { Upload, NotAllowed }` before anything is queued; a joined room that is not the selected one gives `NoRoomSelected` (the local echo lives in the selected room's timeline). Valid jobs go to one worker per session (in `MediaTasks`, so `stop_tasks` ends it), so several files reach the send queue in the order they were sent (a bounded queue of 16, then `Busy`). The worker takes the limit (the server's, with a 15 s deadline, cached by the SDK; or the config override), checks the size of a `Path` source from its metadata before reading it, reads with `tokio::fs::read`, checks again, builds the config and calls `Timeline::send_attachment(AttachmentSource::Data{..}, mime, config).use_send_queue()`. An unknown limit means 100 MiB. Errors afterwards arrive as `ActionFailed { Upload }` with fixed text. `enable_upload_progress(true)` is set in `start_security` (login, OAuth and restore). `timeline.rs`: `map_upload_progress` fills `details.media.upload` from `NotSentYet { progress }` (bytes sent and total).
- Deviations: (1) `attachment_config` takes the size too (`attachment_config(&OutgoingAttachment, size)`): the size of a `Path` source is not known before the read. (2) `CoreConfig` gains `max_upload_bytes_override: Option<u64>` (the plan's "fake limit set in the test core config"); `crates/app/src/main.rs`, the CLI and the app bridge tests set `None`, nothing else in the app changes. (3) The thumbnail is passed only for images (the SDK ignores it for other kinds anyway).
- Rule-8 style choice, recommendation applied: `thread_root` on an attachment is refused with `NotAllowed` (a thread reply needs the thread-focused timeline of Task 8). Task 8 must lift this by sending through the thread timeline (the SDK infers the thread relation from the timeline's focus: `Timeline::infer_reply`).
- Integration tests (`tests/media.rs`; each takes `e2ee_serial`): round trip in a plain room and in an encrypted room (alice's core sends the 1200x900 fixture as bytes with the 64x48 thumbnail and a random 300 KiB file from a path; the echo is seen as `Sending` with kind, size and dimensions before `Sent`; carol's core gets identical full bytes; the thumbnail is the exact 64x48 bytes in the encrypted room and a PNG in the plain one); progress (4 MiB file, a partial `upload: Some(..)` is recorded, then `Sent`); refusals (a fake limit of 10,000 bytes: a path and bytes of 10,001 bytes give `NotAllowed`, no echo on alice's side and nothing at carol, a file of exactly 10,000 bytes sent afterwards arrives); discard (a 16 MiB upload runs, a second file's echo is discarded with `DiscardSend`, it disappears, carol never sees it, the first file arrives). Test helper: `start_core_with_upload_limit`.
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `Timeline::send_attachment` `src/timeline/mod.rs:691`, `AttachmentConfig` (timeline version with `in_reply_to`) :210-221, `AttachmentSource::{Data, File}` :1059, `SendAttachment::use_send_queue` `src/timeline/futures.rs:48` and its `into_future` :58-100 (reads the source, `infer_reply`, `send_queue().send_attachment`), `Timeline::infer_reply` `mod.rs:502`, `EventSendState::NotSentYet { progress }` and `MediaUploadProgress` `src/timeline/event_item/local.rs:69-108`; matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `attachment::{AttachmentInfo, Base*Info, Thumbnail}` `src/attachment.rs:30-165`, `SendQueue::enable_upload_progress` `src/send_queue/mod.rs:308`, `AbstractProgress` `src/send_queue/progress.rs:41` (re-exported `send_queue/mod.rs:197`), `Client::load_or_fetch_max_upload_size` `src/client/mod.rs:3690`.
- Residuals / open issues (none needs the human to stop):
  - The whole file is held in memory (read buffer, then the SDK copies it for the queue and encrypts it): about 2 to 3 times the file size, up to the limit. A `Bytes` source is copied once more out of its `Arc`.
  - `DiscardSend` of an upload that has started: the SDK may refuse; the core reports the existing discard failure (`Internal`) as for text messages. The app shows "can't cancel now" (Task 10). Only the queued-behind case is tested.
  - A refused attachment of a room that is joined but not selected says `NoRoomSelected`; the app only uploads into the open room.
  - The filename is passed to the SDK as the caller gave it (no path stripping): the app must pass the base name only (Task 10).
  - The upload deadline is the SDK's (none); a stuck upload shows progress stalling. The worker only waits for the enqueue, not the upload.
- New human items: none (the picker, drag and drop, paste and progress bar are Tasks 10 and the checkpoint list).

## Phase 5 / Task 6 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (378 core unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`): all green in 578 s wall (including compile). The `notifications` binary (7 tests, about 50 s) passed on three consecutive runs.
- Test-first: the pure functions (`classify`, `parse_event`, `contains_keyword`, `validate_keyword`, `totals`, `run_totals`, mode mapping, `Seen`) were written as `todo!()` stubs with their tests and 31 of 32 failed; then implemented (32 pass). The engine command test for notification commands was edited before the engine wiring but not run red in between. The homeserver tests were written after the code.
- Core: new private `notifications.rs`. `NotificationTasks::start` registers one notification handler per client (before the sync starts) and spawns the settings worker (jobs queue of 32, more gives `Busy`), a settings forwarder (`NotificationSettings` after login and on each `subscribe_to_changes` tick), a room-change forwarder and the unread-totals task. `classify` is pure: drops own sender, events older than session start minus 60 s, duplicates (512-entry ring), non-message events other than invites for the user; `Mention` from a highlight tweak; `Encrypted` with the fixed preview "Encrypted message"; preview capped at 120 characters, one line, formatting dropped, spoilers replaced by "(spoiler)", reply fallback stripped, edits dropped. Names (room, sender) are reduced to one clean line (no control or bidi characters, 120 characters) by `clean_name`; the app still sanitizes for display (Task 11).
- Keywords: the SDK's keyword push rule carries no highlight tweak (matrix-sdk `notification_settings/command.rs:63`), so the core also flags a text message that contains a configured keyword as a whole word (case-insensitive) as `Mention`. `AddKeyword`/`RemoveKeyword` trim and validate (1-64 characters, no control characters, at most 50); failures use fixed text and never quote the keyword.
- Settings commands: `SetRoomNotificationMode{mode: Some}` calls `set_room_notification_mode`; `mode: None` calls `delete_user_defined_room_rules` (replaces the plan's `ResetRoomNotificationMode`, Task 3); `SetDefaultNotificationMode` writes both the encrypted and unencrypted default of the chosen kind; an unknown or malformed room is refused with `UnknownRoom`. `RoomSummary.notification_mode` comes from `cached_user_defined_notification_mode()`.
- Unread totals: `UnreadTotals` over joined, non-muted rooms (muted by its own rule, else by the account default for its kind), sent at once and then only when the value changed, at most once per second (paused-time test). `tokio` gains the `test-util` feature as a dev-dependency (no new packages, lock unchanged).
- `show_message_content` is a local app setting (Task 14/11): the core always supplies the preview, and the app must drop it when the setting is off.
- Integration tests (`tests/notifications.rs`, verbatim ignore reason, serialized, baseline reset first): DM notification with preview; mention in a muted room silent (bounded by two DM sentinels) then `mode: None` gives `Mention`; keyword in a mentions-only room (plain message silent, keyword message `Mention`, removal); bad keywords refused and default modes round trip; own messages never notify; invite gives `Invite`; totals rise after a DM and return to 0 after `MarkRead`.
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `Client::register_notification_handler` `src/client/mod.rs:1380`, handlers run in order after each sync `src/sync.rs:279`, `Client::notification_settings` `:3572`, `joined_rooms` `:1448`, `room_info_notable_update_receiver` `:742`; `NotificationSettings::subscribe_to_changes` `src/notification_settings/mod.rs:124`, `delete_user_defined_room_rules` `:358`, `enabled_keywords` `:417`, plus `get_default_room_notification_mode`, `get_user_defined_room_notification_mode`, `set_room_notification_mode`, `set_default_room_notification_mode`, `add_keyword`, `remove_keyword` in the same file; matrix-sdk-base 0.19.1 `sync::Notification` `src/sync.rs:409`, notification collection `response_processors/notification.rs:59` (called for sync timelines and for invite stripped state), `Room::unread_notification_counts` `room/mod.rs:181`, `num_unread_notifications` `:197`, `num_unread_mentions` `:206`, `cached_user_defined_notification_mode` `:455`. The handler fires under sliding sync (the notifications integration tests pass on the sliding-sync client).

Task 11's UI should not rely on the list alone after `SetRoomNotificationMode`; it can use the `NotificationSettings`/own state.

## Phase 5 / Task 7 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (396 core unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`): all green in 644 s wall (including compile); the `rooms` binary (13 tests) took 63 s and passed again on an immediate second run. The new `rooms` binary (8 command tests plus the 5 helper smoke tests) takes about 70 s on its own.
- Test-first: the pure helpers (`clean_name`, `clean_topic`, `create_room_request`, `parse_room_target`, `merge_via`, `role_of`, `permissions`, `sort_members`, `check_image`) were `todo!()` stubs with 13 tests, all red; the space filter composition (`filter_spec` with `children`) and `spaces::{view_of, children_of}` were written as tests that did not compile or panicked first; the engine test `room_and_profile_commands_refuse_bad_input_before_any_request` failed with "not implemented yet" before the wiring; the `JoinRoom` Debug redaction test failed before the fix. The homeserver tests were written after the code. Two test problems found by the homeserver run (an index into `created` that included an earlier room, and the plan's "all false" permissions, see below) were fixed in the tests.
- Core: new private `rooms.rs` (pure rules plus `RoomTasks`, a job queue of 32 with `Busy`, a 60 s deadline per request, fixed failure texts, a server `M_FORBIDDEN` or a failed local power-level check gives `NotAllowed`) and `spaces.rs` (`SpaceService` forwarder). `Active` gains `rooms: RoomTasks`, started next to the security tasks. The engine validates input first, then finds the room (`UnknownRoom` for an unknown or malformed ID, `NotAllowed` for the wrong state), then queues the job.
- Rules: names and display names are trimmed, 1-255 characters, no control or bidirectional characters; topics are at most 1,000 characters (line breaks allowed), a blank topic clears it; alias local parts are 1-64 characters of `[a-z0-9._=-]`; at most 100 invitees (parsed, deduplicated); `parse_room_target` takes `#alias:server`, `!id:server` (and v12 IDs without a server) or an exact `https://matrix.to/#/` link to a room or alias, trims, caps at 512 bytes and 8 `via` servers, and fetches nothing (users, events, other hosts, `http`, `matrix:` URIs and extra arguments are refused); avatar images (room and own) must be 1 byte to 1 MiB, a PNG, JPEG, GIF or WebP by signature, and declared as exactly that type (the core has no image decoder, so only the signature is checked; the app decodes and downscales, Task 12/15).
- Behaviour: `CreateRoom` gives `RoomCreated` (encrypted rooms carry `m.room.encryption` with `m.megolm.v1.aes-sha2` in `initial_state`); `CreateDm` refuses the user's own ID and serializes under a lock; it reuses the room it made earlier this session (the SDK learns of `m.direct` only at the next sync) or else the existing DM with the smallest room ID (the SDK lists DMs in no fixed order, so the pick is made stable); `JoinRoom` gives `RoomJoined`; `AcceptInvite` gives `RoomJoined` and `DeclineInvite` / `LeaveRoom` / `InviteUser` / the settings commands answer with nothing on success (the room list, the member list and `RoomDetails` show the change); `LeaveRoom` first stops the open timeline when it is that room; `OpenRoomDetails` replaces the previous watcher and re-sends `RoomDetails` when the room info changes and the view differs (a pure power-level change may not trigger a room-info update, so the permissions can lag until the next state change); `can_enable_encryption` is false when the room is already encrypted; `ListRoomMembers` lists joined and invited members, sorted by role then name (named members first), cut at 5,000 with `truncated`; names pass through `clean_line` (one line, control and bidirectional characters removed). `OwnProfile` is sent after login and after each profile command, read from the server; changes made on another device show at the next profile command or login.
- Spaces: `RoomTasks::start` spawns the `SpaceService` forwarder: `CoreEvent::Spaces` (first the current filters, then after each change) and a watch of each space's descendants. `RoomListQuery.space` adds `new_filter_identifiers(descendants)` between the tab and the name match (`filter_spec(query, children)`, unit tests for composition, unknown space = empty list, no space ignores children); the room list re-filters (keeping its pages) when a space gains or loses rooms.
- Also: `Command::JoinRoom` `Debug` no longer prints the target (Task 3 note); `notifications::clean_line(text, max)` is the former `clean_preview` with the limit as a parameter.
- Deviation from the plan: carol, an ordinary member of a `PrivateChat` room on the local Synapse, gets `can_invite: true` (the default `invite` level is 0), so the test asserts only that the four edit permissions are false and `can_invite` is true, not "all false". The core reports what the power levels say.
- Integration tests (`tests/rooms.rs`, verbatim ignore reason, one at a time, alice = user 1, carol = user 3, rooms left at the end): encrypted create + invite + accept (`is_encrypted` for alice, `is_invite` for carol, name and topic after joining, `RoomDetails`); join by alias (and a junk target refused); leave of the open room (list entry and joined member gone, no error); decline (and a later accept refused); settings (name, topic, 64x48 avatar reach carol; a JPEG type on PNG bytes is refused; alice's permissions all true; `EnableRoomEncryption` makes both sides encrypted and turns `can_enable_encryption` off; carol's edit permissions are off and `SetRoomName` gives `ActionFailed { Rooms, NotAllowed }`); members (alice `Creator` or `Admin`, first; carol `User`); two concurrent `CreateDm` give one room, and a DM with oneself is refused; profile (own profile after login, `SetDisplayName` reaches `OwnProfile` and carol's member list, restored at the end); spaces (`create_space_raw` with one child: `Spaces` lists it at level 0 with one child, `SetRoomListQuery { space }` lists exactly that child, an unknown space lists nothing).
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `Client::create_room` `src/client/mod.rs:1931` (marks `is_direct` rooms in `m.direct`), `create_dm` `:1964`, `get_dm_rooms` `:1992`, `join_room_by_id_or_alias` `:1840`, `get_room` `:1487`; `Room::leave` `src/room/mod.rs:471` (an invited room is forgotten as well), `join` `:520`, `invite_user_by_id` `:2033`, `enable_encryption` `:2354` (waits for a sync), `set_name` `:3000`, `set_room_topic` `:3005`, `upload_avatar` `:3040`, `members` `:1233`, `encryption_state` `:1115`; `Account::get_display_name` `src/account.rs:118`, `set_display_name` `:139`, `get_avatar_url` `:194`, `set_avatar_url` `:241`, `upload_avatar` `:330`; `Error::client_api_error_kind` `src/error.rs:428`. matrix-sdk-base 0.19.1 `Room::power_levels` `src/room/mod.rs:392`, `name` `:425`, `topic` `:430`, `avatar_url` `:240`, `canonical_alias` `:250`, `subscribe_info` `src/room/room_info.rs:86`, `EncryptionState::is_encrypted` `src/room/encryption.rs:55`, `RoomMember::power_level` `src/room/members.rs:402`, `membership` `:489`, `RoomMemberships::ACTIVE` `:541`. matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `SpaceService::new` `src/spaces/mod.rs:154`, `subscribe_to_space_filters` `:299`, `SpaceFilter` `:773`, `new_filter_identifiers` `src/room_list_service/filters/mod.rs:94`. ruma-common 0.20.0 `MatrixToUri::parse` `src/identifiers/matrix_uri.rs:283`; ruma-events 0.35.0 `RoomPowerLevels::for_user` `src/room/power_levels.rs:591`, `user_can_invite` `:676`, `user_can_send_state` `:724`, `RoomEncryptionEventContent::with_recommended_defaults` `src/room/encryption.rs:59`; eyeball-im 0.9.1 `VectorDiff::apply` `src/vector.rs:433`.
- Residuals / open issues:
  - `RoomDetails` permissions follow room-info updates only; a power-level change alone may not refresh them until another state change.
  - `LeaveRoom` and `DeclineInvite` leave only the named room (a direct leave request; `Room::leave` would also leave the rooms the creator-controlled `predecessor` names). The SDK marks the room left at the next sync, not at once, and a declined invite is also forgotten on the server.

## Phase 5 / Task 8 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (399 core unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`): all green in 671 s wall (including compile and the prune; the `threads` binary passed 5/5). The new `threads` binary (5 tests) takes about 70 s on its own.
- Test-first: `route_item` was a stub that always answered `Main` (the table test failed on `thread:0`); the five homeserver tests were run against the unimplemented core first (four failed with "not implemented yet" or a timeout; the Element-style one already passed, see below). `map_thread_summary` and its table were written together with the code (no red run). Mutation check: with `hide_threaded_events: false` the summary test fails ("alice's main shows the reply").
- Decision (Task 3 note): `PaginateThread` is **used**, and the plan's "`PaginateBackwards` gains the thread target through the `thread:` prefix rule" is **dropped**. `PaginateBackwards { room_id }` carries no item ID, so there is nothing to route on; `PaginateThread { room_id, root }` names the thread directly. `route_item` still routes the item-ID commands: `Edit`, `Redact`, `ToggleReaction`, `RetrySend`, `DiscardSend`, `FetchMedia`, `SaveMedia`. An ID that starts with `thread:` while no thread is open gives `ItemNotFound`.
- Decision (Task 5 note): the `thread_root` refusal in `SendAttachment` is **not lifted** here. The plan's Task 8 acceptance criteria and file list name `Send` only (`upload.rs` is not in the list), although Q16 says `OutgoingAttachment` has the same field. Lifting it is small (pick `TimelineTarget::Thread(root)` in `try_queue_attachment`, drop the check in `attachment_config`, add a homeserver test); **Task 10 must do it** before the thread panel can attach files. Recommended and left open for the human.
- Core: new private `threads.rs` (`THREAD_ID_PREFIX`, `Route`, pure `route_item`, `OpenThread`, `open`, `paginate_task`). The main timeline is built with `room.timeline_builder().with_focus(Live { hide_threaded_events: true })`. `Selected` gains `thread: Option<OpenThread>`, so selecting another room, leaving the room and logout all close the thread through `Selected::stop` (the forwarder is aborted before the actions end). `OpenThread` needs the room to be selected (`NoRoomSelected` otherwise), replaces the open thread, and builds a `TimelineFocus::Thread` timeline with the `thread:` prefix; it emits `ThreadDiff` (first a `Reset`) and `ThreadPagination`. `CloseThread` with nothing open does nothing (without a session: `NotLoggedIn`). `Send` with `thread_root` goes through the thread timeline, which adds the relation; it fails with `NotAllowed` unless that thread is open (and `NoRoomSelected` for another room). `forward_timeline` now takes a wrapper for the event and a list of follow-up events (the draft for the main timeline, `ThreadPagination { Idle }` for a thread). The `unimplemented_target` / "not implemented yet" stubs are gone.
- Findings from the SDK and the homeserver: (1) thread summaries appear on the root with `ThreadingSupport::Disabled`, so the Q16 stop condition was not hit; the Element-style raw-client test passed before any core change for that reason. (2) A new thread timeline is **empty until it paginates once** (the root and replies that sync did not bring come from there), so `OpenThread` runs one `PaginateThread` itself; the app does not need to. (3) The SDK has no pagination status for a thread (`dummy_pagination_status`, matrix-sdk `event_cache/caches/thread/pagination.rs:53`), so the core reports `Idle` on open, `Loading` while a page loads and `Idle` or `ReachedStart` after it. (4) The thread timeline's item IDs restart at `thread:0` for each opened thread, so an item ID is only meaningful for the thread whose `ThreadDiff` carried it (the app clears its thread model on a new root).
- Summary mapping: `map_thread_summary` gives `replies` always, and the latest sender, name and preview only when the latest event is loaded (`TimelineDetails::Ready`); the preview is the first line of the message cut at 200 characters (as reply quotes), and none for a non-message or an empty body. `MessageDetails.thread` is set from `MsgLikeContent::thread_summary`.
- Integration tests (`tests/threads.rs`, verbatim ignore reason, alice = user 1, carol = user 3): summary and hiding (carol replies through her thread timeline; alice's main shows `replies == 1`, latest sender carol, and neither main timeline shows the reply for 3 s); thread timeline (alice's thread has the root and carol's reply, all IDs prefixed, none in the main timeline; alice sends in the thread and the summary becomes 2); actions in a thread (alice reacts through the `thread:` ID, carol's thread shows the reaction); Element-style reply from a raw client (`m.thread` relation, counted as 1); a closed or never-opened thread refuses `Send`, and selecting another room closes it (`PaginateThread` for the old room is refused, not an internal error).
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `TimelineFocus::{Live { hide_threaded_events }, Thread { root_event_id }}` `src/timeline/mod.rs:126,146`, `RoomExt::timeline_builder` `src/timeline/traits.rs:62,80` (adds read receipt tracking), `TimelineBuilder::with_focus` `src/timeline/builder.rs:74`, `with_internal_id_prefix` `:92`, `build` `:163`, internal ID `"{prefix}{n}"` `src/timeline/controller/metadata.rs:225`, `Timeline::paginate_backwards` (thread: `run_backwards_once`, returns `reached_start`) `src/timeline/pagination.rs:30,57`, `Timeline::send` adds the thread relation through `infer_reply` `src/timeline/mod.rs:363,502`, `Timeline::room` `:228`, `ThreadSummary` `src/timeline/event_item/content/msg_like.rs:51`, `EmbeddedEvent` `.../reply.rs:65`. ruma-events 0.35.0 `Thread::plain` `src/relation.rs:134` (tests only).
- Residuals / open issues:
  - A thread timeline is rebuilt on every `OpenThread` (the older one is dropped); a thread opened by an ID that is not a thread root gives a timeline with just that event.
  - `ThreadPagination` is the core's own state, not the SDK's: two `PaginateThread` in a row share one permit and the second is dropped (the app asks again when idle).
  - Thread read receipts are not sent (rejected in Q16); `MarkRead` still acts on the main timeline.
- New human items: none (the Threads line of the checkpoint already covers summaries, the panel and the hidden replies).

## Phase 5 / Task 9 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (620 app unit tests, 4 in `tests/timeline_layout.rs`, including the Phase 3 `timeline_batches_under_16ms` and layout tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok). App-only task (no core or bridge code touched), so no ignored run.
- Test-first: the `media.rs` pure tests were written against `todo!()` stubs and all 18 failed, then passed after the implementation (one test bug found: JPEG cannot encode RGBA). The `update` tests (`state.rs`, 21 new) were written before the model and handlers and failed to compile, then passed; five failed once on the first run for reasons in the tests or in two existing source-scan guards (`state.rs` must not contain `open::`: the launcher moved to `dialogs::open_file`; the tests drained non-media commands). The widget layout tests (`timeline_pane.rs`, `tests/timeline_layout.rs`) were written after the widgets and passed at once; they were not mutation-checked.
- App code (all in `crates/app`, no new dependency, no `matrix-sdk`):
  - `media.rs` (pure): `media_box(Option<u32>, Option<u32>) -> Size` (fit 400x300, never upscaled, 320x180 when either side is missing or zero, never zero); `decode_media(bytes, Limits) -> Option<(Handle, w, h)>` over `decode_rgba` (format allow-list Png/Jpeg/WebP/Gif, limits 16384 px per side and 256 MiB, `ImageReader::decode` reserves the allocation from the header first, a GIF gives its first frame; EXIF orientation is not applied); `decode_thumbnail` (scaled to at most 400x300 before it reaches the renderer) and `decode_full`; `MediaCache` (byte-budgeted LRU, 64 MiB for thumbnails, an entry over the budget is refused and evicts nothing); `is_dangerous_extension`; `suggested_file_name`; `size_label`, `type_label`, `card_name` (via `text_safety`, 40 characters), `has_caption`; `MediaModel` (cache, in-flight set capped at 24, the viewer, saves waiting for the core, saved paths for Open, the Open question) with a counts-only `Debug`; `SavePath` (redacted `Debug`).
  - `dialogs.rs`: `Dialogs` trait with `SystemDialogs` (`window::oldest()` then `window::run` to parent the `rfd::AsyncFileDialog::save_file`, then the future) and a test-only `RecordingDialogs`; `open_file` (the only call of `open::that_detached` on a saved file). `App::set_dialogs` swaps the fake in.
  - `state.rs`: `Message::{MediaClicked, ViewerClosed, SaveRequested, SavePicked, OpenRequested, OpenConfirmed, OpenDismissed, MediaOpened, MediaDecoded}`. `FetchMedia { Thumbnail }` is sent from `timeline_effects` (diff, scroll, measure, pagination) for image messages in the viewport widened by one screen each way, once per key while it is pending or cached (`MediaModel::needs_fetch`; an evicted entry can be fetched again). The core's `Media` answer is decoded in `Task::perform` (never in `update`) and only when somebody still waits (an in-flight thumbnail, or the viewer on that key); an error or an undecodable image stores `Unavailable` and is not retried. Clicking an image opens the viewer and sends `FetchMedia { Full }`; closing, selecting another room, deselecting and logout drop the full image. `SaveMedia` is sent only from `SavePicked`, with the dialog's path as it is: it must be absolute with a file name, it is never built from `MediaContent::filename` (the filename only seeds the dialog through `suggested_file_name`), and the item must still be media of the open room. `MediaSaved` moves a save this app started to the saved list; `ActionFailed { Media }` forgets the room's waiting saves and shows a notice, so a late success for a save reported failed opens nothing.
  - Open: offered for any file after a successful save (Q8 changed by the human at the checkpoint, 2026-10-08: the confirmation is the guard). It first shows the confirmation bar "Open <saved file name>?" and only **Open** runs `open::that_detached` in a `Task`. When the sender's file name or the name the user saved it under may run code (`may_run_code`: programs, scripts, installers, shortcuts and links, help files, web pages, disk images and their macOS counterparts, 72 extensions; no extension, trailing dots or blanks, and a `/` in the "extension" also count), the bar adds "This file can run a program on your computer. Open it only if you trust the sender."
  - Screens: `timeline_pane.rs` draws an image in the fixed box (a note "Loading..." or "No preview" until the thumbnail is in; the click target is the box), or a 400x64 card with kind, sanitised name, size, type, **Save...** and, when allowed, **Open**; the caption is drawn only when the body differs from the file name. `timeline.rs` estimates a media row from `media_box` / `CARD_H` (it never reads the cache), so a thumbnail arriving changes no height. `screens/media_viewer.rs` is a layer above the main view in an always-two-deep stack (an `opaque` backdrop, the name, **Save...**, **Close**, and `image::viewer`, which zooms with the wheel and pans by dragging); Esc closes it through a keyboard subscription that exists only while the viewer is open. The Open bar sits with the other bars above the composer.
- Tests added: `media.rs` (box rules, the five formats, first GIF frame, other formats refused, 20000x20000 header refused, side and allocation limits are what refuse, truncated bytes of all four formats refused, LRU order and budget, dangerous and suggested names, long names), `state.rs` (visible window plus one screen, once per key, scrolling asks for new rows only, non-images never fetch, a thumbnail arriving changes neither the prefix nor the measured heights nor the estimate, decode runs in a task, failures are not retried, unsolicited answers dropped, viewer open/close/late answers/failure/room change/logout, only images open the viewer, save name and path rules, Open rules and the confirmation bar, failed save, `Debug` sentinels for the app and the media messages, widget-tree tags of the base unchanged by the viewer), `timeline_pane.rs` (image box and card laid out to a fixed size whatever has arrived, estimate equals the drawn box, rows build with and without captions) and `tests/timeline_layout.rs` (laid-out row heights identical before and after thumbnails arrive, and within 8 px of the estimates).
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): iced_runtime 0.14.0 `window::run` `src/window.rs:463`, `window::oldest` :289, `trait Window: HasWindowHandle + HasDisplayHandle` :196 (re-exported by `iced::window`, `iced-0.14.0/src/window.rs`); iced_widget 0.14.2 `image::viewer` `src/image.rs:36`, `Viewer::{content_fit, width, height}` `src/image/viewer.rs:49,61,67`, `Image::{width, height, content_fit}` `src/image.rs:91,97,118`, `button::text` `src/button.rs:673`, `container::bordered_box` `src/container.rs:595`, `opaque` `src/helpers.rs:577`; iced_core 0.14.0 `Handle::from_rgba` `src/image.rs:147`; iced_runtime `Task::{done, perform, then, future}` `src/task.rs:39,48,128,258`; iced_futures 0.14.0 `listen_with` `src/event.rs:26`, `Subscription::none` `src/subscription.rs:106`; rfd 0.17.2 `AsyncFileDialog::{set_file_name, set_title, set_parent, save_file}` `src/file_dialog.rs:221,231,241,328` (`set_parent` takes `&W` with `W: ?Sized`, so the `&dyn Window` fits), `FileHandle::path` `src/file_handle/native.rs:136`; open 5.4.4 `that_detached` `src/lib.rs:285`; image 0.25.10 `ImageReader::{limits, decode}` `src/io/image_reader_type.rs:137,311` (`decode` reserves `total_bytes` against the limits before decoding), `DynamicImage::thumbnail` `src/images/dynimage.rs:913`.
- Residuals / open issues (none needs the human to stop):
  - EXIF orientation is applied to decoded images: `decode_oriented` reads it with `ImageDecoder::orientation` and turns the picture with `DynamicImage::apply_orientation` (image 0.25.10 `src/codecs/jpeg/decoder.rs:140`, `src/images/dynimage.rs:1161`), so the viewer and the thumbnail show a phone photo the right way up. It also refuses a picture whose RGBA size (`width * height * 4`) is over `max_alloc` before decoding, since `DynamicImage::from_decoder` does not reserve against it. Test-first: `exif_rotation_is_applied_to_thumbnails_and_the_viewer` (the 80x60 orientation-6 fixture shows as 60x80, pixels equal to the stored ones turned clockwise) failed with `(80, 60)` before the change.
  - Decoding runs inside `Task::perform` on iced's default thread-pool executor, so it never blocks `update` or drawing, but a 16384 x 16384 image takes a while and a big allocation (capped at 256 MiB by the limits); there is no cancel for a decode whose viewer was closed (its result is dropped).
  - Media keys are `(room_id, item_id)`. Thread timelines (Task 13) use `thread:N` IDs that restart per thread (the open Task 8 MEDIUM); Task 13 must put the root in the key (and in the item commands) before it shows media in the thread panel, or two threads would share thumbnails.
  - Video and audio show a card only (no inline video thumbnail); `MediaContent::has_thumbnail` is not used by the app (every image message asks, the core decides what a preview is).
  - The sender's own local echoes (Task 10) already ask for a thumbnail like any image; upload progress and the progress bar are Task 10.
  - The save dialog is parented to the oldest window; with several windows (not a Phase 5 case) it would be the first one.
- New human items (added to the checkpoint list below): the Save dialog parenting and portal behaviour on GNOME/KDE Wayland and X11; the Open confirmation bar; the viewer's Esc, Close and Save...; card layout with long, bidi and empty names; "Loading..."/"No preview" boxes in both themes; EXIF-rotated photo behaviour.

## Phase 5 / Task 10 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (680 app unit tests, 5 in `tests/timeline_layout.rs`, 12 doc tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh && cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`): all pass in **766 s** (about 12.8 min, under the 15-minute limit, `--no-fail-fast`); `docker compose stop` done. An earlier full run stopped at `notifications::bad_keywords_and_default_modes_are_handled` (group default read back as MentionsAndKeywords instead of AllMessages) and failed a second time on its own rerun of that binary; then it passed alone, 2 more times as a whole binary, and in the 766 s run, and also passes on the clean HEAD. The change touches nothing in that path, so it is recorded as a flake of the Task 6 test (probably a stale settings event or leftover account state after the first failure); watch it. The new `threads` tests were also run twice in a row on their own (7 passed each time, 24 s and 27 s).
