- Core change (Task 8 hand-over, lifted here): an attachment with `thread_root` now goes through the open thread's timeline. `try_queue_attachment` (engine.rs) picks `Selected::timeline_for(TimelineTarget::Thread(root))` (the main timeline otherwise), so a thread that is not open gives `NotAllowed` and a malformed root gives the parse error; `attachment_config` no longer refuses a thread root (the thread timeline adds the relation, `infer_reply` is called with the thread focus). `SendAttachment` docs now say: joined but unselected room gives `NoRoomSelected`, a thread root needs `OpenThread`, failures are `ActionFailed { Upload, .. }`.
- Core tests: homeserver `an_attachment_sent_into_an_open_thread_lands_in_the_thread` (carol opens the thread and sends a file with `thread_root`; her thread shows it, alice's summary counts it, neither main timeline shows it, alice opens the thread and finds it) failed first with `action Upload failed: NotAllowed`, then passed; `an_attachment_for_a_thread_that_is_not_open_is_refused` (`NotAllowed`); the unit test that asserted the old refusal became `a_thread_attachment_is_valid_the_engine_picks_the_thread_timeline` (written together with the fix, not seen red).
- App, pure (`crates/app/src/upload.rs`, written test-first against `todo!()` stubs: all 14 failed, then passed; one test bug found, the `image` build has no BMP encoder): `mime_for(name, head)` (an extension table of passive types only; a picture type is believed only if the first bytes show the same format, and a picture under another name is not; both are sent as `application/octet-stream`; no `html`, `svg`, `js`, `exe`), `kind_for` (only png/jpeg/gif/webp are `Image`), `inspect_image` / `make_thumbnail` (format allow-list and `media::decode_limits()` applied to the header by `into_decoder`, then a second check of `w*h*4` against `MAX_ALLOC`; EXIF orientation applied; no thumbnail for a picture of at most 640x480 under 1 MiB without rotation; otherwise fit 800x600, never enlarged, PNG when the picture has alpha, JPEG quality 85 otherwise; a rotated photo always gets a thumbnail; the sizes sent are those as shown), `pasted_image_name`, `check_size` (default 100 MiB when the server has not said), `prepare_path` / `prepare_bytes` / `prepare_pixels` (open once, size and type from the handle, refuse over-limit before reading, read at most 512 bytes of a non-picture and the whole file only for a picture up to 64 MiB, so a 100 MB file is never held in memory by the app; the core reads the path itself when sending), `name_of` (last path component through `media::suggested_file_name`, so only a base name is ever sent).
- App, state: `Message::{AttachRequested, FilesChosen, FileHovered, FilesHoveredLeft, FileDropped, DropsDue, UploadPrepared, PasteRequested, Pasted, PasteFallback, UploadConfirmed, UploadCancelled}`; `App.uploads` (`UploadModel`: server limit from `MediaLimits`, hover flag, dropped paths with a sequence number for the 300 ms debounce, the `UploadDialog`, and `thread_root`, which the thread panel of Task 13 sets); `Dialogs::pick_files` and a `Clipboard` trait (`SystemClipboard` through `arboard`: `get_image` first, then `get_text`; any error or empty content is `Unavailable`, and `update` then returns `iced::clipboard::read()`, whose text arrives as `PasteFallback`). Ctrl+V and Ctrl+U in the composer are `Binding::Custom` (`composer::shortcut`; Alt makes them ordinary keys); Ctrl+U elsewhere comes from the global event listener only when no widget took the key. `update` reads no file, decodes nothing and encodes nothing: a source-scan test checks the `impl App` in `upload.rs`. Send queues one `SendAttachment` per ready file in order (not while one still loads; refused files are skipped) with the open reply target's event ID, the thread root, and the base name; a reply is used up afterwards. The confirmation is closed when the room changes or at logout; at most 20 files; late answers for a closed confirmation are ignored by entry ID. `ActionFailed { Discard }` shows "It can't be cancelled now."; `ActionFailed { Upload }` shows a fixed line by error kind; an in-progress upload (`media.upload` set) can be discarded from its echo (a plain message that is sending still cannot).
- App, screens: `screens/upload_dialog.rs` (a fixed two-deep stack layer around the media viewer layer: the confirmation card with preview, name, size and type or the refusal, the EXIF note for JPEG, **Cancel** and **Send**, or the "Drop to upload" hint, or nothing; a tag test checks the tree under it does not move), an **Attach...** button beside the editor (always present, disabled without a room), and in `timeline_pane.rs` the upload echo row (muted "Sending...", a 160 px progress bar, a percentage, **Cancel**; a layout test checks progress changes no row height). Esc closes the confirmation (a subscription active only while it is open).
- Task 9 hand-over applied: the new `blocking::run` (a std thread per job behind a 2-permit tokio semaphore; iced's executor is not a tokio runtime, so `spawn_blocking` is not available there) runs every file read, thumbnail, PNG encode and clipboard read, and now also the existing thumbnail and viewer decodes in `on_media`. This closes the Task 9 MEDIUM "up to 24 parallel decodes on the executor" for the parallelism part; the per-decode `Limits` (256 MiB) were not tightened.
- Deviations and choices (recommendation applied, none needs the human to stop): (1) a rotated photo and anything over 1 MiB gets a thumbnail even when at most 640x480, because the criteria demand a swapped-side thumbnail for the 80x60 fixture; (2) the EXIF note is shown for every JPEG (the app does not parse EXIF), worded "can carry"; (3) arboard's `wayland-data-control` feature is not enabled (it adds `wl-clipboard-rs` and more packages; AGENTS.md rule 3), so on a native Wayland session a screenshot may not be found: see the checkpoint line; (4) the pasted picture's file name is made in the worker (the clock is read there, not in `update`); (5) `UploadModel.thread_root` is set by Task 13's thread panel; nothing in the UI sets it yet (a test sets it).
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): iced_core 0.14.0 `window::Event::{FileHovered, FileDropped, FilesHoveredLeft}` `src/window/event.rs:56,66,76` (sent by iced_winit 0.14.1 `src/conversion.rs:335-341`); iced_runtime 0.14.0 `clipboard::read` `src/clipboard.rs:29`, `Task::units` `src/task.rs:282`; iced_widget 0.14.2 `ProgressBar::{new, length, girth}` `src/progress_bar.rs:79,91,97`; rfd 0.17.2 `AsyncFileDialog::pick_files` `src/file_dialog.rs:273` (returns `Option<Vec<FileHandle>>`), `set_title` :231, `set_parent` :241, `FileHandle::path` `src/file_handle/native.rs:136`; arboard 3.6.1 `Clipboard::new` `src/lib.rs:81`, `get_text` :90, `get_image` :129, `ImageData` `src/common.rs:127`, features `Cargo.toml:32-44` (`wayland-data-control` exists and is off); image 0.25.10 `ImageReader::into_decoder` `src/io/image_reader_type.rs:219`, `ImageDecoder::{orientation, set_limits}` `src/io/decoder.rs:53,115`, `DynamicImage::{from_decoder, apply_orientation, thumbnail, write_with_encoder}` `src/images/dynimage.rs:243,1161,913,1383`, `JpegEncoder::new_with_quality` `src/codecs/jpeg/encoder.rs:398`, `PngEncoder::new` `src/codecs/png.rs:609`; matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `Timeline::infer_reply` `src/timeline/mod.rs:502` and `SendAttachment` `src/timeline/futures.rs:76-93` (a thread-focused timeline adds the thread relation and reply fallback).
- TDD gaps: `blocking.rs`, the layout test for the upload row, the `prepare_path` file tests and the `event_message` test were written after their code (they passed at once); the App-level tests (27) and the `shortcut` test were seen failing first (compile error or assertion).
- Residuals / open issues: (1) discarding an upload whose request is already on the wire may be refused by the SDK; the notice appears, but this is untested against a real large upload (checkpoint). (2) `Prepared` keeps a pasted picture's PNG in memory until Send (up to 128 MiB of pixels in, and the PNG out); a path file is re-read by the core at send time, so a file that changes or grows after the confirmation is checked again by the core only against the size limit. (3) The confirmation blocks only the mouse: with the composer focused, typing continues to reach it (same as the viewer, Task 9). (4) Thumbnails are generated with `DynamicImage::thumbnail` (a fast box filter); quality is judged by eye (checkpoint). (5) The picture decode limit is still 256 MiB per image and the peak while resizing is about twice that (Task 9 Low, not tightened). (6) A GIF is sent as `image/gif` with a first-frame thumbnail. (7) The original is sent as it is: EXIF (location) stays in the file; the note says so.
- New human items (added to the Uploads checkpoint line above): the picker on each desktop, Ctrl+U, several dropped files in one confirmation, the paste caveat on native Wayland, Cancel and "can't be cancelled now", the over-limit and folder refusals, the rotated/transparent/GIF thumbnails in Element Web, the renamed-file case, the Esc caveat.

## Phase 5 / Task 11 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (718 app unit tests, 5 in `tests/timeline_layout.rs`, 12 doc tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. No ignored run: the core and the bridge are unchanged (the plan asks for it only when they change). No dependency, version or feature was changed (`notify-rust =4.18.2` and `tray-icon =0.26.1` were added by Task 2; `tokio` features unchanged).
- New app modules: `notifications.rs` (pure `should_show`, `Folding` (3 s per-room window), `build_toast`, `escape_markup`, `ModeChoice`, `muted_mark`, the `Notifier` trait with `SystemNotifier` (notify-rust on a worker thread), `NoNotifier` and the test-only `RecordingNotifier`) and `tray.rs` (pure `icon_rgba`, `tray_tooltip`, `Look`, `TrayModel`, `TrayFactory`/`TrayHandle`, `SystemTrayFactory` (tray-icon, KSNI), `NoTrayFactory`, the click/menu subscription, test-only `FakeTrayFactory`). `App::new` installs the inert `NoNotifier`/`NoTrayFactory`; only `App::booter` (used by `main`) installs the real ones, so no test can touch the desktop.
- Behaviour: a core `Notification` is shown only when desktop notifications are on and the window is **unfocused** (focused shows nothing, also for the open room; unfocused shows the open room's too, since nobody is looking at it; the plan's `selected_room` parameter is therefore not needed and is not in the signature). With "Show message content" off the preview is set to `None` in `on_notification` before anything else and `build_toast` also ignores it and the sender: bodies "New message" / "Mentioned you" / "Invited you", title = room name (a DM with content on uses the sender's name as title). Names and previews go through `text_safety::one_line_capped`; a blank display name falls back to the cleaned user ID; the body is escaped for `&`, `<`, `>` (notification servers may read markup); the event ID is not used at all. A second notification for a room within 3 s of the first replaces it (`Notification::id`, taken from `NotificationHandle::id` via `Message::NotificationShown`) with "N new messages"; if the OS ID has not arrived yet it is a new notification. A mention (a keyword match arrives as `Mention`, Task 6) also returns `window::request_user_attention(Informational)`. Clicking (the Linux "default" action, `wait_for_action` on its own thread; at most 16 waiters, extra ones are shown without a click handler) sends `OpenRoomFromNotification`, which selects the room if it is listed and not already open, un-minimises and `gain_focus`es the window.
- Room header menu: a `pick_list` (Notifications: All messages / Mentions and keywords / Mute / Use default), shown for every selected room that is not an invite. It sends `SetRoomNotificationMode` once per change and nothing when the choice is the current one. The SDK does not refresh `RoomSummary.notification_mode` after a change (Task 6), and the core sends no success event for a room mode, so the app keeps `App.room_modes` (the user's choice, capped at 64) as the first source for the menu and the list; an entry is dropped when a room-list diff makes the summary equal to it, when `ActionFailed { Notifications }` arrives for that room (a notice is shown for the open room), and at login. Room rows show a "Muted" tag for a muted room, and a muted room's unread count is an outline in the muted colour instead of the filled grey badge (mentions keep the highlight).
- Tray: created in `update` on the first `Message::WindowOpened` (from `window::open_events`) or, as a second chance, on the first `WindowFocus` message; a failure sets a flag, prints one fixed line to stderr and the app runs without a tray (fake test). Icon: 64x64 RGBA drawn in code (blue rounded square, white "T"); the badge variant adds a red dot with a white ring at 3/4 width, 1/4 height. Tooltip "Twicecat", "Twicecat – N unread", "Twicecat – 99+ unread". `UnreadTotals` calls `TrayModel::set_unread`, which touches the icon only when the variant changes and the tooltip only when its text changes (a count above 99 changes nothing); with the tray-badge setting off the tray stays plain. Menu: **Show Twicecat** (un-minimise + `gain_focus`) and **Quit** (the existing `CloseRequested` path, so the Phase 4 clipboard flush still runs); a left click on the icon (KSNI emits it) also shows the window. Events reach iced through two small threads that move `MenuEvent`/`TrayIconEvent` receiver items into a `Subscription::run` stream. Closing the window still quits (iced 0.14 has no hide-window task).
- `UiSettings` (desktop notifications, show content, tray badge) is held in `App.ui_settings` at its defaults; the local settings storage is Task 14 and the toggles are in Task 15's Settings screen, so there is no UI to change them yet (tests set the fields). The `NotificationSettings` event is stored in `App.notification_settings` for that screen.
- APIs confirmed in the registry (`~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): notify-rust 4.18.2 `Notification::{appname, summary, body, action, id}` `src/notification.rs:145,153,246,451,465`, `Notification::show` (Linux, returns `xdg::NotificationHandle`) :511, `NotificationHandle::{wait_for_action (the closure gets "default" for a click, "__closed" on close), id}` `src/xdg/mod.rs:101,320`; tray-icon 0.26.1 `TrayIconBuilder::{new, with_menu, with_icon, with_tooltip, with_title, build}` `src/lib.rs:308,327,340,377,392,460`, `TrayIcon::{set_icon, set_tooltip}` :519,:583, `TrayIconEvent::{Click, receiver}` :755,:873 (the KSNI backend emits left and middle click activation, doc at :745), `Icon::from_rgba` `src/icon.rs:142`, `tray_icon::menu` re-exports muda :162; muda 0.21.1 `Menu::{new, append_items}` `src/items/menu.rs:86,145`, `MenuItem::with_id` `src/items/normal.rs:66`, `MenuEvent::{receiver, id}` `src/menu_event.rs:34,29` with `MenuId: PartialEq<&str>` `src/menu_id.rs:35`; iced_runtime 0.14.0 `window::{open_events, oldest, minimize, request_user_attention, gain_focus}` `src/window.rs:227,289,364,415,431`, `Task::{and_then (on Option), units}` `src/task.rs:364,282`; iced_futures 0.14.0 `Subscription::run(fn() -> Stream)` `src/subscription.rs:182`; iced_widget 0.14.2 `pick_list` `src/helpers.rs:1656` and `PickList::{padding, text_size}` `src/pick_list.rs:236,242`.
- TDD: `state::tests::notification_flow` (18 tests) was written first and failed to compile (no `Message::Tray`, `RoomModeChosen`, `set_notifier`, ...); then it passed. Mutation checks: making the tooltip update unconditional failed 2 tests; not dropping the local mode on `ActionFailed` failed 1. Gaps: the pure helpers in `notifications.rs` and `tray.rs` and their tests were written in the same pass (no red run), the room-row and header widgets have only build (smoke) tests, and the real `SystemNotifier`/`SystemTrayFactory` are untested by design.
- Residuals / open issues: (1) a mode picked in Twicecat stays shown until the list changes to the same value, so a change made later in Element for the same room is hidden until the next restart/login (the SDK gives no refresh; same cause as the Task 6 note). (2) The title of a group notification is the room name even when content is off (the plan says so); a DM with content off also uses the room name, which for a DM is usually the other person's name, so hiding the sender is only as good as the room name. (3) The notification waits for a click on its own thread until the notification is closed; threads are capped at 16, beyond that no click handler. (4) No re-notify after late decryption (Task 6 residual). (5) On Windows and macOS the code is uncompiled (Q2): notifications are shown without a click handler. (6) The tray is created on the UI thread by `update`; `ksni` starts its own thread, a creation that blocks (no D-Bus session) would block the UI for as long as the call takes. (7) The Task 6 flake (`notifications::bad_keywords_and_default_modes_are_handled`) was not exercised: the core did not change and the ignored suite was not run.
- New human items (added to the checkpoint list; also for the checklist artifact): notification title/body per kind on GNOME and KDE (DM, mention, keyword, invite, encrypted), a burst of messages in one room folding into "N new messages" in place, a notification for a room muted in Element Web staying silent, the mention taskbar/dock flash, the click opening and focusing the right room on GNOME and KDE (Wayland focus-stealing rules may only flash the window), a notification text with `<b>`, `&` and a link not rendering as markup, the tray icon and dot on KDE and on GNOME with the AppIndicator extension and what plain GNOME shows, whether the tray appears at all (the first-window-event path), the tooltip count, left click, **Show Twicecat** (also when minimised) and **Quit**, the header menu and its immediate update, the muted tag and outline count in both themes.

## Phase 5 / Task 12 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (797 app unit tests, up from 718; 5 in `tests/timeline_layout.rs`), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. No ignored run: the core and the bridge are unchanged (app-only task).
- New app modules: `rooms.rs` (pure `CreateRoomForm::validate`, `check_name`/`check_topic`, `parse_invitees`, `leave_warning`, `pill_label`, `sort_members`, `role_badge`, `member_lines`, `downscale_avatar`/`read_avatar_file`; the `RoomsModel`/`RoomsMessage` state and the `App` handlers), `screens/room_dialogs.rs` (new room, direct message and join dialogs, user card, join bar, and the always-two-deep stack layer), `screens/room_settings.rs`, `screens/members.rs`. `links.rs` gains `parse_room_target`, `parse_user_id` and `valid_server_name` (the app mirror of the core's parser, same table). `Dialogs` gains `pick_image` (rfd filter for pictures); `RichEnv`/`Ctx` gain `members`.
- Behaviour:
  - **Create room** (room list button): name, topic, **Encrypted** (on by default, with a line that it cannot be turned off later), private or public with an address, invitees. **Create** sends `CreateRoom` once (the dialog goes busy until `RoomCreated` or a failure). `RoomCreated`/`RoomJoined` select the room at once when it is listed, else when a later `RoomListChanged` has it (`select_when_listed`). A user who selects another room in between is still switched when the new room appears (accepted; noted below).
  - **Direct message** (`CreateDm`, own ID refused), **Join** (alias, `!id` or `matrix.to` link through `links::parse_room_target`; it sends the parsed ID plus `via`) and **Accept**/**Decline** (buttons in the header of a selected invitation, off while the answer is on its way; only rows that are invitations) each send once. A failed answer re-enables the button and shows a fixed line (`failure_text`; the core's text is never shown).
  - **Room settings** (header button): name, topic, picture, **Enable encryption** (warning card, then confirm), **Leave** (confirmation). Each control works only when `RoomDetails.permissions` allows it; the handlers re-check, not just the buttons. The picture goes through `Dialogs::pick_image`, then `blocking::run(read_avatar_file)`: metadata first (a FIFO is never opened), at most 64 MiB, `media::decode_rgba` (format allow-list plus Limits, first GIF frame), Lanczos downscale to fit 512x512 (never up), PNG if at most 1 MiB else JPEG at 90/75/55, else once more at 256 px. Raw bytes never reach `Handle::from_bytes`. An encrypted room shows "Room pictures are stored unencrypted on the server".
  - **Leave**: `leave_warning(details, members, own)` (third argument is the plan's addition, the app must know who "you" are) warns for an encrypted room, for the last admin seat (creator or admin, no other joined admin, others stay), for the only member, and says when it could not check. **Leave** sends `LeaveRoom` once, closes the panels and deselects the app side as the core does once the job is queued. The room stays in the list until the sync says otherwise (nothing assumes Left); a refused leave shows the fixed failure line.
  - **Member list** (header button): sorted by role then name (the app re-sorts the core's list), role badge (`role_badge`: Creator/Admin/Moderator with the level when unusual, `Level n`, `Invited`), sanitised name, ID elided in the middle, 100 rows then **Show more**, avatars only for the rows shown (one `FetchAvatars` per batch). **Invite** validates the user ID in the app (`parse_user_id`) and needs `can_invite`; the core has no success event, so the line says "Invitation requested." and a failure replaces it. `truncated` shows "Showing the first 5,000 members".
  - **Pills**: `pill_label(target, members, rooms)` builds every label from the target: a known member's current display name (sanitised, 48 characters), else the full ID elided in the middle (48), a listed room's name, else the alias or ID. The sender's label is not an argument any more. The members of the open room come from `ListRoomMembers`, which the app sends once per room selection, the first time its timeline holds a user pill (or when a panel opens). A user pill opens the user card (target ID in full, on its own line; name from the member list when known; **Message** sends `CreateDm` once; no card for the own user). A room pill selects a listed room; otherwise a bar asks "Join <alias or ID>?" (**Join** sends `JoinRoom` once; Cancel).
  - Esc closes the topmost dialog, card, warning card, panel or join bar. Every overlay is a layer in a stack that is always two deep (tag test over 12 states: the base subtree keeps its tags), so the timeline scroll and the composer focus are kept. `Debug` of the new state shows lengths and flags only (sentinel test over the dialogs, panels, card, bar and messages).
- APIs confirmed in the registry: iced_widget 0.14.2 `checkbox::label` `src/checkbox.rs:145`, `on_toggle_maybe` :167, `text_input::{id :157, on_input_maybe :184, on_submit_maybe :201}`, `image::border_radius` `src/image.rs:175`, `helpers::opaque` :577, `iced::widget::operation::focus` (already used in `security.rs`); rfd 0.17.2 `AsyncFileDialog::{add_filter :202, pick_file :268}` `src/file_dialog.rs`; image 0.25.10 `DynamicImage::resize` `src/images/dynimage.rs:875`. No matrix-sdk, ruma, winit, arboard or notify-rust API is used by this task.
- TDD: `links.rs` parser tests, the pure `rooms.rs` tests, the `rooms_ui` update tests (41, in `state.rs`) and the pill tests in `rich_text.rs` were written first and seen red (`todo!()` stubs panicked, or the build failed on the missing field), then implemented. Gaps: `role_badge`, `member_lines`, the widgets (`room_dialogs`, `members`, `room_settings`) and their tag/layout/smoke tests were written together with the code; nothing checks how the widgets look.
- Deviations from the task text:
  1. **No hover tooltip on pills.** iced 0.14 `text::Span` has no tooltip (fields: size, line height, font, colour, link, highlight, padding, underline, strikethrough), so a pill cannot show its ID on hover. The full ID is on the user card and the join bar; an elided label can only be read in full there.
  2. `leave_warning` takes the own user ID as a third argument (`Option<&UserId>`).
  3. Sender profiles in the timeline are not used for pill names; only `RoomMembers` is (one request per room, lazily).
  4. Accept/Decline are in the header of the selected invitation, not on the list rows (a row is one button that selects the room).
  5. Declining does not deselect the app side (the core keeps the invited room selected until the next selection; the room leaves the list at the next sync).
- Choices to review: the "New room" dialog says "Encrypted" is on by default with a line that it cannot be turned off; **Direct message** says that an existing direct message is reused and "may not be encrypted" (the Task 7 CreateDm reuse low); the settings panel says that room pictures are unencrypted (the Task 7 low).
- Residuals / open issues: (1) a room created or joined is selected when it appears even if the user picked another room meanwhile. (2) The member list holds up to 5,000 `RoomMemberView` clones in the app (sorted again on arrival); only 100+ rows are built. (3) Avatar decode of room pictures reads the whole file (at most 64 MiB) and `into_rgba8` allocates beside `max_alloc` (Task 9 low, same path). (4) The file can change between the picker and the read (check-then-use on the path, like Task 10). (5) `ListRoomMembers` failures (`ActionKind::Members`) are ignored for pills: the pill then shows the full ID, which is the safe label. (6) Names in `SettingsPanel` fields are the raw text of the room; they are drawn in a `text_input`, which does not run `text_safety` (a bidi override in a room name can show in the field, never in a label). (7) After **Invite** the field is cleared and "Invitation requested." shows even if the server later refuses; the refusal replaces the line when it arrives.
- New human items (also on the checkpoint list; the checkpoint artifact needs the same lines): create an encrypted room and see the lock in Twicecat and the encryption shield in Element Web; create a public room with an alias and join it from Element Web; join by alias, ID and `matrix.to` link from Twicecat; accept and decline invites made in Element Web (header buttons of the selected invitation); invite someone, leave, and read the leave warnings (encrypted room, last admin, only member); room settings edits (name, topic, picture, enable encryption with its warning card) seen in Element and refused when the power level is too low; the member list of an Element-created room with several power levels (badges, order, 100 rows then Show more, avatars); pills labelled from the target (a message from Element Web whose pill text names someone else shows the real user, an unknown user shows the full ID); the user card and **Message** (and the DM reuse note); joining from a room pill; the layer look in both themes (dim backdrop, card size, long names); Esc closes each dialog and panel; the composer keeps its focus and the timeline its scroll when a dialog opens and closes.

## Phase 5 / Task 13 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked`, `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. This task also touched the core (carry-overs 1 and 2), so the ignored run was done: `docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` (results in the "Ignored runs" bullet), and the `threads` binary twice in a row with no reset (8/8 both times, 24 s and 27 s).
- Carry-overs from earlier tasks, applied as the human asked (recommended option), done before the panel relies on them:
  1. **Thread item IDs are bound to their thread (Task 8 security MEDIUM).** The thread timeline's internal ID prefix is now `thread:{root}:` (`threads::item_prefix`; `with_internal_id_prefix` takes any `String`, matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `src/timeline/builder.rs:92`, and the ID is `"{prefix}{n}"`, `controller/metadata.rs:225`), so an ID is `thread:$root:n`. `Selected::timeline_for` now sends a `thread:` ID to the open thread only when `threads::item_in_thread(id, open_root)` holds (the prefix of that root followed by a non-empty run of digits; the digit check keeps a legacy room-version-1 root like `$a:x` from claiming the items of `$a`); otherwise the answer is `ItemNotFound`, also for the old unbound form `thread:0`. `route_item` is unchanged and still table-tested (one row added). New tests: `item_prefix_binds_the_root`, `item_in_thread_table` (13 rows, including the mismatch, the old form, a missing or non-numeric counter and the prefix-root case) and the homeserver test `a_thread_item_id_of_another_thread_is_not_found` (a reaction on `thread:$other:N` and on `thread:N` both give `ItemNotFound`). The existing homeserver assertions now check the full `thread:{root}:` prefix. Red-first: the unit tests failed to compile before `item_in_thread` existed; the homeserver test was written after the code (not seen red). The Task 8 note "IDs restart at `thread:0` for each opened thread" is obsolete.
  2. **Thread pagination ends with the thread (Task 8 Low).** `OpenThread` owns a `watch::Sender<bool>`; `stop` sets it, and `paginate_task` races `paginate_backwards` against `wait_for(closed)` (a dropped sender also ends it), so a pagination in flight returns without sending `ThreadPagination` for the old root. No unit test (it needs a live timeline); covered by the homeserver suite not regressing and by the app ignoring events of any other root. The app side: `ThreadDiff` and `ThreadPagination` are applied only when room and root are the open pair (tests: another root, another room, nothing open, a straggler after a new root), and a new root starts with a fresh `TimelineView` and an empty draft.
  3. **Media cache key (Task 9).** The key stays `(room, item_id)`; thread item IDs now contain the root and are unique, so thread media cannot collide with the room's or another thread's. Test: `thread_media_opens_with_its_thread_id` (a room image `0` and a thread image whose ID differs only by prefix keep separate viewer keys and `FetchMedia` carries the thread ID).
  4. **Panel attachments (Task 10).** **Attach...** in the panel sets `UploadModel.thread_root` and opens the picker; the room's own **Attach**, a drop and a paste clear it unless a confirmation is already open (its target stays). Closing the panel, selecting another room or logging out drops a confirmation opened from the panel, so nothing is sent into a thread that is no longer open (the core would refuse it with `NotAllowed`). Tests: `attach_in_the_panel_targets_the_thread_and_the_room_attach_does_not`, `closing_the_panel_drops_its_upload_confirmation`; the send path itself (`SendAttachment.thread_root`) is the Task 10 test `send_carries_the_reply_target_and_the_thread_root` and the core's `an_attachment_sent_into_an_open_thread_lands_in_the_thread`. Known edge: if the user presses the panel's **Attach...** and then the room's before the first picker answers, the first answer goes to whichever request came last.
  5. **Spaces are a filter, not a boundary (Task 7).** A space's admins can list any room as a child (Element allows the same), so the sidebar only filters the room list and says nothing about who may join or see a room. This is written in the doc comments of `spaces.rs` and `spaces_sidebar.rs`; no UI text implies protection.
  6. **Task 6 `bad_keywords_and_default_modes_are_handled`:** passed in this task's ignored run (no flake seen).
- New app modules: `spaces.rs` (`SpacesModel`, pure `entries`, `SpacesMessage`; `App::select_space`, `App::on_spaces`), `threads.rs` (`ThreadsModel`/`ThreadPanel`/`ThreadsMessage`, pure `summary_text` and `outgoing`; the `App` handlers), `screens/spaces_sidebar.rs`, `screens/thread_panel.rs`. `timeline_pane.rs` gains `Pane` (scroll ID, scroll and measure messages, thread flag) and `view_pane`; `view_in` is `view_pane(.., Pane::MAIN)`, so every existing pane test is unchanged. `main_view.rs` gains `main_row` (always four slots: sidebar, room list, room pane, panel) and `rich_env`. `Message` gains `Spaces(SpacesMessage)` and `Threads(ThreadsMessage)`; `App` gains `spaces` and `threads`.
- Behaviour:
  - **Spaces sidebar:** **Home** plus one round button per level-0 space (avatar if fetched, else initials on a stable colour), tooltip with the full name (one line, capped), the selected space ringed. The level-1 spaces of the selected top-level space (or of the top-level space that holds the selected sub-space) show under it, indented. The core lists a space and then its sub-spaces; the app takes a level-1 space to belong to the nearest level-0 space above it (there is no parent field in `SpaceView`). Spaces deeper than level 1 are not shown. Selecting sends `SetRoomListQuery` with `space` and keeps the tab and the search; the same query again, or an unknown space, sends nothing. A space missing from a new `Spaces` list resets the filter to Home (tab and search kept). Space avatars are requested once per session. At most 200 spaces are kept. With no space the sidebar slot is empty.
  - **Thread summary row:** under a root that has `MessageDetails.thread`, a clickable line "N replies · name: preview" (`summary_text`: "1 reply" for one, name and preview one line each, capped at 24 and 60 characters, the name from `sender_label`). Its height is part of the row estimate (`THREAD_ROW_H`). The row is not drawn inside the panel.
  - **Thread panel:** a 340 px column right of the room pane: header with **x**, the thread timeline (same row renderer, virtualisation and scroll anchoring as the room's, through `Pane::THREAD`), a one-line text field with **Send** and **Attach...**. Opening sends `OpenThread` once (clicking the same summary again only focuses the field); `ThreadDiff`s go to a separate `TimelineView`; a short thread asks for older events by itself (`PaginateThread`, from the model's pagination rule, until `ReachedStart`). **Send** and Enter send `Send` with `thread_root` (markdown, no mentions, no reply; a blank text sends nothing). Reactions, delete, copy, save, open and the viewer work on panel items with their `thread:{root}:{n}` IDs (the app's item lookup searches the room's items and the panel's). **Reply** and **Edit** are not offered in the panel and are refused for thread items (they belong to the room composer). Esc (when nothing above the panel is open and no widget used the key) or **x** sends `CloseThread`; selecting a room (also the same one again) sends `CloseThread` before `SelectRoom`; deselecting or logging out resets the panel without a command (the core closes the thread itself). An `ActionFailed` of kind `Thread` shows "The thread could not be loaded." and keeps the panel.
  - The room timeline's scroll and the room composer's draft are untouched by opening and closing the panel (`update` test over the scroll model, the anchor, the draft text and the commands sent) and the widget tree keeps its shape (tag test: the pane row always has four children and the room pane subtree has the same tags with the panel closed, open and with spaces present; a smoke test builds the whole window).
- APIs confirmed (registry `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`): matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 as under carry-over 1. iced 0.14.0 / iced_widget 0.14.2: `scrollable::Viewport::{bounds, absolute_offset}` (already used by `TimelineScrolled`), `scrollable::id`/`Scrollable::id`/`on_scroll`/`anchor_bottom` (already used), `tooltip::Position::Right` `src/tooltip.rs:407,416`, `rule::vertical` `src/rule.rs:42`, `Row::from_vec` `src/row.rs:75`, `Viewport::{absolute_offset :1739, bounds :1775}` `src/scrollable.rs`, `image::border_radius` and `text_input::{id, on_input, on_submit}` (confirmed in Task 12), and iced_core 0.14.0 `event::Status::Ignored` `src/event.rs:36`. No matrix-sdk API is used by the app; the only new core API use is `with_internal_id_prefix` with a longer string.
- Not verified by the agent (all on the human checkpoint): how the sidebar, the summary row and the panel look and scroll in both themes; tooltips; the pane widths on a narrow window; Esc closing the panel while the room composer has focus; thread counts against Element Web; sending from the panel and seeing it in Element Web; attachments from the panel; that threaded replies are gone from the main timeline (the core test covers the hiding, not the picture).
- Deviations and choices:
  1. The panel's composer is a one-line `text_input`, not a second `text_editor`: no mentions, emoji suggestions, drafts or multi-line text in the panel (long or formatted text goes through the room composer; a thread reply from it is not possible yet). Recommended follow-up if the human wants it: reuse `Composer` for the panel.
  2. There is no "Reply in thread" button on a message: a thread can only be opened from the summary row of a root that already has replies. Starting a thread from a plain message needs the human's call (it is not in the Task 13 criteria).
  3. The reaction picker still floats over the room timeline's corner when opened from a panel item.
  4. The plan said "the Phase 3 diff tests are reused on it"; the panel uses the same `TimelineView` type, whose Phase 3 diff tests run unchanged, plus new tests for the routing; the diff code itself was not duplicated in new tests.
  5. TDD gaps: the `entries` table, `summary_text`, `outgoing` and the `update` tests were written together with the code in one pass (not seen red, except the two test-side failures noted below); the widgets (`spaces_sidebar`, `thread_panel`) have only the tag and smoke tests. The first run of two `update` tests failed on my own expectations (a short thread's `PaginateThread` came before the command being checked), fixed in the tests.
- Residuals / open issues: (1) a late `ThreadPagination` of the same root after the pagination permit was dropped is harmless; (2) `item_in_thread` assumes the SDK's IDs are `{prefix}{counter}` (pinned 0.19.1; a change there would make every thread item `ItemNotFound`, which the homeserver tests would catch); (3) with Esc, a widget that leaves the key `Ignored` (the panel's own text field) lets Esc close the panel; (4) the panel keeps all items of the thread in memory (only the built window is drawn).
- Ignored runs: the unit counts of the final gate are 830 app and 402 core tests. (1) First full ignored run (after the core change, while I was also compiling and running clippy and tests): all green, **927 s wall** (15 min 28 s, including compile; the test binaries alone sum to 851 s), `bad_keywords_and_default_modes_are_handled` passed. (2) A second full run on a clean machine after `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`: **816 s wall** (13 min 36 s); `timeline_actions::reaction_toggles_on_and_off_for_both_sides` timed out once ("timed out waiting for the timeline") and cargo stopped there; the binary passed on the next run (12/12); then `timeline_items` and `timeline_live` ran: `timeline_live::a_read_receipt_reaches_the_sender` timed out twice in a row with carol holding about 40 rooms, and passed (4/4) right after `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`. I take it as the known "accounts accumulate rooms" effect (Phase 5 Task 1 note: prune before each run), not flakiness of the test or a regression of this task (the core change touches only `thread:` IDs and thread pagination); it is reported here and not loosened. The `twicecat-app` ignored tests passed (3). The agent did not run the real-keychain test.
- Rule-8 note: the plan's stop condition "the ignored suite passes 15 minutes" was met by the first run (927 s) and not by the clean run (816 s). The first run shared the CPU with my builds; the suite is also growing (Task 8: 671 s, Task 13: 816 s clean). Applied the recommendation (keep going) and left it for the human: the suite is close to the limit, so consider running the e2ee binaries in parallel or raising the limit.
- Layout fix (found by the human at the checkpoint, 2026-10-08): `main_view::main_row` builds the panes row with `Row::from_vec`, which does not look at its children (iced_widget 0.14.2 `src/row.rs:68-87`, width and height start at `Shrink`), and only set `.height(Fill)`: the row shrank to its content, so the room pane lost the free width and the whole window looked broken. It now sets `.width(Fill)` too, like `main_column`. Test-first: `the_panes_fill_the_window_with_and_without_a_thread` lays the row out headless in a 1000 x 700 window; before the fix the row was 820 px wide with the room pane at 456 px; after it the row fills 1000 px, the closed panel takes 0 px, and an open panel takes its width from the room pane only.
- New human items (also on the checkpoint list; the checkpoint artifact needs the same lines, see the end of this block): the spaces sidebar with spaces made in Element Web (icons, tooltips, filtering, Home, sub-spaces under the selected space, the tab and search kept when switching); thread summaries match Element Web's counts; the thread panel opens, scrolls and sends, and Element Web shows the reply in the thread; threaded replies no longer appear in the main timeline; opening and closing the panel does not move the room timeline or lose the room draft; attach from the panel lands in the thread; Esc and x close the panel; selecting another room closes it; reactions and delete on a panel item.

## Phase 5 / Task 14 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app 831, core 407 unit tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh` (exit 0; `paths` stays SDK-free), `cargo deny check`. Ignored run (`docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, then the plan's command; `docker compose stop` after): the first run stopped at `notifications::bad_keywords_and_default_modes_are_handled` (left `MentionsAndKeywords`, right `AllMessages` at `notifications.rs:397`), 532 s wall; the binary passed 7/7 on the next run, and a full `--no-fail-fast` run after a prune passed every binary (12 min 35 s wall). Not flaky twice in a row, so no rule-8 stop; it is the same test Task 13 noted as passing, and this task does not touch notifications in the core (only `paths` and serde derives on two api types). The agent did not run the real-keychain test.
- New: `DataPaths::{settings_file, read_settings, write_settings}` in `crates/core/src/paths.rs`. `settings.json` holds the typed `api::UiSettings` (theme, three booleans; no secrets, no content). `write_settings` writes `settings.json.tmp` (0o600 on Unix, `sync_all`) in the data root (created 0o700) and renames it over `settings.json`; the temp file is removed on error. `read_settings` returns the defaults for a missing or unreadable file; a file that does not parse is renamed `settings.json.bad` and the defaults apply. `UiSettings` and `ThemeChoice` derive `serde::Deserialize` (`#[serde(default)]` on `UiSettings`), so unknown fields are ignored and missing ones take their default.
- Test-first: the `paths` tests (missing file, atomic round trip with no temp file left, corrupt file set aside, unknown/missing fields, 0o600 mode) and the updated data-root test were written first and failed to compile (no methods); they passed after the code. The data-root test now expects exactly `["last_account", "sessions", "settings.json"]`; seen red by adding a stray file (`["last_account", "sessions", "settings.json", "stray"]` failed the assertion), then removed.
- Deviation, on purpose: `UiSettings` does not derive `Serialize`, and `write_settings` builds the JSON with `serde_json::json!`. `session::tests::to_bytes_is_the_only_serializer_and_its_errors_carry_no_secret` fails any file other than `session.rs` that contains `Serialize` or `serde_json::to_` (it caught my first version); I kept that guard intact.
- Wiring (so the stored value reaches Task 11's `on_notification`): `main.rs` calls `data.read_settings()` before the core starts and passes it to `App::booter(startup, ui_settings)`, which calls the new `App::set_ui_settings`. Test: `settings_stored_on_disk_reach_the_notification_path` (show_message_content false gives the "New message" body, desktop_notifications false shows nothing). Task 15 owns the UI and the write on change; nothing writes the file yet, so until then the file only exists if made by hand.
- APIs: only `std` and `serde_json::{from_slice, json!, Value::to_string}` (already core dependencies; no package, feature or version changed). No matrix-sdk, iced or other SDK API used.
- Open issues: none. New human items: none (file handling is unit-tested; the theme override surviving a restart is already on the Task 15 list). The checkpoint artifact needs no new item.

## Phase 5 / Task 15 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app lib 861, core lib 411), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. The ignored homeserver suite was not run: the only core file changed is `paths.rs` (no SDK code, no API change).
- New app code: `crates/app/src/settings.rs` (model, `SettingsMessage`, handlers, `SHORTCUTS`, `SettingsStore` with `DiskSettings` and a test fake, pure checks `check_display_name` and `check_keyword`) and `crates/app/src/screens/settings.rs` (the card, a stack-of-two layer under the viewer and dialogs). `Message::Settings`; `App.settings_ui`, `App.settings_store`; `App::current_theme()`; `TrayModel::refresh`; `theme::{resolve_mode, theme_for_choice}`. `App::booter` takes a third argument, the store (`main.rs` passes `DiskSettings::new(data.clone())`). `theme_for(mode)` stays as the system-only helper and `theme_for_choice(system, choice)` is the one that honours the override (the plan says `theme_for`; a new name kept the 12 existing call sites and the layout integration test unchanged). All windows, including the login screen, use `App::theme`, which reads the stored choice from the first frame.
- Opens from a **Settings** button in the status bar (the account area) and Ctrl+, (Cmd+, on macOS; `settings_shortcut` in the global key listener). It refuses to open outside the Main screen or over Sessions, the verification dialog, the upload dialog, the viewer, the security screens and the room dialogs. Opening moves keyboard focus to the display name field (off the composer under the card). Esc closes it (a listener exists only while it is open) and the thread panel's Esc listener stays off meanwhile. **Manage sessions** closes Settings and opens the existing Sessions page.
- Sections as Q17: Profile (name field + Save -> `SetDisplayName`, **Change picture** -> `Dialogs::pick_image` -> `blocking::run(read_avatar_file)` (the Task 12 path, 512 px downscale, 1 MiB cap) -> `SetAvatar`, **Remove picture** -> `RemoveAvatar` only when a picture is set); Notifications (group and DM default-mode menus -> `SetDefaultNotificationMode`, keyword list with Add/Remove -> `AddKeyword`/`RemoveKeyword`, and three checkboxes); Appearance (radio System/Light/Dark); Keyboard shortcuts (the `SHORTCUTS` table, "On macOS, Ctrl is Cmd.", and the Wayland drag-and-drop note); Push-to-talk placeholder line; Sessions. The profile buttons are off while a profile command is unanswered (`profile_pending`, cleared by `OwnProfile` or `ActionFailed(Profile)`), which is what makes each press send once. Display name: trimmed, 1-255 characters, no control or bidi characters (the core's rule), unchanged names send nothing. Keywords: trimmed, blank does nothing, over 64 characters or a control character is refused with a fixed line (the field keeps its text), a keyword already listed sends nothing, 50 at most (the core's rules).
- Flake carry-over (late push-rules echo): the menus show the user's pick (`PendingMode`) until a `NotificationSettings` event agrees with it; events that disagree count as late echoes for up to `MAX_STALE_EVENTS` (3) events, after which the core's value is believed again; `ActionFailed(Notifications)` with an empty room ID (the account-wide commands) clears the picks and shows a fixed line. Tests: the first and second stale events do not undo the pick, the matching one settles it, a never-confirmed pick expires, a failure restores the reported value.
- Local settings (Task 14 carry-over): each toggle/theme change updates `App.ui_settings` at once and hands the new `UiSettings` to the store in a task; a change to the same value writes nothing. `DiskSettings` serialises its writes (blocking threads can overtake each other: every job writes the newest settings under one lock), and a failed write shows "The settings could not be saved on this device." Live effect without a restart: Task 11's `on_notification`, preview code and tray read `ui_settings` on every event (desktop notifications off -> no `Notifier` call; show content off -> "New message"/"Mentioned you"/"Invited you" with no sender or preview, tested for both directions); the tray badge toggle calls `TrayModel::refresh`, which redraws only what differs.
- Task 14 security Lows, fixed in `crates/core/src/paths.rs` (tests first, seen red): `write_settings` removes any leftover temp file first (so it never writes through a stale file or symlink), creates it 0o600 and sets 0o600 on the open handle like `write_last_account` (a stale 0o666 temp file test failed with mode 0o666 before); `read_settings` opens the file, requires a regular file (`File::metadata().is_file()`) and reads at most 64 KiB (`MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES`, read through `take(max + 1)`); an oversized file gives the defaults and is left in place (not renamed). A directory at the path gives the defaults. A round-trip test covers System/Light/Dark crossed with all eight boolean combinations. The session guard test is untouched.
- Test-first record: the `paths` tests and the `theme_for_choice` test were written and seen red before the code. The settings handlers were written before their tests (TDD gap, accepted: they are thin and the tests pin them); I then checked the key tests bite by mutation: letting the first stale event settle the mode, removing the tray refresh, each made a test fail, and the originals were restored. No test exercises `DiskSettings` itself (it needs the blocking pool; the file layer under it is the tested `paths`).
- APIs confirmed in the pinned sources: `iced_core-0.14.0` `keyboard::Event::KeyPressed { modified_key, modifiers, .. }` (`keyboard/event.rs:14-35`) and `Modifiers::command()` (`keyboard/modifiers.rs:80`); `iced_widget-0.14.2` `helpers::checkbox` (`helpers.rs:1281`), `Checkbox::label`/`on_toggle` (`checkbox.rs:145`, `:155`), `helpers::radio` and `Radio::new` (`helpers.rs:1351`, `radio.rs:172`), `helpers::pick_list` (`helpers.rs:1656`), `PickList::placeholder` (`pick_list.rs:218`), `TextInput::id`/`on_input`/`on_submit`/`on_submit_maybe` (`text_input.rs:157`, `:172`, `:194`, `:201`); `iced_runtime-0.14.0` `widget::operation::focus` (`widget/operation.rs:65`). Already used by earlier tasks and not re-read: `Dialogs::pick_image`, `blocking::run`, `read_avatar_file`. No matrix-sdk, no new package, feature or version.
- Not done, by scope: the screen shows "Picture: set/none" as text, not a preview of the picture; no `Command` reads the profile (the core pushes `OwnProfile` after login and on change). The shortcut table lists only the shortcuts that exist now (Ctrl+, Ctrl+U Enter Shift+Enter Esc); Task 16 adds its rows and moves the matching into one place.
- Open issues: none. New human items (on the Phase 5 checkpoint list; the checkpoint artifact needs the same lines, which this agent cannot edit): the Settings card layout, the button in the status bar and Ctrl+, (also on a Wayland session); focus lands in the display name field and the composer stays untouched after closing; Esc closes it; default modes and keywords round-trip with Element Web (the menus must not flicker back to the old value after a change); the three toggles take effect at once (notification with the window unfocused, preview text off, tray dot) and the theme choice applies at once and again on the login screen after a restart; the picture dialog, the 512 px downscale and **Remove picture** (seen in Element Web); the shortcut list wording and the Wayland note; **Manage sessions** leading to the Sessions page.

## Phase 5 / Task 16 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 905, core lib 411), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. The ignored homeserver suite was not run: no core file changed.
- New code: `crates/app/src/keys.rs` (the one `SHORTCUTS` table of `Binding { keys, action, bound }` rows, pure `shortcut_for(key, modifiers, platform)`, `accepts(shortcut, Context)`, `composer_takes`, `event_message`, and the `App` handlers `on_shortcut`, `on_escape`, `any_overlay_open`, `room_after`), `crates/app/src/switcher.rs` (pure `rank_rooms`, `fold`, `SwitcherModel`, `SwitcherMessage`, the `App` handlers) and `crates/app/src/screens/switcher.rs` (the card, a stack-of-two layer under the verification layer). `Message::Shortcut { shortcut, captured }`, `Message::Switcher`, `App.switcher`, `App.escape_spent`.
- Table drives the key handling (Task 15 carry-over): `settings::SHORTCUTS`, `settings::Shortcut` and `settings_shortcut` are gone; the Settings list reads `keys::SHORTCUTS`; `upload::event_message` is now window file events only (no status argument, no Ctrl+U); the composer's Ctrl+U moved into `composer::shortcut`, which asks the table (Ctrl+V paste stays the composer's, it is not a shortcut of the table). The five per-overlay Esc subscriptions (`upload_keys`, `rooms_keys`, `settings_keys`, `thread_keys`, `viewer_keys`) are replaced by the single window listener plus `App::on_escape`, which closes one layer per press (switcher, upload confirmation, viewer, Settings, room dialogs/panels, composer suggestions/reply/edit, thread panel).
- `rank_rooms(query, rooms)`: prefix, then word start, then substring; list order within a tier; cap 20; spaces left out; blank query lists the first rooms. Deviation: the plan says "the same normalisation as the composer search", but the composer search (emoji picker, member search) only lowercases and has no accent folding, so `switcher::fold` lowercases and folds Latin-1 and Latin Extended-A accents by a table (plus ß, æ, œ and dropped combining marks). No normalisation crate is a dependency and none was added. The core's room search is diacritic-insensitive, so the two agree. With fewer than 3 local matches the switcher also sends `SetRoomListQuery` (tab All, no space), remembers the previous query and puts it back on close; results are merged with the rooms the list had when the switcher opened (`seed`), so a core search that shrinks the sidebar list does not remove them.
- Composer Esc: the composer's binding and the window listener both see one Esc. `ComposerKey::Escape` sets `escape_spent` when it had work (suggestions, reply, edit) and the listener's copy (captured) is then skipped, so one press does one thing. This also fixes the Task 10 note that Esc could close the upload confirmation and be taken by the composer. The order of the two messages is not guaranteed by iced; if the listener's copy arrives first, an Esc with composer work and an open thread panel would do both. This is an unverified edge; it is on the human list.
- `navigation.rs` from the interrupted draft was not kept as a file: it is not in the plan's file list, and its content (the `App` handlers and `room_after`) belongs with the table in `keys.rs`. The draft's `keys.rs` and `switcher.rs` were reused after review; `state.rs` wiring, every `update` test, the composer change and the removal of the old listeners are new. The draft was incomplete (it had no `on_shortcut`/`on_switcher` wiring, no view, no subscription change). Tests first and seen red: `keys` (stubbed `shortcut_for`: 5 of 7 failed), `switcher` (stubbed `rank_rooms`: 6 of 7 failed), the `keyboard` update tests (20 of 25 failed before the handlers), the composer binding test and the Esc double-take test. The tag test for the switcher layer passes by construction (it was written with the layer, like the other overlay tag tests).
- Tab and Shift+Tab: Esc/Tab/arrows are matched from the table, `accepts` decides by where the key was pressed (captured by a widget, switcher open, composer suggestions open); Tab runs `operation::focus_next`/`focus_previous` only when nothing used it. Tests check the task is one operation or none. The focus order is the widget tree's; nothing sets it by hand.
- `docs/accessibility.md` written: the keyboard map, the screen-reader gap (a search of the pinned iced 0.14.0 sources finds no `accesskit`), the Wayland drag-and-drop gap and the high-contrast notes (the 4.5:1 palette tests). `room_list.rs` was not changed (no arrow-key list navigation beyond Alt+Up/Down room stepping and the switcher).
- APIs confirmed in the pinned sources: `iced_runtime-0.14.0` `widget::operation::{focus, focus_next, focus_previous}` (`widget/operation.rs:50`, `:55`, `:65`) and `Task::units` (`task.rs:282`); `iced_widget-0.14.2` `text_editor` custom binding publishes the message and captures the key (`text_editor.rs:884`, `:899`), `text_input` captures Esc and unfocuses (`text_input.rs:1235-1243`); `iced_core-0.14.0` `keyboard::Modifiers::{control, logo, alt, shift, command}` and `Key::as_ref`. Already used earlier and not re-read: `text_input`, `stack`, `scrollable`, `button`. No matrix-sdk, no new package, feature or version.
- Open issues: none. New human items (also on the checkpoint list; the checkpoint artifact needs the same lines, which this agent cannot edit): the switcher card in both themes (field focused on open, highlight visible, long and bidi room names, "No room matches."); Ctrl+K from the composer, from the timeline and from Settings (does nothing under an overlay); Up/Down/Enter/Esc and clicking a row; typing a name only the server knows (the core search merge) and the list returning to normal on close; Alt+Up/Down and Ctrl+Shift+Up/Down (unread) with the composer focused and not; Tab and Shift+Tab order across the main view and that the editor's Tab still indents/picks; Esc with reply/edit active and the thread panel open (one thing per press); focus visible on every control in both themes; the IME item below.

## Phase 5 / Task 17 notes (wrap-up)
- Agent work for Phase 5 is done (Tasks 1 to 17). The human checkpoint below stays open and nothing on it is ticked by the agent (rule 9: rendering, dialogs, clipboard, notifications, tray, drag and drop, Wayland behaviour, keyboard-only use and IME).
- Redaction sweep (tests first, seen red): `state::tests::redaction_sweep` builds an `App` in each state the plan names, with one sentinel per kind of text, and asserts none appears in `format!("{app:?}")` or in the `Debug` of the messages that carry them: the media viewer and cache (file name, save path, decoded pictures), the upload confirmation with a pasted image and a chosen file (name, bytes, pixels), a pending notification (room name, sender name, preview; also the `Request` handed to the notifier), room settings with a topic and the member list, the thread panel with a draft, the Settings screen with keywords and a display name, and the switcher with a query. Red run: the upload test failed on a dropped file waiting for its debounce (the derived `Debug` of `UploadModel` printed the dropped paths: the Task 10 Low, fixed here by a hand-written `Debug` that shows the count), the switcher test failed (`SwitcherModel` and `SwitcherMessage::Input` printed the typed query and the room names) and a test that the app `Debug` lists `settings_ui` and `switcher` failed. Fix: hand-written `Debug` for `SwitcherModel` (counts only) and `SwitcherMessage` (character count for `Input`), and `App`'s `Debug` now lists `settings_ui`, `switcher` and `notification_settings` (so the sweep covers them through the app itself; before, the Settings and switcher state were not part of `format!("{app:?}")`). The other seven states already passed. The sweep is a tripwire for these states, not a proof for every model.
- `no_secret_logging.rs`: `FORBIDDEN` gains 19 names (23 to 42): `MediaBytes`, `MediaModel`, `UploadModel`, `SavePath`, `Toast`, `NotificationView`, `SettingsModel`, `SwitcherModel`, `.preview`, `.sender_name`, `.room_name`, `.filename`, `.keywords`, `.keyword_input`, `.switcher`, `.settings_ui`, `.viewer`, `.dropped`, `.draft`. The scanner self-test got a planted case for each family and was seen red before the list grew. `UploadPaths` was tried and left out: it matches the literal in its own redacted `Debug` impl. The scan finds no production print, log or assert using any of them.
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app lib 913, core lib 411), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok).
- Full ignored suite (`docker compose up -d --wait`, `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`, then `cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`; the real-keychain test was not run), then `docker compose stop`: 111 tests, all passed in both runs. Run 1 (after `docker compose up -d --wait` and a prune that left 2 alice rooms): **675 s** (11 min 15 s). Run 2 (after `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` again, no account reset, as in the Phase 4 repeat recipe): **675 s** (11 min 15 s). Both are under the 15-minute limit; no test failed in either run, so the Task 6 flake was not seen. Run 1 overlapped a short app-only unit-test build; the app change in this task (the `Debug` impls) touches nothing the ignored suite exercises.
- No matrix-sdk, iced, winit or other external API was newly used in this task (only `std::fmt` and the existing test helpers), so there is nothing new to confirm in the registry. No dependency, feature or version changed.
- Stash entries `stash@{0}` (task16-interrupted-wip), `stash@{1}` (task6-wip) and `stash@{2}` (an older Phase 4 task 6 draft) are still kept, not applied; the human can drop them.

## Phase 5 open issues (summary)
Every Low or Medium review finding from the task reviews (`docs/plans/phase5-media-notifications.progress.md`), by area, plus the scope cuts and residuals the plan asked for. The ledger holds the full text. Status is as of the end of Task 17: "fixed" names the task that closed it; everything else is open and needs the human or a later phase. There were no High findings left open (the one High, in Task 7, was fixed in 3252ba7).

### Scope cuts (Q21), gaps and untested platforms
- **Out of Phase 5 (Q21):** animated GIFs (first frame only) and inline video or audio playback; captions on send, image editing, galleries (MSC4274), stickers (placeholders), voice messages, location sending; link previews, blurhash; "close to tray"; editing power levels, kick and ban, room upgrades, room directory, knocking; creating or editing spaces; thread list, thread subscriptions, threaded read receipts; shortcut rebinding; push without a running client (pushers) and notification sounds; i18n.
- **Q4, no drag and drop on a native Wayland window:** the file picker and clipboard paste are the Wayland paths; the gap is in the Settings shortcut list and `docs/accessibility.md`. Drag and drop is checked on X11 only.
- **Q2, Windows and macOS code is uncompiled and untested:** the checks are Linux only (Wayland and X11), no CI gate; pushing the branch for a Windows/macOS CI build was not required. The tray, notification and dialog code for those systems has never been built.
- **Screen readers:** none (iced 0.14 has no accesskit); documented in `docs/accessibility.md`.
- **Scope deviations the human decides at the checkpoint:** thread panel has a one-line composer, no "reply in thread" on a plain message, no Reply/Edit on panel items, and its reaction picker floats over the room timeline (Task 13, item p5-thread-actions); pills have no ID tooltip because iced 0.14 `text::Span` has no tooltip field (Task 12, item p5-pills); no picture preview in Settings; the shortcut table lists only shortcuts that exist.

### Q20 residuals (also in the security notes)
EXIF in sent originals (Q9); files the user saves are in clear; notification text goes to the OS notification service; the clipboard image read is not zeroized; a lying `info.size` (Q7, see Task 4 Medium); no re-notify after late decryption (Q10).

### Medium findings still open
- **Task 4, size limits trusted the sender's declared `info.size` (fixed, see the `info.size` fix note under "Phase 5 security notes")** and apply only after the SDK has read the whole body (no timeout, plus a decrypted copy and a cache clone). An encrypted "image" declaring a small size with no thumbnail is fetched whole as its own thumbnail, so automatic thumbnail fetches can hold about 2 to 3 times the server's media limit each, 4 at a time. Options: hard cap from the server's upload limit, no full-file previews of others' media without a click, a streaming size-limited fetcher, `use_cache = false` for thumbnails. Item for the human.
- **Task 9, `Open` uses a deny-list of dangerous extensions** that misses code-running or link types (url, scf, cpl, msc, chm, hlp, inf, ws, wsh, psm1, pyw, pyz, mjs, xll, application, appref-ms, settingcontent-ms, library-ms, search-ms, jnlp, iso, img, vhd(x), mht(ml), htm(l), svg; on macOS terminal, tool, workflow, scpt, applescript, fileloc, webloc, inetloc, mpkg, prefpane; on Linux flatpakref, flatpakrepo). The auditor recommends an allow-list of passive types; that changes the accepted Q8, so the human decides (checkpoint item p5-open-policy). **Decided by the human, 2026-10-08:** any saved file may be opened after the confirmation; the deny-list became `may_run_code`, a warn-list with all the types above, and the bar warns for them.
- Fixed Mediums (for the record; also the Task 10 Low `UploadModel` `Debug` printing dropped paths, fixed in Task 17): Task 8 `thread:N` item IDs restart per thread (fixed in Task 13: `thread:{root}:{n}`, checked in `Selected::timeline_for`); Task 9 parallel image decodes on the executor (fixed for thumbnail and viewer decodes by the 2-permit blocking pool in Task 10; the tighter thumbnail `Limits` suggested there was not done).

### Low findings still open
Core:
- **Task 1:** raw login test helpers never log out (devices and tokens pile up on the test accounts); `reset_notification_baseline` deletes every user-defined room rule on the account (local test accounts only); `send_attachment_raw` has no guard for encrypted rooms (the SDK fetches encryption state, so it works); the invite loop in `create_room_raw` could be shared.
- **Task 3:** `Command::JoinRoom` `Debug` prints the target (alias or matrix.to link) unredacted and, per Task 7, `via` too; the CLI media failure line prints error text unsanitized (as the older lines do); `phase5_commands()` is a hand-built test list (the compiler does not check completeness); review warning that `PaginateThread` overlaps the plan's `thread:` routing (resolved by using `PaginateThread`, recorded in the Task 8 notes).
- **Task 4:** `SaveMedia` accepts relative paths and overwrites silently (the path comes from the user's dialog; refuse non-absolute paths, and the app must never build it from `MediaContent::filename`); `log_hygiene` `raw_login(1)` client is not logged out; "sticker stays a placeholder" has no direct test; the fetch deadline starts after the permit wait; `parts()` duplication.
- **Task 5:** `Source::Path` is read check-then-use and uncapped (open once, check the handle, `take(max + 1)`); `Bytes` are copied before `check_size` and not size-checked before queuing; the filename is sent as given (reduce to `Path::file_name` with no control characters in the core); a joined but unselected room gives `NoRoomSelected` (document on `SendAttachment`); a whole file is held in memory (about 2 to 3 times, up to 100 MiB by default); `send_attachment` failures map to a fixed `Internal` string; discarding an already-started upload is untested (the SDK may refuse); the discard test is timing-dependent; `CoreConfig.max_upload_bytes_override` is test-only and should be marked so.
- **Task 6:** the notification handler is awaited inside sync and can wait up to 5 s per event when the app's event channel is full, so a burst can stall sync (bounded buffer plus `try_send`, or about 250 ms); keywords become server-side push-rule globs (`*`, `?`) while the core matches them literally (reject glob metacharacters in `validate_keyword`); the invite dedupe key `invite:{room_id}` drops a re-invite while it stays in the 512-entry ring; `is_muted` uses `active_members_count() == 2` and `is_dm` uses `is_direct()`, which can disagree; `RemoveKeyword` validates as strictly as add, so a stored over-long keyword cannot be removed; account-wide setting failures report an empty `RoomId`; `RoomSummary.notification_mode` does not refresh on a cached-mode-only change (the app keeps a local override, so a change made later in Element shows after the next login); `tokio` `test-util` is enabled as a core dev-dependency (no new package; flagged under rule 3).
- **Task 7:** `CreateDm` can reuse a peer-made `is_direct` room that is unencrypted or has a third member (reuse only encrypted rooms with at most 2 active members); a space's admins can list any room as a child and it shows under that space (local view only, as in Element); a leave refused by the server after queuing still closes the timeline; room and own avatars in encrypted rooms are uploaded in plaintext (Matrix design); the app must not assume `Left` right after `LeaveRoom`; `require()` leaves the decision to the server when power levels are unavailable; `rooms.rs` is about 1000 lines (split the job execution); share an `ActionFailed` builder between `room_job` and `open_room_details`.
- **Task 8:** a running `paginate_task` outlives `CloseThread` (partly fixed in Task 13: it now cancels through a watch signal, without a unit test); `OpenThread` on a non-root event gives a one-event timeline (document); comment the `unwrap_or_default()` in `paginate_thread` and the `{prefix}{n}` SDK ID-format dependency by the version pin.
- **Task 14:** a corrupt settings file silently reverts a privacy choice (content off becomes on); a second corrupt file overwrites `settings.json.bad`; an unreadable file is later overwritten by `write_settings`. (The temp-file permission and the FIFO or huge-file Lows were fixed in Task 15.)
- **Task 15:** `write_settings` removes then opens `settings.json.tmp`, leaving a same-UID symlink window (open with `create_new(true)`); `read_capped_regular_file` opens before checking the type, so a FIFO hangs startup and symlinks are followed (check `symlink_metadata` first; `O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK` would need `libc`, a new direct dependency).
App:
- **Task 9:** `into_rgba8`, the EXIF turn and the thumbnail resize allocate copies outside `max_alloc` (peak about 2 to 3 times; `width * height * 4` is checked against it first); `Open` acts on the remembered path, which a local process could swap (check `symlink_metadata` and the length before opening); the viewer overlay blocks only the mouse, so the composer may keep keyboard focus (consider unfocusing on `MediaClicked`); the thumbnail `Limits` stay at 256 MiB; `MediaCache` eviction is O(n).
- **Task 10:** `prepare_path` opens before checking `is_file`, so a FIFO blocks a blocking-pool permit (two stall all decodes until restart); the core re-reads `Source::Path` at Send, so the file can change between preview and send; JPEG EXIF/GPS goes as-is (the dialog warns); `arboard` decodes clipboard images with default limits before the 128 MiB check; the size check can go stale for path sources; a pasted picture holds its PNG (up to about 128 MiB) until Send; arboard is built without `wayland-data-control` (item p5-wayland-paste, needs a new package).
- **Task 11:** notifications already on screen keep previews and sender names after logout or turning content off (close them on logout via the kept OS IDs); a DM toast title shows only the sender's display name (impersonation, for example "IT Support"; add the Matrix ID or keep the room name); only the body is markup-escaped, not the title; a stale fold ID can be reused after dismissal; folding is keyed on room only, so a mention or invite followed within 3 s by another message becomes "N new messages"; tray creation can block the UI thread without a D-Bus session; the global `WAITING` atomic could move into `SystemNotifier`.
- **Task 12:** the room settings panel fills name and topic with server text uncleaned (bidi or control spoofing inside the field; saving is validated); the "Join ...?" bar hides the sender-chosen `via` servers (show the first host); `on_rooms_failed` routes an `ActionFailed { Rooms }` to whichever panel is open, not the failing room; a created or joined room is selected when it appears even if the user picked another room meanwhile; avatar decode reads up to 64 MiB off-thread (bounded).
- **Task 13:** `Esc` may need two presses when a text field in the panel is focused (iced 0.14.2 `text_input` captures Escape; the Task 16 `on_escape` handles the captured case, and the human counts presses, item p5-threads); a transient empty `Spaces` event resets a selected space to Home; the Phase 3 diff tests are not re-run on a second `TimelineView` instance.
- **Task 15:** `save_display_name` clears `name_edited` before the send, so a failed save can be overwritten by a later profile event (set it back in `on_profile_failed`); the own server display name enters the field unfiltered (re-checked before sending).
- **Task 16:** `any_overlay_open()` ignores the verification dialog, so Alt/Ctrl+Shift arrows, Ctrl+K and Ctrl+U act on the room behind it (add verification, and guard `Shortcut::Attach`); `switcher_choose` re-adds a seed room that may have been left, kicked or banned since opening (re-add only while `restore.is_some()`); `switcher::fold` (Latin-1 and Latin Extended-A accent folding) differs from the composer search, which only lowercases, so the two searches treat accents differently, and non-Latin scripts are only lowercased; `escape_spent` relies on iced message order (if the listener's copy arrives first, one Esc with a pending reply and an open thread panel does both; human item); `switcher_step` `isize` casts; the composer-focused test checks `accepts()` with a captured flag, not a real widget.

Watch for a second consecutive failure of any of them.

The suite grows with every core task; the margin to 15 minutes is small, so prune the test rooms before a run.
