## Phase 5 security notes
Written by the agent from the code, the tests and the task audits; the human accepts or rejects the residuals (last item of the checkpoint).
- **Media at rest.** The app keeps decoded pictures in memory only (`MediaModel`, capped, emptied by `media.clear()` on logout). Full files fetched with `use_cache = true` are cached in the SDK media store, which encrypts values at rest. `log_hygiene` sends and fetches an encrypted image whose bytes contain a sentinel; neither the log capture nor the scan of every file under the data root finds it (the scan covers the literal values, not base64 or URL-encoded forms). Encryption keys of media (`EncryptedFile`) never cross the API. Images are decoded with an explicit format allow-list (PNG, JPEG, WebP, GIF first frame) and limits (16384 px a side, 256 MiB allocation), on a 2-permit blocking pool; remote bytes are never given to `Handle::from_bytes`. Open Medium: a lying `info.size` can make the SDK buffer more than the declared size (see the Medium list).
- **Notification content.** The core always sends the preview in the `Notification` event; the app drops the preview and the sender when "Show message content" is off. The text handed to the desktop is plain (the body is escaped for markup) and lives in the OS notification service, where other apps and a history may keep it; turning content off avoids this. Notifications already on screen survive logout and a settings change (Low). Keywords are stored on the server as push rules (visible to the homeserver). Previews, names and keywords are kept out of every `Debug` string of the app (the Task 17 sweep) and out of print, log and assert macros (`no_secret_logging.rs`). No re-notify after a late decryption (Q10).
- **Saved files.** Saving writes the decrypted file to the path the user chose in the native dialog, in clear, by the user's choice. The suggested name is reduced to one path component (`suggested_file_name`) and the app only sends absolute paths from the dialog; the core never builds a path from the sender's file name, but its `SaveMedia` itself accepts relative paths and overwrites an existing file (Low). `Open` appears only after saving and asks first; it uses a deny-list of dangerous extensions that is incomplete (Medium, human decision), and it acts on the remembered path (a local process could swap it).
- **EXIF and uploads.** The original of a sent picture goes unchanged, so JPEG EXIF (camera model, GPS position) reaches the room; the confirmation shows a note about it. A pasted screenshot is encoded to PNG in memory (the clipboard read is not zeroized). A picked file is read by the core at Send, so it can change between the preview and the send. File names are reduced to a base name in the app; a file over the server's limit is refused with the limit shown.
- **Accepted by plan and still open:** EXIF in originals, saved files in clear, notification text in the OS, the clipboard image not zeroized, no re-notify after late decryption.
- **`info.size` fix (2026-10-11), lands as a fixup of the Task 4 commit.** `media_fetch::CappedMediaFetcher` replaces the SDK's `DefaultMediaFetcher` on the client (`ClientBuilder::media_fetcher`, matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `src/media.rs:167`), so `Media::get_media_content` keeps its media cache. It builds the same requests as the SDK (authenticated media v1 when the server has it, else the v3 endpoints), sends them with `Client::http_client()` and the session token, reads the body in chunks and stops at the caller's cap: a `Content-Length` over the cap is refused before the body is read, a body that streams past it is dropped at the cap. The cap travels in a task-local (`media_fetch::with_cap`): a thumbnail 10 MiB, a file the declared `info.size` plus 1 MiB of slack (never more than 512 MiB, and the declared size can only lower it), an avatar 1 MiB; a download with no cap set gets 10 MiB, so a new caller fails closed. A 401 refreshes the token once and resends (`Client::refresh_access_token`). An encrypted file is decrypted in `spawn_blocking`, so its peak is up to twice the cap. No new dependency: `matrix_sdk::reqwest` is the re-export `call/jwt.rs` already uses (rule 3 did not need the human). Tests: unit tests for the reader (within the cap, past it without `Content-Length`, an announced length over it, a lying small length, statuses), for the cap table and for the cap reaching the fetcher; `tests/media.rs::a_file_bigger_than_it_declares_is_refused_while_reading` (a 3 MiB file declaring 10 bytes is refused, an honest one is fetched whole; without the cap the first is returned whole, checked). Not covered: a 401 mid-session (the refresh path), and `Content-Length`-less downloads from a real server.

## Phase 1 / Task 1 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (3 new paths tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh` (exit 0), `cargo deny check` (advisories, bans, licenses, sources ok). TDD: the 3 paths tests were first run against `todo!()` stubs and failed, then passed after implementation.
- `cargo tree -p twicecat-core --depth 1`: matrix-sdk v0.19.1, matrix-sdk-ui v0.19.1, keyring v4.2.0, directories v6.0.0, rand v0.10.3, rpassword v7.5.4, tokio v1.53.1, tracing-subscriber v0.3.23, tempfile v3.27.0 (dev). `iced` stays 0.14.0 and wgpu 27.0.1 in Cargo.lock; no existing package version removed.
- deny.toml: `MPL-2.0` re-added to the allowlist and a `[[licenses.exceptions]]` entry for `webpki-root-certs` with `CDLA-Permissive-2.0`, each with a reason comment (Q2). The iced advisory ignores are unchanged.
- Reference study (tag `matrix-sdk-0.19.1`, cloned in the session scratchpad, nothing copied into the repo): `labs/multiverse/src/main.rs` (SyncService builder, `entries_with_dynamic_adapters` plus `set_filter(new_filter_non_left())`, per-room timelines), `examples/persist_session/src/main.rs` (`sqlite_store(path, Some(passphrase))`, `restore_session`, session file split), `examples/oauth_cli/src/main.rs` (`LocalServerBuilder::new().spawn()`, `finish_login`, OAuth session restore), `examples/timeline/src/main.rs` (`timeline.subscribe()`), `examples/login/src/main.rs` (password login). Patterns adopted: per-session encrypted sqlite store with a random passphrase kept out of the data dir (keychain instead of the example's plain JSON file), session restore through `restore_session`, dynamic-adapter room list with explicit filter.

## Phase 1 / Task 2 notes

No homeserver tests exist yet, so no ignored-test run.

- APIs used: no SDK API. `api/` is SDK-free (std, `thiserror`). `ListDiff` mirrors the 11 `VectorDiff` variants named in the plan (Append, Clear, PushFront, PushBack, PopFront, PopBack, Insert, Set, Remove, Truncate, Reset); the SDK mapping is Task 6/7 and will be confirmed there.
- Design: `ListDiff::apply` ignores out-of-range indices instead of panicking. `TimelineItemKind` has a manual redacted `Debug` (body shown only as byte length), so any event holding a timeline item is safe to log. `SendStatus` is `Sending | Sent | Failed`.
- Negative self-check of `scripts/check-core-api.sh`: with a temporary `use matrix_sdk::Client;` as the first line of `api/mod.rs` it exits 1 (reports the line); a temporary `pub use matrix_sdk::Client;` in `lib.rs` also exits 1; reverted, it exits 0. A missing `api/` dir exits 2 (fail closed).
- CI: `lint` job runs `scripts/check-core-api.sh` after `check-app-deps.sh`.
- Review fix: `Command::SendMessage.text` is now `MessageText` and `CoreEvent::OAuthUrl` holds `AuthUrl`; both have redacted `Debug` (text shows byte length, URL shows scheme+host only) with `expose()` accessors. Tests added for both, plus `ListDiff::Insert` at `index == len`.
- Open issues: none. New human items: none (the `api` approval is already on the checkpoint list).

Serde error text is never forwarded, because serde can quote values. `from_bytes` rejects versions other than 1. Passphrase: 32 bytes, lowercase hex (64 chars), no new dependency (no base64 crate in core).

## Phase 1 / Task 4 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (40 core unit tests; 4 login tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (zeroize licence ok), shellcheck (docker) on check-core-api.sh. Ignored-test run: `docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/create-test-users.sh && cargo test -p twicecat-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` passed 3 times in a row (4/4), then `docker compose stop`. The real-keychain test was never run (Decision 7). `grep -c` of the ignore string in tests/login.rs = 4 = number of `#[tokio::test]`.
- TDD: login tests were first run before `Core` existed (compile error, unresolved imports). Session-record validation and path tests were added with their code in one step (not run red separately for paths).
- Integration failures found and fixed (not flakes): the first runs timed out because Synapse's default login rate limits (per-address burst 5, failed-attempt limits) returned 429 with a retry_after of minutes. Fix: `dev/synapse/start.sh` now relaxes `rc_login` (dev only); it applies only to a fresh volume, so run `docker compose down -v` once locally. The engine also bounds password login with a 20 s timeout (`LOGIN_TIMEOUT`), because the SDK waits out `retry_after`.
- SDK APIs used (matrix-sdk 0.19.1, registry src/): `ClientBuilder::{server_name_or_homeserver_url client/builder/mod.rs:283, homeserver_url :221, sqlite_store :301, handle_refresh_tokens :534, build :656}`; `ClientBuildError` (:945); `MatrixAuth::login_username` (authentication/matrix/mod.rs:175), `LoginBuilder::{initial_device_display_name login_builder.rs:135, send :174}` (send uses `RequestConfig::short_retry()`); `Client::{restore_session client/mod.rs:1591, session :889, logout :1652, subscribe_to_session_changes :3542, user_id :818, device_id :823, homeserver :711}`; `SessionChange::{UnknownToken, TokensRefreshed}` (client/mod.rs:167); `AuthSession` and `From<MatrixSession>` (authentication/mod.rs:193, :235); `matrix_sdk::Error::{Http, client_api_error_kind}` (error.rs:250, :428) and `HttpError::Reqwest` (:66); `ErrorKind::{Forbidden, UserDeactivated, LimitExceeded}` (ruma-common-0.20.0/src/api/error/kind.rs:124, :357, :168); `Client::devices` (client/mod.rs:2972, test helper only). zeroize 1.9.0: `Zeroizing` (src/lib.rs:604; `serde` feature for Serialize/Deserialize :706).
- Design: `engine.rs` (private; only `Core`, `CoreConfig`, `CoreHandle` are re-exported from lib.rs), `client.rs` (builder + error mapping to fixed messages). Server choice: `homeserver_override`, else the command's `server`, else the MXID's server name; a bare localpart with neither gives a `Login` error. Discovery note: with `@alice:localhost` and no override, discovery tries `https://localhost` and fails, which is why tests pass the override (`HOMESERVER_URL`). Restore failures (missing store dir, invalid record, keychain error) emit `Error`, not `NoSession`, so a transient keychain failure does not push the user into a duplicate login. `LoginOAuth`, `SelectRoom`, `SendMessage` are stubs until Tasks 7 and 9. OAuth records are refused on restore until Task 9. On `UnknownToken` (hard or soft logout) the record, marker and store dir are deleted and `SessionExpired` is emitted.
- Review carry-overs: (a) `session_store_dir` is an allowlist (1..=128 of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, Windows reserved names rejected; tests include "C:x", "C:", "a:b", "CON", "x."). (b) data root, `sessions/` and the session dir are created 0o700, `last_account` is 0o600 and tightened if older (Unix test). (c) `SessionRecord::from_bytes` checks session_id, homeserver URL (https, or http only for localhost/127.0.0.0/8/::1) and user_id == auth user id, with non-secret `CoreError::Storage`. (d) every `SecretStore` and file-system call goes through `spawn_blocking`. (e) the module-wide `allow(dead_code)` in session.rs is gone. (f) SDK errors map to constant messages; no SDK error text is forwarded or logged. (g) `.expose()` is used only at the login call; nothing logs exposed values.
- (h) `scripts/check-core-api.sh` new rules, each with a negative self-check (temporary edit, exit 1, reverted, exit 0): multi-line `pub use matrix_sdk::{ Client, };` in lib.rs fails; `pub mod engine;` in lib.rs fails; `pub(crate) mod foo;` in lib.rs fails; `pub use crate::engine::Core as X;` in api/mod.rs fails; the same split across lines (`pub use crate::` newline `session::..`) fails; `pub use crate::api::CoreError as Y;` in api/ is allowed (exit 0). Allow-list: api, paths, secrets. shellcheck clean.
- Zeroize (item i): `Password` and `MessageText` hold `Zeroizing<String>` (clones zeroize separately); `SessionRecord.store_passphrase` is `Zeroizing<String>`; `SessionRecord::to_bytes` returns `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>`; the passphrase generator zeroizes its 32 raw bytes and the hex string wipes on drop; `InMemorySecretStore` stores `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>`; `SecretStore::get` returns `Option<Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>>` (both backends). Known limitation: the SDK-owned `MatrixSession`/`UserSession` tokens (and the SDK's own copies inside `Client`) are not zeroized by us, and `AuthUrl` is not wiped; no SDK type is wrapped or forked. Residual copies can also exist in serde_json intermediate buffers and the keyring backend.
- Open issues: none. Parallel integration tests share the three accounts: bob is used only by the restore test (it counts devices).
- New human items: none beyond the existing list (real keychain restore on each OS is still covered by the keyring item); `docker compose down -v` once to pick up the relaxed rc_login.
- Review fix (Security Medium): password login accepted plain `http://` homeservers (caller `server`/`homeserver_override`, or an https `.well-known` returning an http `base_url`) while restore rejected them. `build_client(ServerSpec::NameOrUrl)` now applies the restore policy (`session::is_allowed_homeserver_url`: https, or http only for localhost/loopback) to an explicit http(s) URL before the SDK contacts it, and again to the resolved `client.homeserver()` before any credential is sent. Failure emits `LoginFailed(Login("the homeserver must use https"))` and the half-created store dir is removed as for other failures. Any future OAuth login must also use `build_client`. Tests: `homeserver_policy_on_resolved_urls` (unit) and `http_non_loopback_homeserver_is_refused_before_login` (no server needed, so not `#[ignore]`; asserts the exact message, empty secret store, no store dir, no marker); seen failing first. Local-homeserver tests (http://127.0.0.1:8008) still pass.
- Review fix (Security Medium, follow-up): `build_client` now calls `.respect_login_well_known(false)`. Verified in matrix-sdk 0.19.1 registry source: setter `client/builder/mod.rs:383` (default `true` at :165, passed to the client at :756); consumed in `Client::maybe_update_login_well_known` at `client/mod.rs:1576`, which otherwise calls `set_homeserver` without a scheme check. Defence in depth: `client::ensure_homeserver_after_login` re-checks `client.homeserver()` right after a successful login and before `persist_new`; on failure there is no server logout (no request over http), the client is dropped, the caller removes the store dir, and `LoginFailed(Login("the homeserver must use https"))` is emitted. Tests: `recheck_rejects_cleartext_non_loopback_homeserver`, `recheck_accepts_https_and_loopback_http` (no network; seen failing first as a compile error). No mock login-with-well_known test: the setter was verified in source instead.
- Known limitations: (1) `LOGIN_TIMEOUT` can orphan a server-side device if the server accepted the login before the timeout. (2) The SDK/reqwest default redirect policy could resend the `/login` body on a 307/308 redirect to http (upstream behaviour; only reachable from the already trusted https endpoint).

- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (47 core unit tests; 6 homeserver tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. The real-keychain test was never run (Decision 7). tests/room_list.rs: 2 ignore reasons = 2 `#[tokio::test]`.
- TDD: unit tests (`sync::map_state` over a fake state sequence, `room_list::map_diff` over all 11 variants and apply-equivalence, name fallback) were run red against `todo!()` stubs (4 failed), then green. The two integration tests were run red before the engine wired sync (both timed out waiting for `SyncState(Running)`), then green.
- SDK APIs used (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 / matrix-sdk 0.19.1 registry src/): `SyncService::{builder sync_service.rs:606, room_list_service :612, state :617, start :629, stop :657}`, `SyncServiceBuilder::{with_offline_mode :812, build :849}`, `sync_service::State` (:64; variants Idle, Running, Terminated, Error(Arc<Error>), Offline), `sync_service::Error` (:891, `Supervisor` constructible for the unit test); `RoomListService::all_rooms` (room_list_service/mod.rs:465); `RoomList::entries_with_dynamic_adapters` (room_list_service/room_list.rs:139; yields nothing until a filter is set, doc at :124-:130), `RoomListDynamicEntriesController::set_filter` (:342); `filters::new_filter_non_left` (room_list_service/filters/mod.rs:98); `RoomListItem: Deref<Target = Room>` (room_list.rs:458); `matrix_sdk::Room: Deref<Target = BaseRoom>` (matrix-sdk room/mod.rs:219); `Room::cached_display_name` (matrix-sdk-base-0.19.1/src/room/display_name.rs:61, `Option<RoomDisplayName>`, Display), `num_unread_messages/notifications/mentions` (matrix-sdk-base room/mod.rs:189/:197/:206), `room_id` (:141); `matrix_sdk_ui::eyeball_im` re-export (matrix-sdk-ui lib.rs:19, so no new dependency) and `VectorDiff` (eyeball-im-0.9.1/src/vector.rs:349); `eyeball::Subscriber::{get eyeball-0.8.8/src/subscriber.rs:72, Stream impl :197}`. Usage pattern matches `labs/multiverse/src/main.rs:216-235` (SyncService build/start) and `:273-274` (entries_with_dynamic_adapters(..) then set_filter(Box::new(new_filter_non_left()))), read from the tag clone in the scratchpad. Test helpers: `Client::create_room` (client/mod.rs:1931), `join_room_by_id` (:1819), `Room::send` (room/mod.rs:2550), `Client::get_room`, `ruma::api::client::room::create_room::v3::Request` and `RoomMessageEventContent::text_plain` via `matrix_sdk::ruma`.
- Design: new private modules `sync.rs` (`map_state`, mapping table in its doc comment: Idle/Running/Offline/Terminated 1:1, `Error(_)` to `SyncState::Error("sync failed")`, SDK error text never forwarded) and `room_list.rs` (`map_diff`, `summarize`, `name_or_id`; the name is untrusted and never logged). Engine: after `LoggedIn` (login or restore) `start_sync` builds `SyncService::builder(client).with_offline_mode().build()`, spawns two forwarders (state: subscribe, emit current, then distinct changes; room list: one `RoomListChanged` per diff batch), then `start()`. `Active::stop_sync` aborts and awaits the forwarders (they hold client clones), then calls `stop()`; it runs on `Logout`, `Shutdown` or command-channel close, and `UnknownToken`, before the client is dropped and local data deleted. `CoreConfig` gained `room_list_page_size` (public const `DEFAULT_ROOM_LIST_PAGE_SIZE` = 500; 0 is clamped to 1). Existing `CoreConfig` literals in tests were updated.
- Test fix (not a product bug): `tests/login.rs::no_token_on_disk` failed once in 8 runs with ENOENT. With sync running, SQLite journal/WAL files can disappear while the SDK closes the store in the background after the event channel closes, between the directory listing and the read. The test now skips files that vanished (and tolerates a directory vanishing); it still scans every file that exists. Three consecutive full ignored runs passed afterwards. Not the same failure twice in a row.
- Open issues: Sync errors and reconnection policy are Task 8 (the `Reconnecting` state is not produced yet). The SDK may drop store connections slightly after the event channel closes (see above).
- New human items: none.

## Phase 1 / Task 7 notes
- Checks run (all pass; counts current at 13d78fe): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (62 core unit tests; 9 homeserver tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. Ignored-test run: `docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/create-test-users.sh && cargo test -p twicecat-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` passed 3 times in a row (4 login + 2 room_list + 3 timeline; ignored run 9/9 at 13d78fe), then `docker compose stop`. The real-keychain test was never run (Decision 7). tests/timeline.rs: 3 ignore reasons = 3 `#[tokio::test]`.
- TDD: unit tests (`map_send_state` table, `parse_room_id`) were run red against `todo!()` stubs (2 failed), then green. The three integration tests were run red against the Task 4 stub (they got `Internal("not implemented yet")` or timed out), then green. A test bug found on the way: the first draft of the receiver's initial wait returned immediately; it now waits for the first `TimelineDiff` and asserts it is a single `Reset`.
- SDK APIs used (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 / matrix-sdk 0.19.1 registry src/, paths under `matrix-sdk-ui-0.19.1/src/timeline/` unless noted): `RoomExt::{timeline traits.rs:51 (impl :76), timeline_builder :62}` (`.timeline()` is `timeline_builder().build()`; used as in `labs/multiverse/src/main.rs:320-330`); `Timeline::subscribe` (mod.rs:332, async, returns `(Vector<Arc<TimelineItem>>, impl Stream<Item = Vec<VectorDiff<Arc<TimelineItem>>>> + use<>)`; `examples/timeline/src/main.rs:75-76`, `labs/multiverse/src/widgets/room_view/mod.rs:161`); `Timeline::send(AnyMessageLikeEventContent) -> Result<SendHandle, Error>` (mod.rs:363; `labs/multiverse/src/widgets/room_view/mod.rs:561` sends `RoomMessageEventContent::text_plain(..).into()`); `TimelineItem::{as_event item.rs:68, unique_id :86}` and `TimelineUniqueId(pub String)` (:27); `EventTimelineItem::{send_state event_item/mod.rs:224, sender :287, content :313, timestamp :342, is_own :347}`; `TimelineItemContent::as_msglike` (event_item/content/mod.rs:257), `MsgLikeContent.kind` (content/msg_like.rs:69), `MsgLikeKind::Message` (:27); `Message::{msgtype content/message.rs:76, body :83}`; `EventSendState::{NotSentYet, SendingFailed, Sent}` (event_item/local.rs:69, `error: Arc<matrix_sdk::Error>`); all re-exported from `matrix_sdk_ui::timeline` (timeline/mod.rs:88-105). `Client::get_room` (matrix-sdk client/mod.rs:1487); `RoomId::parse` (generated by ruma-macros-0.20.0 src/identifiers/id_dst.rs:241); `RoomMessageEventContent::text_plain` (ruma-events room/message.rs:126); `MessageType::Text` (room/message.rs:418); `MilliSecondsSinceUnixEpoch::get() -> UInt` (ruma-common-0.20.0 src/time.rs:124, `u64::from(UInt)`); `matrix_sdk::Error::AuthenticationRequired` (error.rs:258, used only to build a failed send state in a unit test). `recursion_limit = "256"` was already set in Task 1.
- Design: new private `timeline.rs` (`map_item`, `map_send_state`, `parse_room_id`); the generic `room_list::map_diff` is reused for `ListDiff<TimelineItem>`. Only `MessageType::Text` maps to `Message`; notices, emotes, images, state events, virtual items (date dividers, read marker), redacted and undecryptable items map to `Other`. `sent`: local echo `NotSentYet` is `Sending`, `SendingFailed` is `Failed`, `Sent` and every remote event is `Sent`. Item `id` is the SDK's `unique_id`, stable across local echo to remote echo. Engine: `Active.selected: Option<Selected { room_id, timeline, task }>`. `SelectRoom` validates the ID (malformed or not in the store gives `Error(UnknownRoom)`, and the previous selection is kept), aborts and awaits the previous forwarder, builds the timeline, subscribes, and spawns `forward_timeline`, which sends the initial items as one `Reset` and then one `TimelineDiff` per batch. `SendMessage` with no login gives `Error(NotLoggedIn)`, with no selection `Error(NoRoomSelected)`, and a `send()` error gives `SendFailed { room_id, error: Internal("the message could not be queued") }` (the SDK error text is not forwarded). `MessageText::expose()` is used at exactly one place, the `text_plain` call. `Active::stop_sync` was renamed `stop_tasks` and now stops the timeline forwarder first, so logout, shutdown and `UnknownToken` release the timeline before the client. No sender or body is logged anywhere.
- Test setup note: the receiving account in the A-to-B test is carol (user 3), not bob as the plan says, because `login::restore_without_logging_in_again` counts bob's devices and the room_list unread test already uses bob. The plan's "unknown room" case also has an integration test (`selecting_an_unknown_room_is_an_error`).
- Open issues: `SelectRoom` right after `Running` can return `UnknownRoom` if the room has not synced yet (tests wait for the room in the room list first); the front end should select from room list entries. A failed send is only reported through the item's `sent == Failed` and `SendFailed` for queueing errors; there is no retry command (out of scope). `room_list::unread_count_reaches_two` uses bob concurrently with the restore test's device count (pre-existing from Task 6; it passed 3 of 3 runs here, but is a potential flake).
- New human items: none (timeline rendering is Phase 2).

- Keychain note (Task 7 review): an existing keychain record that cannot be read or parsed is treated as absent and is overwritten by the new login.

- Supervisor, first design (SUPERSEDED by "Task 8 follow-up" and "Task 8 follow-up 2" below; kept for the record): `backoff_delay(attempt, jitter_unit)` is a pure function (1 s, 2 s, 4 s ... base capped at 60 s, then scaled by 0.8..=1.2, so at most 72 s; NaN and out-of-range samples are clamped; this part is unchanged). `supervise` is generic over a private `SyncControl` trait (`restart`, `stop`, `now`, `sleep`, `jitter_unit` in the final form); tests use a fake control and a fake `State` stream, with no network and no tokio `test-util` feature (sleep is injected and recorded). On `Error` or `Terminated` only `Reconnecting { attempt }` is emitted (no separate `Error` event); `Idle`, `Running`, `Offline` are mapped 1:1 and deduplicated. `SyncState::Error` is no longer produced by the engine (`map_state` keeps the row). No longer true: "`Running` resets the count" (now only a 30 s stable `Running` does), "`Offline` is reported and never restarted" (now throttled after 3 unstable cycles), and "the engine aborts the supervisor first" (now it is cancelled between steps, never aborted).
- Offline mode (SDK side): in the SDK supervisor, a failure with `offline_mode` sets `Offline`, polls `/versions`, and sets `Running` again when it answers (matrix-sdk-ui-0.19.1/src/sync_service.rs:313-330; `Error` appears only if the offline check itself ends in error, :317-319).
- SDK APIs confirmed (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 registry src/sync_service.rs): `State` enum (:64-98: Idle, Running, Terminated, Error(Arc<Error>), Offline; Offline doc :80-97), `SyncService::state() -> Subscriber<State>` (:617), `start()` (:629; from Idle, Terminated or Error it starts, from Offline it restarts, from Running it is a no-op, :633-648), `stop()` (:657; no-op from Idle, Terminated, Error), `SyncServiceBuilder::with_offline_mode` (:812). `eyeball::Subscriber` is `Unpin` and a `Stream` (eyeball-0.8.8/src/subscriber.rs:197, as used since Task 6).
- tracing-subscriber 0.3.23 (registry src/): `fmt()` (fmt/mod.rs:324), `SubscriberBuilder::with_env_filter` (:957, cfg feature `env-filter`, already enabled in crates/core/Cargo.toml), `with_writer` (:1057; `std::io::stderr` is a `MakeWriter`), `try_init` (:503); `EnvFilter::try_new` (filter/env/mod.rs:379), `EnvFilter::DEFAULT_ENV` = "RUST_LOG" (:249), Display of the filter used by the tests. tracing 0.1.44: `info!`/`warn!` (src/macros.rs:1896, :2176). rand 0.10.3: `rand::random` (src/lib.rs:190; `StandardUniform` for f64 at distr/float.rs:108). No dependency or feature changes.
- `logging::init(default_filter)` (`DEFAULT_FILTER` = `warn,twicecat_core=info`): `fmt` to stderr, `RUST_LOG` overrides when set, non-empty and valid; an invalid or empty `RUST_LOG` falls back to the default; an invalid default gives `LogInitError::InvalidDefaultFilter`; a second install gives `AlreadyInstalled`. It is a public module so the CLI and app can call it, so `scripts/check-core-api.sh` allow-list is now `api|paths|secrets|logging` (with a comment). Negative self-check: a temporary `pub mod sneaky;` in lib.rs exits 1 and is reported, reverted it exits 0. `logging` names no SDK type.
- Grep review `grep -nE 'body|token|password|passphrase' crates/core/src` (counts per file: timeline 1, logging 1, client 6, engine 21, session 31, others 0): all `tracing` macro calls in the core are `warn!`/`info!` at engine.rs (3 constant messages, 1 `info!("session restored")`) and sync.rs (`attempt` count and delay in ms, fixed text). No hit lies inside a tracing macro; the hits are identifiers, type names (`Password`, `body` fields in the redacted types), doc comments and tests. No token, password, passphrase, sender, room name or body is formatted into any log.
- Reconnection check (`docker compose stop synapse` / `start synapse` with a CLI): DEFERRED to Task 10, because the plan names the CLI and `twicecat-cli` is Task 10 here; no temporary binary was built and synapse is not stopped in the automated tests (it would break concurrent integration tests).
- Open issues: the reconnection check above (open item for Task 10: with alice logged in and syncing, `docker compose stop synapse` must report `Offline` or `Reconnecting`; after `docker compose start synapse`, `Running` within 90 s and a message sent afterwards is delivered; record the observed event sequence here; also try to provoke the unstable-Offline loop with the real synapse (e.g. a server that answers `/versions` while sync fails, or stop/start synapse repeatedly) and record the observed sequence and the delays). `Reconnecting` is produced after the SDK's own `Error`/`Terminated` and by the offline-cycle throttle; with offline mode on, ordinary network loss shows as `Offline`.
- New human items: none.

### Task 8 follow-up (review Critical, Security Medium x2, Low)
- Supervisor, final behaviour (`sync.rs`, SDK facts checked in matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `src/sync_service.rs`: `SyncServiceInner::start` sets `Running` at once, before any request succeeds (:503-510); with offline mode a sync error sets `Offline`, polls `/versions`, then sets `Running` and restarts at once (:313-328, `offline_check` :158-220); `stop()` from Running/Offline ends in `Idle` (:657)).
  - Stability window: `STABLE_RESET` = 30 s. The attempt count and the offline-cycle count reset only when a `Running` has lasted 30 s without a failure (evaluated when the next failure arrives, with an injectable `SyncControl::now`). Before this, `Running` reset the count at once, so a persistent failure stayed at ~1 s forever. Now `Error`/`Terminated` give attempts 1, 2, 3 ... with 1, 2, 4 ... s sleeps (cap 60 s, jitter as before).
  - Offline-cycle throttle (human decision: throttle in the supervisor, offline mode kept, `SyncState(Offline)` still reported): a `Running` that is not stable before the next `Offline` is an unstable cycle. After 3 in a row the supervisor emits `Reconnecting { attempt }`, calls `stop()`, waits `backoff_delay(attempt)`, then `start()`; the attempt counter is shared with the `Error` path; the next unstable cycle after a throttle throttles again with a longer delay; a stable `Running` resets everything. A genuine drop that recovers to a stable `Running` is never throttled. The `Idle` caused by our own `stop()` is swallowed so it does not show as flapping.
  - Remaining visible sequence under a persistent failure: `Running`, `Reconnecting{1}`, `Running`, `Reconnecting{2}` ... (the `Running` is the SDK's own, set by `start()`); the attempt number now grows instead of repeating `Reconnecting{1}`.
  - Tests (fake with a virtual clock; `restart` pushes `Running` and `stop` pushes `Idle` into the state stream like the real SDK): Error/restart/Error/restart/Error gives sleeps 1, 2, 4 s and attempts 1, 2, 3; a stable `Running` resets; a `Running` 1 s short of the window does not; rapid Offline cycles throttled with sleeps 1, 2, 4 s and 3 stops; a single Offline then stable `Running` is not throttled; stable `Running` after a throttle resets. `backoff_delay` tests unchanged.
  - Red run (before the supervisor change, new tests against the old logic): `cargo test -p twicecat-core --lib sync::` gave `6 passed; 6 failed`: a_running_that_stays_stable_resets_the_attempt_count, a_stable_running_after_a_throttle_resets_everything, backoff_is_capped_during_a_long_outage, rapid_offline_cycles_are_throttled_with_growing_delays, a_running_just_short_of_the_window_does_not_reset, error_reconnects_with_growing_backoff_when_running_is_unstable. Green after: 12 passed.
- `scripts/check-core-api.sh` now also scans the other allow-listed public modules (`paths`, `secrets`, `logging`: `<name>.rs` and a same-named directory) for SDK crate names, failing closed when a module has neither. The comment now says logging exposes `DEFAULT_FILTER`, `LogInitError` and `init`. shellcheck clean (run with the `koalaman/shellcheck:stable` docker image, `--network none`; not installed locally). Negative self-checks: `// matrix_sdk::Client` appended to logging.rs exits 1 and names the file; `// ruma` appended to paths.rs exits 1; secrets.rs moved away exits 2 ("public module secrets not found"); all reverted, script exits 0.
- Checks: full gate and the ignored run (2 runs in a row, 9/9 each, then `docker compose stop`) pass; keyring test skipped.
- Open item for Task 10: the CLI help and README must warn that `RUST_LOG=debug` or `trace` makes the SDK log request URIs and room/user identifiers. Also note in the CLI help that the SDK's own `error!`-level lines (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `src/sync_service.rs` ~:425, `{:#?}` of the sync error, which can include request URLs) are visible at the default filter (`warn,twicecat_core=info`), so stderr should not be shared or posted unreviewed.

### Task 8 follow-up 2 (review Security Medium and Low)
- Coalescing (verified in eyeball 0.8.8 `src/subscriber.rs:123-131`: `poll_next_ref` yields only the latest value once the version moved; `Subscriber::next_now` marks the current value observed). SDK transitions (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `sync_service.rs`): `Offline` is set only inside the supervisor loop after a failure while `Running` (:315); `Running` is set by `SyncServiceInner::start` (:509) or after `Offline` (:328); `SyncService::start` while `Running` does nothing (:635). So a repeated state proves a missed transition. Final rules in `supervise`: an `Offline` with no `Running` seen since the last `Offline`/restart counts as an unstable cycle; a `Running` right after a `Running` counts as an unstable cycle (missed `Offline`) and restarts the stability window; the `Running` produced by our own `restart` is expected (window starts when `restart` returns). The initial state is read with `next_now()` (not `get()`), so it is not delivered twice. Remaining blind spot: a coalesced own-restart `Running` that hides a missed cycle is read as the expected echo (only possible during the `restart` call itself).
- No consumer stall: `supervise` publishes through a `StateSink`; production uses a `tokio::sync::watch` latest-value slot (`WatchSink`) drained by a forwarder task (`forward_latest`) that waits on the 256-slot event channel. Supervision never waits for the consumer, so blocked time cannot look like a stable `Running`. Intermediate `SyncState`s may be dropped; the latest is always delivered. Public API unchanged.
- Uncancellable SDK stop/restart: the supervisor is no longer aborted. `Syncing` holds a `CancelTrigger`; `Active::stop_tasks` cancels it and awaits the supervisor task, which checks cancellation between steps (waiting for a state, the backoff sleep, before `restart`) but always runs an in-flight `SyncService::stop`/`start` to the end. Only then are the room list forwarder aborted and `service.stop()` called, so logout and UnknownToken delete the store only after the SDK stop fully completed.
- Tests (first, against plumbing with the old logic): red run `cargo test -p twicecat-core --lib sync::` gave `13 passed; 5 failed`: coalesced_running_between_offlines_still_counts_a_cycle, coalesced_offline_between_runnings_counts_a_cycle, a_missed_offline_does_not_make_the_window_look_stable, cancelling_during_stop_still_finishes_the_stop, a_stalled_consumer_does_not_stop_the_backoff (the last one failed on a flawed final assertion that I then removed; against the old blocking `events.send` it is red by construction, not recorded). Green after: 18 passed. New fake modes: `Fake::coalescing` (no echo of `Running`/`Idle`) with a `coalesce` helper that keeps only the last of several states; cancel fired from inside `stop`.
- Checks (all pass): fmt, clippy (-D warnings), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (83 core unit tests; 9 homeserver tests ignored), check-app-deps, check-core-api, cargo deny; ignored run 9/9 (4 login + 2 room_list + 3 timeline), then `docker compose stop`. Keyring test skipped.

### Follow-up fixes (human-approved)
- Security Low/Medium (Task 4/7): a login response's user ID is checked before anything is persisted, on every path (bare localpart, full MXID, explicit URL, homeserver override). Rule: (1) `client::login_account_matches`: a full MXID needs exact equality, a bare localpart an equal localpart (exact, no case folding); (2) `client::server_name_bound` binds the returned user ID's server name to the homeserver actually used (`client.homeserver()`, after `ensure_homeserver_after_login`): accept if the client was built by discovery from a bare server name equal to the returned one; or, narrow local-test exception, the homeserver is a loopback http URL and the returned name is exactly `localhost` (`.env.test` `HOMESERVER_DOMAIN`), without discovery; otherwise the returned server name is resolved by `.well-known` discovery (`client::discover_homeserver_url`) and the discovered URL must have the same origin (scheme, host, port with default ports) as `client.homeserver()`; discovery failure or mismatch refuses. Discovery API (matrix-sdk 0.19.1, `src/client/builder/`): `ClientBuilder::server_name` (`mod.rs:235`) on a throwaway client with the default in-memory store, which runs `HomeserverConfig::discover` (`homeserver_config.rs:63`, `ServerName` arm `:76-87`, lookup in `discover_homeserver` `:193`) with no fallback to a URL; the result must also pass the https/loopback policy, with a 10 s timeout. (3) Defence in depth in `persist_new` (`Engine::existing_record_session`): an existing secret-store record for the returned user ID with a different homeserver origin is refused and left untouched; one for the same origin is replaced and its old store dir (validated session_id) is removed after the new record is saved. Refusal (either place): no secret-store write, no `last_account` write, no logout request, store dir removed, `LoginFailed("the homeserver returned a different account")`. Unit tests use injected discovery results (no network): the two gaps, the delegated case (`@u:example.org` served by `https://matrix-client.example.org`), same-origin comparison, the loopback exception, and the existing-record check with `InMemorySecretStore`. No integration test for a lying homeserver (none available locally); the existing local-homeserver tests cover the `localhost` exception.
- Security Low (Task 6): `tests/login.rs::files_under` tolerates only `ErrorKind::NotFound` and panics on any other `read_dir` or dir-entry error.
- Security Low (Task 5): the CI `integration` job has `timeout-minutes: 20`; actionlint clean.
- Checks: full gate and the ignored run (see commit) pass.

## Phase 1 / Task 9 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (89 core unit tests; 10 homeserver tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`; ignored run `cargo test -p twicecat-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` 10/10 (incl. the new `tests/oauth.rs`), then `docker compose stop`.
- TDD: the unit tests (client metadata JSON, auth-URL policy, error mapping, 127.0.0.1 listener, OAuth record restore path) were seen failing to compile before the code existed; the integration test was seen failing (`Internal("OAuth login is not implemented yet")` instead of `OAuthNotSupported`) before the engine change.
- Design: `crates/core/src/oauth.rs` (metadata, `check_auth_url`, `map_oauth_error`, `spawn_redirect_listener`, `authorize`). `LoginOAuth` runs in a spawned task (build client, `authorize`) so that `CancelOAuth` and `Shutdown` stay responsive; the engine loop awaits the task (`next_oauth`) and `on_oauth_done` then runs the same `adopt_login` as a password login (https/loopback recheck, returned account bound to the server asked for, no cross-origin record overwrite, persist, sync). `persist_new`, restore, and `rewrite_record` now handle both session kinds through `SessionAuth::{to_auth_session, from_auth_session}`, so refreshed OAuth tokens are written back to the keychain. `CancelOAuth` aborts the pending authorization (`abort_login`), the task ends with `Cancelled`, `LoginFailed(Cancelled)` is emitted, and the store directory is removed. Unrequested additions: a 10 minute limit on the browser step, and refusing a second login while one is pending.
- Security carry-overs applied: the redirect listener binds `127.0.0.1` only (`LocalServerIpAddress::Localhostv4`); the auth URL must be `https` (or loopback `http`) without userinfo before `OAuthUrl` is emitted; SDK OAuth error text is never forwarded (fixed messages).
- APIs used (matrix-sdk-0.19.1, `src/`): `authentication/oauth/mod.rs` `OAuth::login` :923, `finish_login` :958, `abort_login` :1095, `full_session` :596, `restore_session` (via `Client::restore_session`, `AuthSession::OAuth(Box<OAuthSession>)` `authentication/mod.rs:198`), `Client::session`/`logout` `client/mod.rs:889`/`:1652` (both dispatch on OAuth); `auth_code_builder.rs` `OAuthAuthCodeUrlBuilder::build`; `registration.rs` `ClientMetadata::new`, `Localized::new`, `OAuthGrantType::AuthorizationCode`, `ApplicationType::Native`; `error.rs` `OAuthError`, `OAuthDiscoveryError::NotSupported`, `OAuthClientRegistrationError::NotSupported`; `utils/local_server.rs` `LocalServerBuilder::{ip_address, spawn}` :91/:135, `LocalServerIpAddress::Localhostv4` :303; `utils/mod.rs:276` `From<QueryString> for UrlOrQuery`. Registry path: `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/matrix-sdk-0.19.1/`. I did not read the repo's `examples/oauth_cli` (not in the tarball).
- Open issues: the SDK validates the authorization server's metadata URLs itself (`validate_urls`), but Twicecat does not re-check them beyond the auth URL. A cancel during client build or registration drops that future (the network call is abandoned, not awaited); `finish_login` is deliberately not raced against cancel, so a cancel in that window waits for it (bounded by the 5 s shutdown timeout) and a login that still succeeds is logged out and discarded. A stray local GET to the redirect port makes that login fail (state check), by design of the SDK listener (one request). Local MAS does not exist, so the real flow and token refresh are unverified.
- New human items (also in the Phase 1 checklist): OIDC login on matrix.org through the CLI; the session survives a CLI restart; CLI kept running over 10 minutes so MAS refreshes the access token, then restart and confirm restore works.

## Phase 1 / Task 10 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (bin unit tests for args, REPL parsing, room resolution, status lines, terminal-control sanitising, help text), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`; ignored run `cargo test --workspace -- --ignored --skip keyring` against the local Synapse (includes `tests/cli.rs::ephemeral_password_login_and_quit`), then `docker compose stop`.
- TDD: `tests/cli.rs` was seen failing to compile (no `CARGO_BIN_EXE_twicecat-cli`) and the bin unit tests were seen failing against `todo!()` stubs before the implementation.
- Design: `crates/core/src/bin/twicecat-cli.rs` uses only `Core::start`, `Command`, `CoreEvent` and API types (a test greps the source for `matrix_sdk`, `ruma`, `eyeball`, `imbl`). One stdin reader task feeds lines to the loop; stdin is not read while a password login is in flight, so `/rooms` queued behind a login runs after it. `--password-stdin` reads the password from the next stdin line, otherwise `rpassword::prompt_password`. `--ephemeral` uses `InMemorySecretStore` and `<tmp>/twicecat-cli-<128-bit random hex>`, removed at exit; it cannot be combined with `--data-dir`. Room names, senders, and bodies are printed with control characters replaced by `?` (terminal escape injection). Room indices are 0-based. Own sent messages are echoed back through the timeline as `[room] sender: body`.
- APIs used: only twicecat-core's own (`Core::start` engine.rs:72, `CoreConfig`, `CoreHandle`, `Command`, `CoreEvent`, `ListDiff::apply`), `rpassword` 7.5.4 `prompt_password` (`~/.cargo/registry/src/*/rpassword-7.5.4/src/lib.rs:270`), `rand::fill` (already used in session.rs), `tokio::io::{stdin, AsyncBufReadExt::lines}`. No matrix-sdk or iced API was added.
- Deferred Task 8 reconnection check, run with this CLI (alice, `--ephemeral`, local Synapse; times local):
  - 11:42:05 `Logged in as @alice:localhost`, `[sync] idle`, `[sync] running`
  - 11:42:13 `docker compose stop synapse`; 11:42:15 `[sync] offline` (2 s later; it stayed Offline for the 35 s outage, no Reconnecting)
  - 11:42:49 `docker compose start synapse`; 11:42:53 `[sync] running` (4 s, limit 90 s)
  - then `/select` and `/send` of a unique text: echoed in the timeline, and a REST read of the room's messages as alice found it, so it was delivered. stderr during the outage had the SDK's `ERROR ... matrix_sdk::http_client: Error while sending request` line about every 7.6 s (the SDK's own error lines, as documented below).
  - The unstable-Offline-loop provocation (a server that answers `/versions` while sync fails) was not reproduced: the local Synapse is either fully up or down. That path stays covered by the supervisor unit tests only.
- CLI help warns that `RUST_LOG=debug` or `trace` can make the SDK and HTTP crates log sensitive request data, and that the SDK's own error lines (request URLs) show at the default filter so stderr should not be shared unreviewed. The same help text carries the orphaned-OAuth-session note.
- Open issues:
  - A cancelled or shutdown-aborted OAuth login may leave an orphaned server-side session (device) that the user must revoke from the account settings of their homeserver/MAS.
  - Task 9 follow-up: a shutdown abort during `finish_login` (over 5 s) can also leave such a session, and a failed best-effort logout of a cancelled login leaves it valid server-side.
  - The password prompt blocks the CLI loop while waiting (events queue in the engine's bounded channel, 256); fine for interactive use.
  - `/rooms` prints every room; no paging.
- New human items: see the Phase 1 checklist below (CLI real-account run, restart without prompt, keychain viewer entry `io.github.ga_lep.Twicecat` and no token in the data dir).

## Phase 1 / Task 10 review follow-up
- Password prompt race fixed: one reader thread owns the terminal and serves one request at a time (`Request::Line` or `Request::Password`), driven by a `Gate`; a password read is only started when no line read is outstanding, and a password input can never be routed to the REPL (unit tests: `password_is_read_apart_from_repl_lines`, `gate_never_routes_a_password_to_the_repl`, `gate_allows_one_reader_at_a_time`). Real-terminal behaviour is unverified (human list).
- `sanitize` also maps U+200B-U+200F, U+202A-U+202E, U+2066-U+2069, U+FEFF to `?` (unit-tested).
- Ctrl-C: `tokio::signal::ctrl_c` runs alongside the REPL (tokio `signal` feature added to `crates/core/Cargo.toml`, human-approved, no version change) and takes the normal Shutdown and temp-dir cleanup path. With `--ephemeral` the CLI sends `Logout` and waits (10 s) before `Shutdown` so the server session is revoked; documented in the help text. A SIGINT during a login in flight relies on the engine's Shutdown cancel path.
- `--password-stdin` with a terminal on stdin prints a warning to stderr (echo); documented in the help text.

### Task 10 final-review follow-up (human-approved)
- Stuck startup: on `CoreEvent::Error` while Busy and not logged in (restore or login in flight; the engine's `restore()` emits `Error`, not `NoSession`, for an unavailable keychain, an inconsistent or unparsable record, a missing store dir, or a failed `restore_session`) the CLI now prints the error, a fixed hint, and returns to Idle, so stdin is read and `/login` and `/quit` work. Other not-logged-in exits from Busy were checked: `LoginFailed`, `NoSession` (goes to the username prompt), `SessionExpired`, `LoggedOut` already leave Busy. Tests: `error_during_restore_or_login_leaves_busy` (seen failing first: Busy vs Idle), `error_keeps_other_modes`, `other_not_logged_in_failures_leave_busy`, `quit_works_after_a_failed_restore`. While a login or restore is genuinely in flight the CLI still does not read stdin (so piped input queued behind it is not lost); a hung login can only be left with Ctrl-C.
- Terminal echo: Ctrl-C is now a message from a forwarding task to the loop instead of a `select!` arm in `main`. While a hidden password read (`rpassword`) is pending, Ctrl-C is deferred (`ctrl_c_action`, `Cli::interrupt_action`): the CLI prints a note, the read finishes on Enter (rpassword restores echo), the password is dropped, and the CLI exits through the normal shutdown path. With `--password-stdin` no echo is involved, so Ctrl-C quits at once. No new dependency. Tests: `ctrl_c_waits_for_a_hidden_password_read`, `ctrl_c_during_a_password_read_waits_for_the_read_then_quits` (the Ctrl-C tests were seen failing first as compile errors). Real-terminal behaviour is on the human list (item reworded).
- Open issues: a stale record whose store dir is missing still yields `Error` on every restore (core behaviour, not changed here); the user must log in again, which replaces it. Ctrl-C at the password prompt needs Enter to take effect (a second Ctrl-C is deferred too). A tiny window exists between two `ctrl_c()` registrations in the forwarder where a SIGINT could be missed.

### CI triggers follow-up (human-approved)
- `ci.yml` now triggers on `push` to `main`, on `pull_request`, and on `workflow_dispatch`, so a push to a branch with an open PR no longer runs the workflow twice. Feature branches get CI through their PR; a branch without a PR can be run manually via workflow_dispatch; main runs on push after merge. Nothing else in the workflow changed.

- Task 9 follow-up (human-approved): shutdown can no longer hang (the `OAuthUrl` send and the client build are raced against cancel; the engine waits 5 s for the task, then aborts it, waits for the abort so the store is released, and removes the store dir). A cancel that arrives after `finish_login` succeeded now logs the client out (best effort, 5 s) and emits `LoginFailed(Cancelled)` without persisting anything. The account binding was extracted to `client::account_bound` with a test that an OAuth user ID from another server name is refused (OAuth has no typed username, so `server_name_bound` is the only binding).

### CI disk and time follow-up (human-approved)
- `ci.yml`: workflow-level `CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_DEBUG: line-tables-only` (the Ubuntu runner ran out of disk linking ~880 MB debug test binaries). The test job drops the separate `cargo build` step, runs clippy before tests only on non-Linux (the lint job covers Linux), and has `timeout-minutes: 60` (Windows took ~35 min). No disk-freeing step was added; add one (plain shell, no new action) only if Linux still runs out of space.
- rust-cache keys include `CARGO_*` env, so the first run after this change starts with a cold cache; that is expected.
- Not verifiable locally: the effect on the CI runners; check the next CI run.

### Flaky `unread_count_reaches_two` (human-approved)
- Root cause (test race, not a core bug): the room list syncs with `timeline_limit` 1 and `num_unread_messages` is computed client-side from the timeline events the core receives. The test sent both messages right after joining, so if the core's sync only saw the room afterwards (slow CI), it got just the latest event and the count stayed at 1 forever. Reproduced deterministically by sending both messages before the core logs in (count stays 1 for 5 s+), and under `taskset -c 0,1` with CPU hogs (original test failed at `room_list.rs:51`).
- Fix: the test now waits for the room to appear, then sends "first" and waits for unread >= 1, then "second" and waits for unread >= 2. No timeout or sleep change.
- Verified: fixed test 12/12 under `taskset -c 0,1` plus 3 CPU hogs; full `--ignored --skip keyring` suite 6/6 (3 plain, 3 with `taskset -c 0,1 --test-threads 2`); gate and boundary scripts ok; compose stopped.

The tab test failed once with a 30 s timeout in a full-file run, then passed alone and in the next full run (not twice in a row). If it recurs, reset the Synapse volume (`docker compose down -v`).
- Flaky integration run, root cause (two independent causes, both test infrastructure; core code unchanged):
  1. Slowness: Synapse's default rate limits. `docker compose logs synapse` showed `SynapseError: 429 - Too Many Requests (rc_room_creation)` on `createRoom` and `(rc_messages)` on message sends for `@alice` (defaults: 10 room creations then 1 per minute). The SDK honours `retry_after` and sleeps, so a create took up to about a minute each (hence the minutes-long runs and the earlier 30 s timeout of `tab_queries_split_dms_rooms_and_spaces`). Fix: `dev/synapse/start.sh` sets `rc_message`, `rc_room_creation`, `rc_joins`, `rc_invites`, `rc_3pid_validation` to 1000/s (local test server only), appended once behind a `# twicecat-dev-rate-limits` marker so an existing volume gets it on the next container start too. After the fix the logs show 0 `Too Many` lines.
  2. Deterministic failure of `dm_and_space_flags` (the 30 s timeout, even with no 429s): `Client::create_dm` marks the room in `m.direct` by reading the client's local copy and PUTting the whole event. The test helper clients never sync, so that copy is empty and each DM replaced the server's `m.direct`; `dm_and_space_flags` and `tab_queries_split_dms_rooms_and_spaces` run concurrently on the same account (alice to bob), so one test's DM was dropped from `m.direct` and stayed `is_dm == false`. Passed alone (5 s), failed in the full file. Fix: `create_dm` in `tests/common/mod.rs` runs under a process-wide lock, fetches `m.direct` from the server first and writes the merged event back after creating the DM. No assertion or timeout changed.
  - Possible product note (not changed, not this step): a room list `Set` is not emitted when `m.direct` arrives after the room, so a DM seen before its `m.direct` stays non-DM until the list is rebuilt. Worth a look in a later step.
- Verification of the fix: ignored run (`cargo test -p twicecat-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`) three times in a row, run 1 on a fresh `docker compose down -v` volume; all passed. `room_list` file wall time: 11.14 s, 12.35 s, 14.12 s (was 190 s). Before the fix on a fresh volume: 189 s, failing.

## Phase 2 / Task 5 notes
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked`, `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` and the ignored run (`cargo test -p twicecat-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`) all pass (all suites ok at that commit; the integration suites were avatars 1 passed in 8.5 s, room_list 8 in 17 s). The keyring test was not run.
- TDD: the first 11 `avatars.rs` unit tests were written against `todo!()` stubs and failed (11 failed, 1 passed), then the code was added; later security rounds raised the module to 18 unit tests (see the follow-up notes below).
- Core: private module `avatars.rs` (not in `check-core-api.sh`). `Active` owns `avatars: Avatars` (a `JoinSet`, the seen set, a 4-permit `Semaphore`) and `fetcher: Arc<SdkFetcher>`; `stop_tasks` calls `avatars.stop()` (abort all, drain, clear the seen set) first, before the sync tasks and the store go. With no session, `FetchAvatars` answers every ID with `image: None` (`answer_unavailable`).
- Security rules from the reviews, all covered by unit tests:
  - Each `AvatarId` is parsed as an `OwnedMxcUri` and must be `mxc://server/media` (non-empty media ID, at most 255 bytes) before any request. `"not-mxc"`, `https://` and `file://` URLs, userinfo, `/`, `?`, `#`, `..`, whitespace, wrong case of the scheme, empty media ID and over-long IDs give `image: None` with zero fetch calls. The 255-byte bound also avoids a wrap in ruma's validator (`(index + 6) as u8` then `NonZeroU8::new(..).unwrap()`, ruma-identifiers-validation mxc_uri.rs:25, which could panic for a 250-byte server name).
  - Fetching is only through `client.media().get_media_content(&MediaRequestParameters { source: MediaSource::Plain(parsed), format }, true)`. The `Fetcher` trait takes the parsed `OwnedMxcUri`, so no URL is ever built from the raw string.
  - Cap: 1 MiB (inclusive) on the thumbnail; empty is unavailable; an oversized thumbnail is refused. There is no `File` fallback (removed in the security follow-up below). Residual: the SDK's media API returns a whole `Vec<u8>` (no streaming limit exists in 0.19.1: no response-size option in `RequestConfig`), so the cap applies after the download and the SDK has already stored it in its encrypted media cache; the bytes are not handed on. Memory for one download is bounded only by the server.
  - A command looks at no more than 256 IDs (`bounded_unique`: order kept, repeats removed, the rest ignored and not answered; the app asks again for what it still renders). Every answer is sent from a task, so the command loop never waits on the network or on a full event channel.
- Behaviour: an ID in flight or already answered gets no second event (the second `FetchAvatars([id])` gives none); a repeat of an ID within one command is dropped too, so each ID gets exactly one answer per session. At most 4 fetches run at once (permit held for the fetch only). The thumbnail is 96x96 with `use_cache = true`; the original file is never requested. SDK error text is dropped (it can quote server data); image bytes are never logged (`AvatarImage` Debug shows the length).
- Fixture: `crates/core/tests/fixtures/avatar.png` is a 64x64 8-bit RGB PNG (7858 bytes) generated for this step with a short Python script (zlib and struct only, a plain gradient); it is original, with no third-party content or licence.
- Integration test (`tests/avatars.rs`, verbatim ignore reason): alice uploads the fixture as a new room's avatar through `Room::upload_avatar`; the core's list shows `avatar: Some(id)`; `FetchAvatars([not-mxc, id, id])` gives one `None` and one `Some(bytes)` (non-empty) within 30 s; a second `FetchAvatars([id])` gives no event within 3 s; then the tempdir is scanned: any file containing the returned bytes or the fixture must be a SQLite file (`.sqlite` in its name).
- APIs used, confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/` (pinned versions):
  - matrix-sdk 0.19.1: `Client::media` client/mod.rs:928; `Media::get_media_content` media.rs:431 (cache read/write inside, :431-480); `Room::upload_avatar` room/mod.rs:3040 (tests).
  - matrix-sdk-base 0.19.1 media/mod.rs: `MediaFormat` :46, `MediaThumbnailSettings` :65 with `new(width, height)` :99, `MediaRequestParameters` :130, `MediaSource::Plain` (re-exported by ruma events::room).
  - ruma-common 0.20.0 identifiers/mxc_uri.rs: `MxcUri::parts` :35, `is_valid` :51; ruma-identifiers-validation `mxc_uri::validate` :7.
  - tokio 1.53.1: `JoinSet::spawn` task/join_set.rs:142, `join_next` :296, `try_join_next` :323, `abort_all` :463; `Arc<Semaphore>::acquire_owned` sync/semaphore.rs:796.
  - `uint!` is re-exported through `matrix_sdk::ruma` (compiled). `mime` is not re-exported by the SDK, so the test passes `&"image/png".parse().unwrap()` (inferred as `Mime`) and adds no dependency.
- The CLI is unchanged (it ignores `Avatar`). Nothing is cached outside the SDK store.
- Security follow-up (audit of bb42bd3), deviation from plan Q11 ("fall back to `MediaFormat::File` only if the thumbnail request fails"): the fallback to the original file is removed; only the server-generated 96x96 thumbnail is requested. Reason: in matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `DefaultMediaFetcher` sets `.timeout(Some(Duration::MAX))` (media.rs:818) and reads the whole body into memory, and `get_media_content(.., true)` caches it before our size check, so a room with a huge non-image avatar could buffer and cache large downloads and hold all 4 permits forever. The SDK offers no per-request media timeout (the fetcher builds its own `RequestConfig`), so every fetch is wrapped in `tokio::time::timeout` with `FETCH_DEADLINE` = 20 s (the timed-out future is dropped, which releases the permit). Tests: `thumbnail_error_is_unavailable_and_the_file_is_never_requested`, `a_fetch_that_never_completes_times_out`, `hanging_fetches_release_their_permits` (real short deadline, never-completing fake; no tokio `test-util`). Residual: a thumbnail request is still unbounded in size if a malicious server ignores the thumbnail parameters and returns a huge body, but only until the 20 s deadline.
- Local media: `parse_avatar_id` now refuses server names `*.localhost` (case-insensitive, port ignored), because `Media::is_local_uri` (media.rs:792, `LOCAL_MXC_SERVER_NAME = "send-queue.localhost"` :63) serves `mxc://send-queue.localhost/<txn>` from the local store (the user's pending uploads). The bare `localhost` (the local test homeserver) stays valid.
- Retry: a fetch that returns nothing (error, timeout, empty, oversized) removes its ID from the shared `seen` set before the `None` answer is sent, so a later `FetchAvatars` retries it; in-flight and successful IDs stay deduplicated; invalid IDs are not retried. `seen` is capped at `MAX_SEEN` = 4096 and cleared when full.
- Open issues: none new. No new human items beyond the checkpoint item below.

## Phase 2 / Task 6 notes
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app: 16 lib tests, 5 bin tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` and the ignored run (`cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`) all pass (app `tests/bridge.rs` 1 passed in 4.8 s; all core suites ok). `docker compose stop` afterwards. The keyring test was not run. actionlint is not installed here: the only CI change is the one `cargo test` line of the `integration` job (Q16), not run through actionlint.
- TDD: the `state` tests (11) were written against a `todo!()` `update` and the two stream tests against an empty stream; 11 failed, then the code was added. The integration test then exposed a bug the unit tests missed (`Bridge::deadline` built `tokio::time::sleep` on the UI thread, which has no runtime context: "there is no reactor running"); a regression test `timer_can_be_started_from_a_thread_without_a_runtime` was written, seen to fail against the old code, and the `sleep` is now created inside the spawned task (`spawn_timer`).
- App: `crates/app` is now a lib (`bridge`, `state`) plus the `twicecat` bin. `main.rs` parses `--data-dir`, `--homeserver`, `--ephemeral`, `--help` (Q20 note in the help text), calls `logging::init("warn,twicecat_core=info,twicecat_app=info")`, and runs `iced::application(App::booter(startup), App::update, App::view)` with `.title`, `.subscription`, `.theme`, `.exit_on_close_request(false)`. Deviation from the plan text: the boot function is `App::booter(Startup)` (a closure that takes the `Startup` once) instead of the bare `App::boot`, because boot needs the already started bridge and Iced's `BootFn` is a zero-argument `Fn`.
- `bridge.rs`: `Bridge::start(config, secrets) -> io::Result<(Bridge, EventsSlot)>` (the runtime build can fail), `Bridge::send(&self, Command) -> Option<Task<Message>>` (`try_send`; a full channel gives a `Pending` whose task does the channel `send`; a closed channel drops the command), `Bridge::deadline(Duration) -> Task<Message>` (timer on the core runtime, Q18), `events_stream`, `events_subscription(slot)`, `shutdown_timeout(5 s)` in `Drop`.
- `state.rs`: `App` holds a `Box<dyn CommandSink>` (`Bridge` in production, `mpsc::Sender<Command>` in tests, no runtime). Boot sends `RestoreSession` and returns `system::theme().map(Message::ThemeMode)`. `Screen` is `Starting | Login | Loading | Main | ShuttingDown | Disconnected`. `CloseRequested` sends `Shutdown` once and shows `ShuttingDown`; `CoreClosed` (or the 10 s `ShutdownDeadline`) while shutting down sets `exit_requested` and returns `iced::exit()`; `CoreClosed` otherwise shows `Disconnected`. `RoomListChanged` is applied with `ListDiff::apply`; the first list diff or `RoomListLoaded` moves `Loading` to `Main` (Tasks 7 and 9 refine this). `LoginSubmitted` carries an `api::Password` (redacted `Debug`, tested with a sentinel). The theme is plain `Theme::Light`/`Dark` from the mode; Task 8 adds the palettes. `ThemeMode` from `system::theme_changes()` is not subscribed yet (Task 8).
- The "full capacity-1 sink" test checks that `update` returns within 50 ms, that the fallback task is non-empty (`Task::units() > 0`) and that nothing but the boot command is queued; the delivery itself is tested on `Pending::deliver` directly, because `iced::runtime::task::into_stream` is not reachable from the app (iced re-exports `iced_runtime` only partially) and a `Task` is opaque.
- Integration test (`tests/bridge.rs`, verbatim ignore reason): ensures alice has a room through a few raw HTTP/1.0 requests to the local homeserver (login, `createRoom`, logout; no new dependency), starts a real `Bridge` with an in-memory secret store and a scratch data dir, pumps the slot's stream into `App::update`, logs in, waits for `Screen::Main` with at least one room (60 s), sends `CloseRequested`, and waits for `exit_requested` (the `CoreClosed` path, 10 s). The test creates one room per run on the local server and leaves one device session of alice (as the CLI tests do).
- `--ephemeral` removes its temp dir on exit but does not revoke the session on the server (the CLI does); stated in `--help`.
- APIs used, confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/` (iced 0.14.0, tokio 1.53.1):

## Phase 2 / Task 7 notes
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, clippy (`--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -D warnings`), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (app: 38 lib tests, 5 bin tests), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` and the ignored run (`cargo test -p twicecat-core -p twicecat-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`) all pass; `docker compose stop` afterwards. The keyring test was not run.
- TDD: the `room_list` tests (11 per-variant, batch, out-of-range, total, selection, timeline, logout, timing) were written first; the build failed (no `selected`, `timeline`, `total_rooms`, `Message::SelectRoom`), then the code was added. The 11 variant tests and the timing test passed on the existing `ListDiff::apply` as soon as they compiled.
- App: new `room_list.rs` (`apply_room_diffs`, `contains`). `App` gains `total_rooms`, `selected: Option<RoomId>`, `timeline: Vec<TimelineItem>`, and `Message::SelectRoom(RoomId)` (ignored for a room not in the list; otherwise sets `selected`, clears the timeline and sends `Command::SelectRoom`). After each `RoomListChanged` batch the selection is dropped (with the timeline) if the room is no longer in the list, so a `Remove`+`Insert` reorder in one batch keeps it. `TimelineDiff` applies only when its `room_id` is the selected room. `LoggedOut`/`SessionExpired` also reset `total_rooms`, the selection and the timeline. The lists are changed in place; only `Reset`/`Clear` rebuild.
- Timing (Q17; debug profile, this machine, `cargo test -p twicecat-app --lib room_list_batches_under_16ms -- --nocapture`; median of 15 runs after a warm-up, each on a fresh app, `Instant` around `update` only; deterministic index-based rooms with name, avatar ID, 100-char preview): reset 2000: 1.84 ms; 4 x append 500: 2.06 ms; mixed (100 Set, 50 Remove+Insert, 10 PushFront) on 2000: 0.43 ms; clear then reset 2000: 0.63 ms. Budget 16 ms. `ListDiff::apply_owned` was not needed and not added; the core API is unchanged.

The 20 new `state` tests were written against `todo!()` update arms (20 failed), then the code was added. Not seen red: the small pure helpers `last_messages`, `sync_label`, `room_count_label` and the views (they were written together with their tests).
- App: new `screens/` (`mod.rs` with `centered_text`, `login.rs`, `loading.rs`, `main_view.rs`). `Screen` gains `WaitingForBrowser`. `Message::LoginSubmitted{..}` is reshaped (Task 6 open issue): `ServerChanged`, `UsernameChanged`, `PasswordChanged(Password)`, unit `LoginSubmitted`, `OAuthRequested`, `CancelOAuth`, `BrowserOpened{ok}`, `CopyLink`, `ComposerChanged(MessageText)`, `ComposerSubmitted`, `LogoutRequested`. `App` gains `login: LoginForm`, `login_in_flight`, `notice` (replaces `login_error`), `browser_link`, `browser_opened`, `composer: MessageText`, `send_error`, and a manual `Debug` (screen, counts, login form, composer; never rooms, timeline bodies or the sink). `tests/bridge.rs` now types the form and sends `LoginSubmitted`.
- Transitions (all unit-tested): boot `Starting`; `NoSession` to `Login`; `LoggedIn` to `Loading` (also when restored); `Loading` to `Main` on `RoomListLoaded` or a non-empty list (an empty first diff no longer opens `Main`); `LoginFailed(e)` to `Login` with `e.to_string()` as the notice (`Cancelled` shows "Sign-in cancelled.") and the password cleared; `LoggedOut` / `SessionExpired` to `Login` with different notices; `OAuthUrl` to `WaitingForBrowser`; Cancel sends `CancelOAuth` and the core's `LoginFailed(Cancelled)` returns to `Login`.
- Login: Enter or **Log in** needs a non-empty user name and password (else a notice, nothing sent). The server is trimmed, empty means `None`. On submit the password is moved out of the form into `Command::LoginPassword` (`mem::replace`) and the field is cleared; `login_in_flight` blocks every second submit (password or OIDC) and the widgets lose their `on_input`/`on_press` while it is set. **Sign in with browser** needs a non-blank server. A password field keeps the `Password` type end to end (the widget reads `expose()`); iced's own text-input buffer is not zeroized (known residual, Q19).
- OIDC (Q21 plus the security review): `screens::login::check_browser_url(&str) -> Result<BrowserUrl, UrlRefused>` is pure and has no URL crate (the app may only depend on core, iced, image, tokio, open). It requires pure ASCII with no whitespace, control characters or backslash; scheme `https`, or `http` only when the host is `127.0.0.1`, `[::1]` or `localhost` (case-insensitive); no `@` in the authority (userinfo); a plain DNS-label, IPv4 or bracketed-IPv6 host; an optional numeric port within u16. `UrlRefused` (`Malformed`, `Scheme`, `Userinfo`, `InsecureHost`) never carries the URL. `BrowserUrl` is the only type `open_in_browser` takes, and its `Debug` is redacted. Tests: https and loopback http allowed; other http, `127.0.0.2`, `localhost.evil.com`, `127.0.0.1.evil.com`, IPv4-mapped IPv6 refused; userinfo variants incl. `http://127.0.0.1@evil.com`; `javascript:`, `data:`, `file:`, `ftp:`, scheme-less and empty refused; ~25 malformed forms; no error or `Debug` shows a sentinel `state`.
- Opening the browser: `App::on_oauth_url` runs `check_browser_url` first and only for a login that is in flight; a refused URL gives a notice without the URL, sends `CancelOAuth` and returns no task (tested with `units() == 0`). An allowed URL returns `Task::perform(async move { open_in_browser(&link) }, BrowserOpened)`. `update` never calls `open`: `open_in_browser` lives in `screens/login.rs`, and a test checks that the production part of `state.rs` has no `open::` or `that_detached`. The URL is never logged or displayed: the waiting screen shows "Continue in your browser" with **Cancel**, and **Copy link** (`clipboard::write` of the checked URL) only when the launcher failed.
- Loading screen: "Sync: Running / Offline / Reconnecting (attempt n) ..." and "n rooms loaded" (pure `sync_label`, `room_count_label`, tested).
- Main layout: fixed 300 px left pane (a placeholder list of the first 100 rooms as plain buttons that send `SelectRoom`; Task 10 replaces it with the windowed list), right pane with the selected room's name, the last 50 `Message` items as `sender: body` (`last_messages`, tested), `SendFailed` line, and a single-line composer (`MessageText`; no `on_input`, so disabled, with no selected room). Enter sends `SendMessage` and clears the field; blank text sends nothing. Selecting another room, deselecting and logging out clear the draft and the send error. A status bar shows the sync state, the account and **Log out** (`Command::Logout`).
- Redaction tests: `Debug` of `PasswordChanged("hunter2-x")`, `ComposerChanged("secret-body-x")`, `LoginForm` and the whole `App` after typing both contain neither sentinel.

New `App.pending_cancel` is set when the app ends an OAuth attempt itself and sends `CancelOAuth` (`cancel_in_core`); exactly one `Cancelled` reply is consumed with it. A `Cancelled` with no login in flight is also ignored.
  - Warning: a late `Cancelled` after a new attempt started is consumed by `pending_cancel`, so the new attempt stays in flight. A user Cancel does not set the flag (the attempt stays in flight until the reply). `pending_cancel` is also cleared by `LoggedIn` and by any other `LoginFailed`, since a cancel reply would have arrived first (the core sends no reply when no OAuth was pending, so the flag must not linger).

A changed query sends exactly one `SetRoomListQuery`; an identical one (including a whitespace-only change to the search) sends nothing. A new query clears `load_more_at`. The query is kept across logout because the core keeps its own (engine.rs `room_list_query` watch).

The pane builds only that window between two `space()` spacers; a test checks `top + rows + bottom == len * ROW_HEIGHT`. An empty list builds no scrollable, so the stored offset is reset to 0 when the list is empty and on logout.

Note: `total_rooms` is the account total, not the filtered count, so with a filter active one `LoadMoreRooms` per loaded length may be sent near the end; harmless (a no-op in the core when nothing more is listed).
